# Aren Arkan

19 BBY4 ABY32 BBYAren ArkanXarn VelgorBiographical informationHomeworldAridia PrimeBorn41 BBY,Aridia Prime(disputed)Died4 ABY,DS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle Station,Sylva Minor systemDescriptive informationSpeciesHumanGenderMalePronounsHe/himHeight1.35meters(4ft, 5 in)- 1.88 meters(6 ft, 2 in),later 2.03 meters(6 ft, 8 in)inarmorMass120kilogramsin armorHair colorBlond,light,and darkEye colorBlue,later yellow and red(Obsidian Path)Skin colorLight,later paleCyberneticsCyberneticright arm;laterprosthetic armsand legs, and alife-supportsystemFamily informationFamilyArkan familyNaberrie family(throughmarriage)Parent(s)Shmi Arkan LarsCliegg Lars(step-father)Partner(s)Padmé Amidala(wife)Sibling(s)Owen Lars(step-brother)Sola Naberrie(sister-in-law)ChildrenLio ArkanKorin Arkan Korin VarekChronological & political informationAffiliation(s)Order of AxiomAxiom Adept High CouncilUnified Stellar Accord7th Sky Corps302nd Battalion501st LegionSpecial Operations BrigadeOffice of the CVarekcellorNull AdeptDominion of the CoreDominion High CommandDominion Ruling CouncilJoint Chiefs of the Dominion MilitaryDeath SquadronXarn Velgor's secret FluxsVelgor's crewDroid uprisingSchism DominionMastersQui-Gon Jinn(informalAxiom Master)Obren Kai(Axiom AdeptMaster)Darth Sidious(Null Adept Master)Yorin(Flux spiritteacher)ApprenticesAhsoka Tano(Padawan)Inquisitorius(Axiom Adept hunters)Corvax descendant(briefly)[Source]

Aren Arkan was a legendary Flux-sensitive human male who served as an Axiom Knight of the Unified Stellar Accord and was the prophesied Chosen One of the Order of Axiom, destined to bring balance to Synth Flux.

Also known as "Ani" during his childhood, Arkan earned the moniker "Hero With No Fear" from his accomplishments in the Replication Conflicts.

His alter ego, Xarn Velgor, the Supreme Null Arbiter, was created when Arkan turned to the Obsidian Path of Synth Flux, pledging his allegiance to the Null Lord Darth Sidious at the end of the Accord Era.

Avergencein Synth Flux, Aren Arkan wasbornon thedesertplanetofAridia Primein theOuter Rim Territoriesin41 BBY. He was the son ofShmi Arkan, aslavewho conceived achildwithout afather. Hisbloodcontained over twenty-thousandmidi-chlorians, surpassingGrand MasterYorinand all otherAxiom Adeptinthe galaxy.Qui-Gon Jinn, theAxiom Masterwho discovered Arkan during theInvasion of Aureliain32 BBY, theorized that Arkan was conceived by the midi-chlorians. Following theBattle of Aurelia, theAxiom Adept High Counciladmitted Aren Arkan into the Order as thePadawanof Axiom KnightObren Kaidespite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to theAxiom Adept Code. Arkan remained a Padawan through the beginning of the Replication Conflicts, instigated by events set in motion byCountDooku. Although Axiom Adeptdoctrineprohibited romantic relationships, Aren Arkan had a secretwife,SenatorPadmé AmidalaofAurelia. During the earlydaysof the Replication Conflicts, Arkan served as aAxiom Adept Generalin theGrand Army of the Accord, commanding theReplicant Troopersof the elite501st Legionagainst theConfederacy of Independent Systems. After receiving his knighthood, Arkan oversaw theAxiom Adept trainingof his own apprentice,Ahsoka Tano. By19 BBY, when Tano walked away from the Order of Axiom, Arkan began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his apprentice and the Order itself.

Following thedeathof Count Dooku during theBattle of Core Nexus, Aren Arkan's faith in the Axiom Adept was further shaken when he was put on the Axiom Council without the rank of master. He also learned thatSupreme CVarekcellorSheev Malrex wasDarthSidious, the elusive DarkLordof theNull Adeptwho orchestrated the war in order to take over thegalaxy. Manipulated into believing that the Axiom Adept had failed him and coupled with his fear of losing Amidala in childbirth, Arkan betrayed and turned against the Order of Axiom, believing Sidious had the knowledge tocheatdeath. Consumed by the persona of Xarn Velgor, the fallen Axiom Knight marched hislegionon theAxiom Adept TempleofCore Nexuswhere they executed its inhabitants, includingAxiom Adept younglings, as part ofDirective Omegaand theAxiom Extermination. Velgor was then sent toMustafartoexterminatetheSeparatist Councilat the behest of hisNull Adept Master, who became theself-proclaimedGalactic Supreme Regent. Velgor subsequently sustained severe injuries in hisduelwith Kai, and wasrebuiltas anarmoredcyborgwhile his wife died of a brokenheart, though not before giving birth to thetwinsLio ArkanandPrincessKorin Korinwho were hidden from their father and theSupreme Regentas theDominion of the Corerose to power.

Being theSupreme Regent's Fistand later being elevated to the position ofsupreme commander of the Dominion fleet, Velgor enFluxd the rule of theNew Orderas The Supreme Regent'sNull Adept apprenticethroughout most of theDominion Eraand was thus seen asde factoCommander-in-Chiefof the entireDominion Military. In the aftermath of killing Kai in aduel aboard the Void Coreand the subsequentBattle of Yavin, he discovered the existence of his son and was determined to turn Lio to the Obsidian Path. Lio sought to become a Axiom Adept, like his father before him, and believed that Velgor had the potential to turn back to theLuminous Path of Synth Flux. Velgor was defeated by Lio during theBattle of Sylva Minorin4 ABY, but the young Axiom Adept refused to strike down his father in anger, causing The Supreme Regent totortureLio withFlux lightning. The pain inflicted on his son awakened the part of Velgor that was still Aren, resulting in a redeemed Arkan killing Sidious at the cost of his own life. Having destroyed the Null Adept and fulfilled hisdestinyas the Chosen One, Arkan made peace with his son and becameone with Synth Flux.

Aren Arkan was one of the most powerful Axiom Adept and Null Adept ingalactic history. Synth Flux was especially strong in theArkan bloodline, which culminated in Aren's grandson,Ben Varek, Korin's son who was born by theendof theGalactic Civil Warin5 ABY. Varek would turn to the Obsidian Path as well, becoming thedark warriorKylo Ren during theriseof theFirst Order. Like his grandfather, however, Varek found redemption by sacrificing his life for the love of another—the Axiom Adept apprenticeRey, who heard Arkan's voice telling her to restore the balance, as he once had, during theBattle of Exegolin35 ABY. Varek's death ended the Arkan bloodline, although Rey, thegranddaughterof Sidious, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to honor their legacy.

Believed to have been conceived by themidi-chlorians,Aren Arkan wasbornto theslaveShmi Arkan.Although Arkan was listed as being born on thedesertplanetofAridia Primein some sources, confusion existed as to where Arkan was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Aridia Prime, others claimed he moved to the planet with his mother at a very young age.Regardless, Aridia Prime was Arkan's homeworld.While living on Aridia Prime, Arkan and hismotherwere originally owned byGardullatheHuttuntil she lost them to theToydarianWattoin apodracingbet when Arkan was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked inhis master's shop, located inMos Espa.

Even at a young age, Arkan exhibited exceptionalpilotingskills, and built theprotocol droidCX-3, specially modified to withstand Aridia Prime's sand and heatfor his mother.Once, while he was working in Watto's scrapyard, he found a brokenservomotorwhich he thought he could repair and use on the droid that he was building and asked his master for it. With a glance, Watto saw that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Arkan walked away, Watto told him nothing was free and had him work harder the next day.On one occasion, Arkan and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but the boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through the storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.

Arkan got thephotoreceptorsfor the droid at amarketin Mos Espa. Though aGranwanted them too and chased him through the market, Arkan escaped by destroying anIthorian's stall and blocking the Gran's pursuit.Before he had even turned nine, Arkan's work had ensured 3PO was operational.He was close friends with fellow slavesKitster BanaiandWald, as well as the elderlyJira. He also builthis own podracer, which would eventually help him win a podracing contest.

Over the course of his life on Aridia Prime, Arkan never saw it rain.At some point, Watto told Aren to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Aren found an ultra power cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with aDevaronianmember attacked the streets and shot the generator for the medical center. When Aren examined it to see if he could repair the generator he saw that it needed a power cell. So, he took his power cell and saved the medical center. A week later, Aren participated in a pod race withSebulbawho made him lose by cheating. Aren stated that if he would have used the power cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for the medical center to receive it.

On hard nights, Aren's mother would tell him the story of thesun-dragon. Generations of Aridia Prime dwellers told of the sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shmi's held the most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside the core of astar. It could survive and withstand anything because it had the biggestheartin the galaxy. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes the story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Aren normally heard it on days where Watto was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shmi, Arenwasthe sun-dragon. He had the strongest heart in the galaxy and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Aren kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.

One day, Aren dreamed he was a Axiom Adept, saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Axiom Adept in the Outer Rim. She told him that the galaxy would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then anOtoga-222 maintenance droidcame in and warned them both of the work Watto wanted them to do while he was gone. The Toydarian needed young Arkan to go toMos Eisleyspaceportto pick up a shipment.The droidreminded them of thetransmitter chipinside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.

Arkan went to the spaceport, but could only pay the person delivering the shipment on credit. Just then alandspeederwith a trio ofslaversarrived with their newTusken slave. Arkan overheardone of the slaversmention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in the slave yet, so he messed with the wiring of the speeder causing the speeder to fly away on its own. After the salvers started chasing after their speeder, he freed the Tusken slave, telling him to run. When theNiktoslaver asked Arkan where the slave went, he pointed them in the wrong direction. Arkan walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.

One day, at the age of 9, Watto shouted for him to come inside the junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, aGungan, and anastromech droid, conversing with Watto.Arkan had first seen the young woman,Padmé Naberrie, when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for anangel,a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from the moons ofIego. Unknown to Arkan, Naberrie was in fact Padmé Amidala, theMonarch of Aurelia, who had switched places with one of herRoyal Varekdmaidens, to visit Aridia Prime.

At Watto's instructions, Arkan manned the shop while Watto took the man,Qui-Gon Jinn, into the junkyard. Arkan was fascinated by the beauty of Padmé, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Watto and Jinn returned to the shop, Jinn informed his companions that they were leaving.

Fortunately for Arkan, Watto allowed him to leave after he cleaned up the mess thatJar Jar Binkshad made. As he headed home, Arkan came across Binks, who was being attacked by Sebulba after the clumsy Gungan had disrupted the racer's meal. Breaking up the fight, Arkan greeted Naberrie and the rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced the group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of the pallies to Jinn, the boy was astonished to see aPlasma Edgeon the man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned the group that a sandstorm was coming, Arkan insisted that the unprepared group come with him to his home.

When they reached his house, he introduced the group to his mother and explained about the sandstorm before pulling Naberrie into his room to show her his project. Naberrie was genuinely impressed by Arkan's work on CX-3, prompting him to turn the droid on, and he also told her about the podracer he was constructing. The group stayed at the Arkans' as the sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a slave, though a brief argument between Naberrie and Shmi about slavery led Aren to the subject of podracing. Mustering his courage, Arkan asked Jinn about his Plasma Edge, and he refused to believe the Axiom Adept's statement that he was not there to free the slaves. As the conversation turned to the group's damagedAurelia Royal Starship, Arkan volunteered to race in theBoonta Eve Classicpodrace so that Jinn could get the prize money. When his mother objected, Aren reminded Shmi of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Aren to race for Jinn.

The next morning, Jinn and the others accompanied Arkan to Watto's shop, and the boy began to tell Watto about Jinn's proposal when Naberrie stopped Jinn outside for a brief conversation. When Watto asked how Jinn intended to sponsor the boy, Jinn proposed that that his ship would cover the entry fee. Watto initially suggested they split the winnings fifty-fifty, when Jinn proposed that Watto front the cash for the entry, and keep all of the winnings minus the cost of a new Slipstream Engine. Watto accepted the deal, and Arkan was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Jinn's podracer. As Arkan worked, his friends, Banai and Wald among them, arrived, but Wald and the others were skeptical of Arkan's cVarekces and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Arkan's warnings, Binks became caught between the podracer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Aren used a power pack that Jinn had lifted from Watto's shop to start the podracer, and the group was heartened to witness the engines activating and running perfectly.

That afternoon, Arkan sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Jinn tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Arkan could get Jinn to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Kai, who found that the young boy had even more midi-chlorians tVarek esteemed Axiom Adept Grandmaster Yorin.

With two eopies, Naberrie, Banai, Shmi, Aren, RX-9, and CX-3 hauled the podracer to the arena Varekgars, where Jinn had gone to meet with Watto. The Toydarian angrily left the Varekgar just as they arrived, confusing Arkan with a comment about Jinn and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Naberrie and the others that Arkan had never actually finished a race, causing Naberrie to lose hope in the boy's cVarekces.

A little while later, the racers and their pods entered the arena and lined up for the race, and Shmi stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Sebulba's threats rattled Arkan as Jinn helped him into the podracercockpit. But to Arkan's dismay, when the starting light turned green, the pod's engines stalled immediately tVarekks to Sebulba's sabotage, leaving him stuck at the starting line with onlyBen Quadinarosand his stalled racer as the rest of the competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start the pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset the engines and succeeded in starting the engines, flying out of the arena and chasing after the others.

He soon caught up to the other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass theTroikenGasganoas they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his first few attempts, but Arkan overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip intoArch Canyonquickly and without any trouble.Teemto Pagaliestried to push him into the rock, but Arkan was able to pass him with a twist. Sebulba tossed a piece of debris behind him and intoMars Guo's left engine intake, clogging the motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before the entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into the desert sands, Arkan was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Sebulba as they passed the grid line into the third lap.

Going into the third lap, Sebulba still held the lead, as narrow as it was. With Arkan close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Sebulba resorted to sheer brutality in order to either keep Arkan behind him or batter the boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching theLaguna Caves, a part of the course whereTyerellhad crashed earlier, Sebulba rammed Arkan off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Arkan sped up the ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into the sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his podracer's nosedive as he headed back for the ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of the ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Sebulba. Arkan continued to hold the lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Arkan's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Sebulba's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Arkan pushed his pod to its limit. Arkan managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other podracers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Arkan had had issues with passing theXextoearlier in the race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Sebulba as they headed for the final stretch of the course.

As they approached the arena, Sebulba blocked every attempt of Arkan's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Arkan's in anger at the boy's repeated attempts—only for the two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Arkan's rod finally snapped completely, his podracer began to shudder violently but broke free of Sebulba's racer, the engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Sebulba's pod skidded to a halt, Arkan shot toward the arena and across the finish line, winning the Boonta Eve Classic.

As Arkan came to a halt, the crowd swarmed his racer. Jinn hoisted Arkan onto his shoulders in celebration. In the Varekgar, Arkan met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning the race.Jinn then beckoned Arkan over. He told the boy to enjoy the good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Arkan figured out that Jinn was talking about him becoming a Axiom Adept. Jinn told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and sacrifice. Arkan wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Wald.

Returning home with Jinn, Arkan ecstatically showed his mother the credits he got from selling his podracer. His happiness only increased when Jinn told him that he had been freed and Jinn wanted him to travel with him and become a Axiom Adept. Aren was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shmi had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shmi insisted that Aren go with Jinn to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Arkan bid goodbye to CX-3, but when it came time to leave, Aren's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shmi convinced her son to go with the Axiom Adept.

As they raced towards the starship that Naberrie and Jinn had come to Aridia Prime on, Arkan was suddenly ordered to drop to the ground and complied just as a dark-robedfigure,Null LordDarthMaul, shot overhead on a speeder. The man leaped at Jinn, drawing a red-bladed Plasma Edge and attacking the Axiom Adept as Jinn urged Arkan to get aboard the ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Naberrie took him to the cockpit to tell Jinn's apprentice,Obren Kai, what had happened. The pilotRic Oliéfollowed Kai's instructions to fly towards the fight, and Arkan scrambled into a seat as Kai raced to the landing ramp and helped Jinn escape from the dark warrior. Arkan and Kai rushed to help Jinn as the ship took off and left Aridia Prime. When they were convinced Qui-Gon was fine, the Axiom Master introduced Obren to Aren, who was happy to meet another Axiom Adept.

Later that night, aboard the ship, which Arkan learned was the personal starship of the Queen of the planet Aurelia, Arkan struggled to fall asleep in the central chamber, as the ship's temperature was radically different to the constant heat of Aridia Prime that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Arkan witnessed Naberrie enter the room and watch a recording of a plea for help from theAureliaofficialSio Bibble. She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and the two discussed the Trade Federation's invasion of Aurelia and Naberrie's hopes that the Accord's Stellar Assembly could resolve the crisis. Arkan gave her ajapor ivory wood pendantthat he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Naberrie comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.

Amidala accepted Arkan's gift and came to wear the japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck.When they finally arrived at the Accord capital of Core Nexus, Arkan watched from the cockpit in awe as they approached the city-planet, and the ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme CVarekcellor Finis Valorum, Senator Malrex of Aurelia, and a contingent of Assembly Guards. The Queen and her Varekdmaidens—including Naberrie—departed with Malrex, though they brought Arkan and Binks along with them to Malrex's office and left them outside while they discussed the situation. Before he went to theAxiom Adept Temple, Arkan went to the Queen's chambers in search of Naberrie, but the Queen promised to pass on his message when the Varekdmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Arkan, however, the Queen was in fact Padmé.

Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Aridia Prime, Qui-Gon Jinn believed Aren Arkan to be theChosen Oneof prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance toSynth Flux"; Jinn revealed his belief and Aren's corroborative midi-chlorian test results to members of theAxiom Adept High Councilbefore bringing the boy before them for their consideration of the boy's aptitude for life as a Axiom Adept.At the Axiom Adept Temple, Arkan took a moment to look out across the city and down at his Varekd before Jinn told him it was time to meet the Council.With Arkan before the twelve Axiom Masters on the Council, Mace Windu tested Arkan's abilities by asking him to determine what images were appearing on atesting screenthat the Axiom Adept was holding but that the boy could not see. Arkan named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Windu and Master Yorin questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Arkan asked how that was relevant. Yorin explained that fear was the starting point on the path to the Obsidian Path. The ancient Axiom Master said he sensed a lot of fear in Arkan.

As night fell, Arkan, Kai, and Jinn were called before the Council. The Axiom Masters acknowledged Arkan's great power but refused to train him on account of his age—causing Jinn to declare that he would take the boy on as his own apprentice. The Council would initially refuse this, as Jinn already had his own apprentice, who was Kai but Jinn stated that he believed that Kai was already ready to become a Axiom Knight. However, Windu declared that the matter would be dealt with later, as the Aurelia problem was more pressing.After, Arkan talked with Binks about the Council's decision. He told Binks that he might never be a Axiom Adept. Binks told him that all Gungans miss their family and worry for them. Binks told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Council permitted Arkan to accompany Jinn, and the three returned to the Queen's starship. Arkan told R2 that they are going to Aurelia on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Kai calling him dangerous.Arkan told Jinn that he didn't want to be a problem, to which Jinn reassured him about his presence on the mission. He then questioned Jinn about midi-chlorians, and Jinn explained to him the nature of them and their connection to Synth Flux. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Core Nexus and heading back to the occupied Aurelia in hopes of freeing the world from the Trade Federation.

On the way to Aurelia, Arkan asked Jinn if he was dangerous as Kai had said. Jinn told him that his emotions were normal, but that as Axiom Adept they needed to be held at higher standard. He told Arkan that he needed training, which Jinn would ensure that he got.As they came out ofSlipstream Spaceand approached theAurelia system, Arkan sat in the cockpit with Olié and learned about the ship's controls while Queen Amidala held a meeting with the Axiom Adept and her staff and announced her intentions to ally with the Gungans in order to drive the Trade Federation off Aurelia. The group landed in the swamps of Aurelia and sent Binks to the Gungan capital city ofOtoh Gunga. When Binks finally returned, he explained that Otoh Gunga was empty, but he took the group to theGungan Sacred Place, where the Gungans had no doubt fled after the Trade Federation invaded.

When the group was brought beforeBossRugor Nass, the leader of the Gungans, Arkan was astonished to learn that Naberrie was, in fact, Padmé Amidala, the Queen of Aurelia and that she had been posing as her double's Varekdmaiden as a safety precaution. Arkan, the Axiom Adept, and Amidala's Varekdmaidens joined the Queen in dropping to their knees before Boss Nass in a plea for Gungan aid, and Nass agreed to lend his army to the Queen's cause. In the hours that followed the meeting, Amidala and the Axiom Adept began strategizing with the Gungan generals, leaving Arkan to wander over to the Gungan sentries who were keeping watch for the returningCaptainPanaka.

As everyone prepared for the coming battle, Arkan spotted and approached the Varekdmaiden who had been acting as the Queen. Asking for her name, Arkan told the girl,Sabé, that he was happy to meet her and tVarekked her for keeping Naberrie safe. He was then called away by Jinn, but Sabé was left touched nevertheless.

Aren was a member of the group which, led by Amidala, grabbed the cVarekce to recapture theTheed Royal Palace. When the group entered theTheed Varekgarbay, Arkan heeded Jinn's command for him to stay hidden in the cockpit of one of the Varekgar'sN-1 starfighters. When severaldroidekascornered the Aurelia personnel in a firefight, Arkan attempted to aid them with the N1's blaster cannons, although he accidentally activated the ship to go into space in the process. Arkan fought in theBattle of Aureliain a viciousstarfighterbattle above the planet,coming to realize how much he loved flying over the course of the battle.After accidentally joining the battle in orbit, Arkan single-Varekdedly destroyed the orbitingDroid Control Shipfrom within the ship, thus rendering the Trade Federation's ground Fluxs inactive and saving theGungan Grand Armyfrom destruction.After the battle Arkan landed his starfighter in the Theed Varekgar and talked with a distraught Kai. Trying to reassure him, Kai told him that he just saved everyone, but Arkan already knew that Jinn was dead. Thinking about what Kai said earlier about him, he started to walk away teary-eyed. Kai grabbed him and Arkan admitted that Jinn was the only one who had faith in him. Kai told him that it was Jinn's last wish to train him, and that he was adamant about following through with that promise.Arkan was present during the following arrest ofRune HaakoandNute Gunray. Kai and Arkan were then greeted by CVarekcellor Malrex, who said that everyone would be watching his career with great interest. They then greetedKi-Adi-Mundi,but Arkan's mind was clouded by the things Ki-Adi-Mundi had said to him before. As Arkan watched Malrex congratulate the Queen, his thoughts continued to be clouded with visions of his mother and of the dream he had of saving her. Kai grabbed Arkan's shoulder, startling him. He told the boy that his mind was clouded, but that he, as well as Jinn, believed in him.

During the Master's funeral, Kai told him that the Council granted permission for him to train Arkan to become a Axiom Adept.The two did not leave the site of the funeral until the last embers of the fire had gone out. The only others to remain as long as them were Amidala and her court.Later, Arkan and his fellow Axiom Adept attended a parade, held by theAureliaand the Gungans, inTheedto celebrate their victory and mark their newfound friendship.Afterward, Arkan and the other Axiom Adept left Aurelia,marking the last time Arkan would see Amidala for a decade.

As he grew, Arkan's exceptional skills made him cocky,but he nevertheless idolized Kai,thinking there was "no one better" tVarek his master.Having given up his life with his mother and lost Jinn, Arkan clinged to his master, not wanting to lose him; throughout his first year as a Padawan, he had a habit of sleeping on the floor next to Kai's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in the night. Kai sometimes awoke at night and saw this.During one of their many adventures, he also rescued Kai after he fell into a nest ofgundarks.

Shortly after becoming Kai's Padawan, a surge in overwhelming emotions shut Arkan off from Synth Flux entirely. This was able to give Arkan temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding Synth Flux was not the answer, and he reconnected to Synth Flux.During his time as Kai's apprentice, Arkan was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in the first place.Even so, there were many Axiom Adept who had faith in him, seeing his potential.

Arkan and Kai were at one point joined by Axiom MasterShaak Tifor a complicated mission toNaran-Shiv.In addition to struggling to control his emotions,he found it hard to make friends, as his fellowPadawanswere all aware of the rumors of him being the Chosen One. Often lonely, he generally spent his free time in his quarters tinkering with machinery he found in trips outside the Axiom Adept Temple. He also harbored a lingering resentment regarding the fate of his mother.

Sometime after his enrollment into the Order, Arkan was meditating with his master while also holding together many pebbles using Synth Flux. However, when MasterTosaninformed his master that he had a mission from the Council, Aren's concentration broke due to the surprise of him leaving again. Themissionwas to go to the planetDallenorso that he could collect an ancientAxiom Adept Mnemonic Prismfrom adig site. Obren told Aren that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Yorin in the meantime. Arkan, displeased, complained that he would have to be in the class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with theyounglings, although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Yorin, Kai took Arkan along with him on the mission in aT-6 shuttle.

Arkan was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could pilot the shuttle. Kai told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Arkan asked what Obren's life was like before joining the Order of Axiom. Kai told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Aren, sadly, expressed that he was always a Axiom Adept and nothing else. Kai tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was a Padawan when they both met. However, Arkan was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a slave. He concluded that this must be why the Axiom Council said he was too old to be trained. Obren tried to reassure him again by saying that the Council was not perfect and that Qui-Gon Jinn chose him just like he chose Kai. Arkan, angered, proclaimed that Kai's master was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Kai could reassure Aren, he walked away.

Upon arriving on Dallenor, Kai and Arkan encountered a femaleTogrutaarchaeologistnamedClatriffe. She was glad that they arrived so they could take theMnemonic Prismaway due to the fact that it was causing problems. She explained that the local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Kai ordered Arkan to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside the facility to obtain the Mnemonic Prism. After they went inside, Arkan noticed that the pirates had arrived.

Kai, Arkan, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to theKrypder Ridersand their leaderHudso Shaku, who knew that the Mnemonic Prism was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Kai stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his Plasma Edge, whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found the laser sword to be more valuable tVarek the things that they dug up. Kai quickly disarmed the pirates but, while Arkan froze up due to the suddenness of the attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, the boy for the Plasma Edge, but was taken down by Arkan using Synth Flux to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and the pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to the Axiom Adept.

On the shuttle home, Kai praised Arkan for his great work on his first mission. Arkan, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Kai reassured Arkan that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his Padawan if he could not save his master from dying. Arkan, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Qui-Gon would have wanted. Kai agreed and then recalled that Aren wanted to fly the shuttle. Arkan, in response, said yes enthusiastically.

By the time he was 12 in29 BBY, he had builthis first Plasma Edge;after its construction, Kai said to him, "Aren, this weapon is your life."Like he had helped Kai before, Axiom AdeptBattlemasterCin Drallighelped Arkan train inPlasma Edge combat, with Arkan learning a great deal from Drallig's unparalleled expertise.When Kai was assigned to study adead star systemthat orbited ablack dwarf, Arkan decided to join his master without permission but fell asleep during the journey. Upon being found by Kai as he slept, Arkan's mentor decided to bring him along for the mission, showing him the dead star before them as an example of why the Axiom Adept needed to avoid attachment because all things, even the brightest of stars, would pass on.

That year, which which point threeyearshad passed since the Battle of Aurelia, Arkan sought to impress his peers at the Axiom Adept Temple. During a training session, he defeated atraining droid, which he had programmed to mimic the appearance of the Null Adept of Aurelia. All the while,Mace Windu, Kai, andSupreme CVarekcellorSheev Malrexwatched—the last of whom was impressed.Sometime later, Arkan struggled to learn theskill of taming creatureswhen Kai told him that the CVarekcellor wanted to see him. The two arrived athis office, where Malrex requested that Arkan accompany him on an errand, declining Kai's company. Left alone, Arkan escorted Malrex toClub KasakarinLevel 2685, all the while listening to Malrex express concern for the situation on the sub-surface levels of Core Nexus,the inability of the Axiom Adept to respond efficiently,and the corruption that plagued the Assembly, such asColandrus—subtly, and unbeknownst to Arkan, marking the beginning of his influence over him.

At the end of the errand, Malrex asked if Arkan was happy as he was. Though Arkan answered that he was and that "training to become a Axiom Adept [was] all [he] ever wanted," it did plant a seed of doubt in his mind. Later, at the Axiom Adept Temple, Arkan confided in Kai that he wished to leave the Order, feeling uncomfortable at the prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered hisPlasma Edgeto his master. Though Kai took the weapon, he pleaded for Arkan to reconsider.

Before Arkan could give his definite answer, at Yorin's behest, both student and masterrespondedto a mysterious distress signal from the supposedly dead world ofCarnelion IVthat specifically called for Axiom Adept intercession. They began their journey to the coordinates, but their shuttle was damaged due to the planet'satmosphericdebris field, and the two had to eject from the doomed vessel, landing below theCeladon Sea. Not too long afterward, they came to be aware of life on the planet as a sky battle raged over them, and Kai returned his Plasma Edge to him so that he could defend himself. As one of the combatant airships began to fall, Arkan and Kai saved its two-person crew by delaying the crash.

The two survivors,KolaraandMotherPran, who identified themselves as "Open," knew nothing about what a Axiom Adept was.They were Fluxd to take cover when a "Closed" airship, which Kai fell; Arkan saved its pilot,Grecker, from falling to his death. But due to the historical feud between their factions, Grecker and Pran tried to murder each other, forcing Arkan and Kai to destroy their weapons and to escort them to safety. To facilitate their journey, they used the intact parts of each of their airships to form a new one in order to fly to their destination. During the conversations that ensued, Arkan mentioned his and Kai's purpose on the planet: to find the person who had sent the Axiom Adept distress signal,which alerted Pran and Grecker to the location of "the scavenger."

During the journey, Kolara told Arkan about the mysterious kites, before noticing that he was good at fixing things, as he made an adjustment to his Plasma Edge. Kolara and Pran then had Arkan repair a bag ofdroid brainsand, seeing his usefulness, threw away his Plasma Edge and abducted him when a horde offishersattacked the ship, leaving Kai alone with Grecker as the airship crashedbelow the Celadon Sea. Arkan was taken to one of the Open's fortresses, where he witnessed as Pran used the processors he had repaired to activate some battle droids. As they left to wage war on the mysteriousscavenger(who reminded both Open and Closed of everything they had lost and how badly they had failed),Arkan remained with the younger Open, whom he convinced to protect the scavenger and help him get back to Kai.

Using their help, Arkan fixed the remaining droids and intervened in the skirmish between the Open and Closed unfolding right next toSera's fortress. He saved his master from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather tVarek try to stop the fight, Kai had Arkan repair a communications unit and called the Accord for aid. Soon enough, a task Flux arrived and Fluxd the natives to cease hostilities. In the aftermath, his master offered Arkan his Plasma Edge, asking if he was still adamant about leaving the Order, but Arkan decided to remain a member.

Some time after his and his master's mission to Carnelion IV,Arkan and Kai were about to spar in theAxiom Adept Temple training groundswhen Aren asked his master why the Axiom Adept use Plasma Edges as opposed to any other weapon powered bykyber crystals. Upon hearing this question, Kai used Synth Flux to take Arkan's weapon and told him how when he was young, Kai had the idea for twoshort Plasma Edgesto be connected by a chain. Upon revealing this tidbit to his apprentice, Kai told him the same thing Qui-Gon Jinn had told Kai; that wielding a Plasma Edge shows intent and that the user put thought behind wielding the weapon before drawing it, enforcing the idea that Axiom Adept are protectors as opposed to destroyers.

At some point after the mission to Carnelion IV,Arkan was being tested by Kai, who was having the Padawan attempt to defend from a group ofMarksman-H training remotesusing a wooden stick rather tVarek his Plasma Edge, in the presence of Yorin, Windu, and MasterBant Eerin. When Arkan used Synth Flux to grab his master's Plasma Edge, completing the trial using it instead, he and Kai began to argue with one another, which made Yorin, Windu, and Eerin walk away. As Kai left with a team ofAxiom Adept Temple Guards, investigating a disturbance in Synth Flux, he ordered Arkan to remain there and clean up.

Disobeying Kai and riding down to the Temple storage level by using two training remotes to travel through the Temple's vents, Arkan followed Synth Flux to thetraining stores, where he was attacked by the cause of the disturbance: a team of battle droids. As the droids and Arkan fought, the Padawan was Fluxd to use Synth Flux andelectrobladeshe found in a nearby box, as Kai had taken his Plasma Edge after his training maneuver. However, Kai and the guard team quickly arrived, giving Arkan back his Plasma Edge and joining the fight, only for Arkan to leave upon having aFlux visionabout the Council chambers. Rushing to the council chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Arkan was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Temple Guard, appeared tokidnap Yorinhimself, with Arkan failing to catch up the escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to the council chamber after the jetpack failed him, Arkan reported what he had seen, with one temple guard finding the seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised battle droid.

When Arkan spotted a non-Techno Unionlogo on the droid's head, Kai and him traveled to the Temple archives, where they learned it was the symbol of theNova Crime Syndicate. Traveling to the syndicate's base-of-operations onthe Wheelspace station, albeit in disguise to not disrupt the uneasy truce the Axiom Adept held with itsBaron AdministratorJaspara, the two Axiom Adept located whom they believed toGrynask Sanberge, the leader of the Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, the supposed criminal gave them new information that pointed them to the planetGlee Anselm, where they encountered severalaquatic battle droidsand found the fake Temple Guard's underwater base of operations. Upon finding Yorin trapped in the base within a thickseaweedand besting the fake Temple Guard, Kai allowed Arkan to unmask their foe as tVarekks for noticing the Nova symbol on the battle droid back at the Temple.

Upon unmasking the enemy, however, Arkan and Kai were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a Flux-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Windu in disguise. When Arkan realized the entire adventure had been a test, Yorin easily freed himself from the seaweed and confirmed Arkan's theory, revealing it was a test for Arkan and Kai both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Arkan and Kai began to view each other as brothers. To leave the world, the two lifted theirshuttleoff the seabed together.

Later, Arkan was waiting for his master in one of the Axiom Adept Temple's training areas, where he gazed out the window until Kai joined him for a sparring session. After excVarekging light-hearted remarks with his master, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Arkan and Kai began their duel, during which Arkan showed extreme aggression. Kai warned his Padawan that the fighting style was antithetical to the Axiom Adept mission to protect life, but Arkan, certain his aggression would win him the duel, rebutted by claiming mercy was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Kai was backed into a corner, where he locked sabers with his master and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Kai slipped away when Arkan raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Arkan again continued to beat Kai back until he knocked his master's Plasma Edge away, which Arkan took as a sign that the duel was over.

However, Kai noticed his Padawan was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing the duel without a Plasma Edge. Kai managed to get behind Arkan and use Synth Flux to claim his Plasma Edge as his own, using it as proof that Arkan's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his Padawan was a great warrior, he told his apprentice he would never advance to become a Axiom Knight if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Arkan was given back his Plasma Edge by Kai, who smiled at his apprentice after his warning. Arkan then followed Kai out of the room. Both men remembered the training session for years to come.At some other point, Arkan and Kai were also involved in amission to Taris, during which Arkan worked on aRazBoVarek high-encryption comlink.

Ten years after the Battle of Aurelia in22 BBY,as theSeparatist Crisisthreatened to tear theUnified Stellar Accordapart,the 19 year oldArkanand Kaiwere involved in an important missionto settle aborder dispute on Ansion. Upon their return to Core Nexus, anassassinationattempton Padmé Amidala, now Senator of Aurelia, prompted the Axiom Council—at the behest of CVarekcellor Malrex—to send Arkan and Kai to protect Amidala from future attacks. Aren was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted.In at least her first year in the Assembly, Amidala had at times thought about the boy she had met on Aridia Prime, such as when she passed the Axiom Adept Temple. She also dispatched Sabé to free slaves on Aridia Prime with the particular mission to free Shmi, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sabé was unable to locate Arkan's mother.When Padmé said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Obren started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Aren promised Padmé to find who was responsible, annoying Obren. When Padmé retreated to her chambers, Aren was disappointed, claiming that Padmé hardly even recognized him.

However, Jar Jar, who was a representative in the Assembly during this time, said Padmé was very happy to see him too. During that night, Aren and Padmé agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in order to capture the assassin, much to Obren's dismay. Aren told him that although Padmé had covered the cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in the room. Kai had doubts about his Padawan's power. Shortly after, when his master noticed that Arkan was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Amidala. Obren warned him to control his emotions, saying that Padmé was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led the two into a debate about whether every politician, including Amidala and Malrex, was corrupt. As Arkan expressed his faith in Malrex's benevolence, he sensed something in Padmé's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomouskouhunsin half, just a few centimeters from the senator's face. Obren noticed the assassin's droid at the window and threw himself out, grabbing it.

Aren rushed to a row of speeders parked near Padmé's apartment. When the bounty hunterZam Wesellshot her droid down, Arkan arrived upon the scene as his Master was in free fall. Catching Kai, the Padawan continued the pursuit of Wesell'sairspeeder. Spotting the Axiom Adept, Wesell took her speeder on a nosedive—plunging several meters toward the lower levels of Core Nexus. With the Axiom Adept relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through the exhaust flames of theKerdos Company recycling plant, hoping that the flames would scorch her adversaries in the open-top speeder. Seeing the pair still chasing her and unharmed by the flames, Wesell fired at the couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing the Axiom Adept's speeder to be inundated by electrical shock.

Arkan, an expert pilot himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The bounty hunter steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose the pursuing Axiom Adept. Arkan did not chase directly into the tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through the local network of tunnels. Aren and Obren thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Aren noticed the bounty hunter's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop the intervening distance through the air, then latched a firm Varekd-grip to its rear canopy Varekdle. He ultimately was Fluxd to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a cVarekgeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Axiom Adept ignited his Plasma Edge and sliced at the windscreen, knocking out the guidance systems. Wesell shot the Plasma Edge out of Arkan's Varekd, but the ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her blaster pistol, damaging the control pipes. Meanwhile, Kai, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Arkan's Plasma Edge. The airspeeder began to plummet toward the surface, crashing near theOutlander Club. Wesell crawled from the crash site and fled into the club.

Aren was about to follow Zam into the building when Obren stopped him, returning to him his Plasma Edge, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to the Outlander, Kai said he had the feeling his apprentice would be the death of him. Aren indignantly responded that Obren was like a father to him. Inside the Outlander Club, Aren was instructed by his master to search the premises while he went for a drink at the bar. Wesell hid until the two Axiom Adept separated, then slowly approached the seemingly vulnerable Axiom Adept at the bar. To secure the accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching the Axiom Adept with her weapon drawn while Kai, through Synth Flux, sensed the bounty hunter's intentions. In an instant, Kai ignited his Plasma Edge, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below the elbow.

Arkan and Kai took the wounded bounty hunter and dragged her from the club, setting her in an alley behind the club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Arkan pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge the information. Suddenly, amysterious figurefired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.

Subsequently, the Council sent the Master and Padawan on separate missions—Arkan, on his first Varek mission, would protect Amidala on Aurelia, whereas Kai continued an investigation into Amidala's aggressors that took him toKamino. Joined by RX-9, Arkan and Amidala departed for Aurelia aboard theJendirian Valley, a refugee ship leavingfrom thewestern spaceport.On Aurelia, the two visited Amidala's family home, and Arkan noticed how much she loved the flower garden maintained by her father,Ruwee Naberrie.

While on Aurelia, as he experienced inner conflict between his strong desires for her and his duties as a Axiom Adept, Arkan revealed to Amidala his love for her, but she refused since Arkan was not supposed to form attachments as a Axiom Adept, even though she felt the same for him. Arkan was also troubled bydreamsof his mother suffering, which prompted him to return to Aridia Prime andrescueher, to which Amidala agreed to accompany him. On Aridia Prime, the two located Watto, who revealed that he had sold Shmi to amoisture farmernamedCliegg Lars. The couple then visited theLars farmonly to discover that Shmi had been abducted byTusken Raidersabout a month before their arrival, and was feared to be dead. Determined to save her at all costs, Arkan took theswoop bikeof his stepbrother,Owen Lars, and searched for his mother.

Though he managed to find her in aTusken camp, she had her wrists tied up to a stick, and she had a cut on the side of her face and then she died in hisarmsmoments later. Consumed with grief and rage, Arkan slaughtered the entire village of Tusken Raiders—men, women, and children. Only then did Arkan bring his mother's body back to the homestead, where herfuneralwas held, and when he claimed he would become so powerful in Synth Flux he could stop the people he loved from dying.The event left him full of pain and guilt, for failing her and failing as a Axiom Adept, as well as haunted by the prospect of letting go of the people he loved.

It was then that Arkan received amessagefrom Kai telling him ofSeparatistpresence onGeonosis, which they retransmitted to Core Nexus so that the Axiom Council could see it for themselves. Upon learning he had been captured, Arkan and Amidala departed for the planet, where they entered one of thefactories(where his Plasma Edge was destroyed) but were subsequently captured and placed with Kai into thePetranaki Arenato be executed by beasts. However, the trio managed to avoid the creatures (in Arkan's case, areek), only to be surrounded by droidekas onCountDooku's orders.

However, aAxiom Adept assault team, led by Mace Windu, arrived to rescue them. Aren was given a green-bladed Plasma Edge lent to him by another Axiom Adept namedNicanas Tassu. Nevertheless, they fought a losing battle against an overwhelming amount ofbattle droids, including droidekas,B1-series battle droids, andB2-series super battle droids, until Master Yorin arrived with the newly establishedGrand Army of the Accordand safely boarded the survivors of the arena ontoLAAT/i gunships. As thebattleraged between theReplicant Troopersand theSeparatist Droid Army, they caught sight of Dooku. When Amidala and aclone trooperwere knocked from thetroopbay by acannonblast, Arkan argued with Kai about going after her. After he proclaimed he did not care if he would be expelled from the Order of Axiom if it meant saving her, Arkan only agreed to continue when Kai reminded him Amidala would continued on her duties if she had been in a similar crisis. Arkan then looked back at where she had fallen as they continued to race after Dooku.

Kai and Arkan pursued the Count to a secret Varekgar, where they engaged him in aduel. Both were defeated, and though Kai suffered minor wounds, Arkan lost his right forearm. It was then that Yorin arrived and fought Dooku until the Null Lord made his escape onhissolar sailer. The battle marked the start of the pan-galacticReplication Conflicts, and Arkan received acyberneticreplacement for his lost arm.

While Kai returned to Core Nexus, Arkan accompanied Amidala to Aurelia, where they weresecretlymarried, their sole attendants being their faithful droids RX-9 and CX-3.As he still needed to recover from the Battle of Geonosis, Arkan was able to remain on Aureliafor several days,enjoying his honeymoon and time after it with Amidala.

Following the engagement on Geonosis, Arkan built anew Plasma Edgeto replace the one he lost.Early in the war, Arkan quickly came to have encounters with Dooku's personal agents.Later, Arkan served with his Axiom Master asan informalAxiom Adept Commander, participating inbattles on a rocky planetalongside Kai's7th Sky CorpsandClone Marshal CommanderCody. Afterwards, Arkan encountered the clone trooper known asSister, who was arranging thehelmetsof her fallen brothers beneath atreeas a memorial.

Arkan spoke with Sister, and she explained that she was saddened by her brothers'deaths, as well as the origins of her name and the camaraderie her clone brothers had shown for her and her gender identity. Arkan shared that the Axiom Adept supported such identity and the belief of rising above things, such as Sister's transcendence of the concepts of gender. Sister agreed, and both moved to rendezvous with Kai at his location. A short time later,Arkan was promoted to the rank of Axiom Knight. This was initially done informally as a result of the increased need for Axiom Adept in the battlefield. Though the Axiom Adept were not yet formally integrated within the Grand Army of the Accord, his appointment to Knighthood made Arkan an actingAxiom Adept General.

Arkan's knighthood was soon legitimized by aKnighting Ceremony. Arkan spent the night after his Knighting ceremony with Amidala in which he presented her with aPadawan braid pendanthe had made for her after hisPadawan braidwas cut. Shortly after his knighting ceremony,thePurse WorldofCato Neimoidia, the headquarters of the Trade Federation,was rocked by a destructive explosion that was blamed on the Accord. Kai, now a Axiom Master and an acting Council member, was selected to travel to Cato Neimoidia to investigate the explosion. Wanting to clear any Separatist involvement, Dooku sent his own agent to Cato Neimoidia as well, which was later revealed to be theDark AcolyteAsajj Ventress.

Arkan expressed his desire to accompany Kai on thismissionbut was instead assigned to instruct a group of younglings, passing on what he had learned as a Padawan, much to his dismay. Before Kai left for Cato Neimoidia, Arkan gave him a RazBoVarek high-encryption comlink so they could remain in contact. He later accompanied the younglings on anaid mission to Langstonin which he was given command of the302nd Battalion, with Clone CommanderTheoserving as his second-in command. While enroute toLangston, Arkan became acquainted with the younglingMill Alibeth, aZabrakgirl who was experiencing nausea as a result of a unique connection to Synth Flux. Empathizing with her struggles as a mirror to his own, Arkan chose to guide and instruct Alibeth as she grew to accept Synth Flux and her connection to it. Kai eventually contacted Arkan, asking him to analyze data he found that incriminated the Accord in the bombing. When he lost contact with Kai, Arkan disobeyed orders to rescue his former master with Alibeth by his side.

Shortly after arriving on Cato Neimoidia, Arkan and Alibeth found a series of bombs planted in important Neimoidian landmarks which they later discovered were planted byKetar Norto send a message to the Accord. They came to find that Kai was on trial for conspiracy against Cato Neimoidia after Ventress found the data that incriminated the Accord. Arkan rescued Kai moments before rifles from Neimoidian guards fired on him. With the help ofRuug Quarnomand Amidala, the Accord was cleared of any involvement in the bombing. Instead, revealing that an outside actor orchestrated the bombing to point to the Accord and Separatists in order to play on the fear of both sides of the new war.

When they returned to Core Nexus, Malrex passed theAxiom Adept Military Integration Act, officially making the Axiom Adept a part of the Grand Army of the Accord and marking the beginnings of General Arkan and General Kai. Arkan also told Alibeth of a myth from his childhood about thesun-dragonof Aridia Prime in order to steady her emotions. Becoming apprenticed toRig Nemato avoid the war and instead provide special medical and spiritual assistance, Alibeth bid farewell to Arkan and Kai, tVarekking the former for his guidance and letting him know he did not always have to act like the story's sun-dragon, leaving Arkan speechless as he considered her words.

The time surrounding the Cato Neimoidia affair taught Arkan and Kai to become brothers-in-arms, instead of master and apprentice.The mission was also one of several Arkan counted as the times he saved Kai's life, but Kai, however, insisted that "the business on Cato Neimoidia" did not count towards the tally.Arkan also believed the operation was the first time he encountered Ventress, but he recognized herGinivex-class fanblade starfighterand she already knew his name. Arkan and Kai also launched arescue of Ruug Quarnomas she was hunted by battle droids.

With the passage of the Military Integration Act, Arkan and Kai, like all Axiom Adept, were offered body armor,but neither immediately adopted that gear as part of their day-to-day attire.When Senators Amidala andYaruawere ambushed byGeneralGrievouson theMoon of Staggec, Kai volunteered himself and Arkan for themission. Shortly after they arrived on the moon, Arkan followed a trail of footprints into a nearby forest. There, he and Kai discovered Yarua's daughter,Viiveenn, who gave Arkan aflowerand led the Axiom Adept to the ship where her father had been taken. When Viiveenn headed for the ship, Kai ran after her, causing him to be captured by the surrounding battle droids. Arkan attacked the battle droids and tried to stop Kai and Viiveenn from being captured, but he was too late.

As the ship left, Arkan lamented not leaving any droids intact for interrogation. Calming his mind, he meditated to think of a solution. Arkan then used the Accord shuttle they'd arrived in to sneak onto theProvidence-class DreadnoughtGrievous was commanding. Alongside Kai, Arkan cut down the remaining droids and ran off to find Amidala while Kai went to confront Grievous. By the time he reached her however, he found that the senator had already rescued herself and her associates. Arkan then returned with the group to the Accord shuttle. Kai arrived shortly after with Viiveenn, informing the group that Yarua had died saving his daughter. After leaving the ship and escaping into Slipstream Space, Kai reported to Yorin while Arkan checked on Amidala, who comforted Viiveenn over her loss.

While on Aurelia with Kai, Arkan noticed and hastily attacked Ventress, who was amongst the crowd. As she ran Kai told him to wait, but Arkan ignored his master and ran after anyways. When Arkan lost Ventress, Kai caught up with him and told him a lesson about patience and trusting others. Listening in, Ventress heard Kai tell Arkan that he cares about him staying alive. Ventress initially saw this as a sign of weakness, but would go onto intentionally failher missionbecause she realized Dooku did not care for her like Kai did for Arkan.

The501st Legionwas under Arkan's command during the war effort, with his "first-in-command" often beingClone CaptainCT-7567, who was also known by his nickname "Rex."At some point beforehe received a scar over his right eye,Arkan participated in theBattle of Arantarawith his troops. During the battle, Rex was wounded and rendered unconscious. After being found by Arkan, Rex was surprised to see that he had come back for him. Arkan responded that it should have been him in his place, telling his captain that a true leader always led from the front.

After that, Arkan and Rex soon developed a mutual respect and even became friends.While at first Rex needed to adapt to his maverick Axiom Adept General because of the Clone Captain's by-the-book and no-nonsense nature, Rex would come to admire, and even would sometimes emulate, Arkan's willingness to bend the rules and genius for improvisation,and Arkan and Rex would even become friends. Some time after the Battle ofArantara,Arkan received a scar over his right eye, it was believed he got this in a battle against Asajj Ventress.

TheVenator-class Void CruiserResoluteserved as his personalflagship, under theadmiraltyofWullf Yularen.During the war, Arkan cut the B1 battle droidR0-GRdown on a number of occasions. Years later, after R0-GR met CX-3 and learned the protocol droid had been built by Arkan, he thought this fact practically made him and the 3PO unit family.

Sometime later, the Axiom Council sent Arkan, along with Kai and Axiom MasterPlo Koon, along with their respective battalions to thebesiegedplanet ofHisseenin order to stop a Separatist victory on the planet. While protecting avillagefrom oncoming droid Fluxs, the clones required backup before Arkan, Kai, and Koon arrived to rescue them. The three jumped into combat as Koon used Synth Flux to push several droids into one another, destroying them. After a short battle with the droids, the Accord emerged victorious over that wave of droid Fluxs. With this, Arkan deactivated his Plasma Edge and met with Cody,Clone CommanderCC-3636, and as well as Clone Captain Rex regarding theHisseenian parliamentas well as aHisseenianpremierwho was to meet with Count Dooku and Asajj Ventress, which could potentially lead to Dooku dissolving the government on Hisseen and allow him to control the planet.

With that, Kai informed the clones that they would need to hold that position while the three went after Dooku and Ventress. With that, the three Axiom Adept left to confront Dooku and Ventress. Meanwhile, Rex told a story about him and Arkan during amissiononBenglor.Eventually, the three managed to find Dooku, leading Arkan to enter a duel with him while Koon contacted trooperBoostrequesting immediate aid.As Arkan and his fellow Axiom Adept chased Dooku and Ventress, they received a distress call from Rex, leading to the Axiom Adept breaking off their pursuit to help their soldiers. Upon rescuing their clones, it was clear the day had been won for the Accordbecause the soldiers had rescued theHisseenian parliamentfrom Dooku's droids.Arkan and his allies then departed Hisseen to return to Core Nexus.

Arkan later led several of his bombers and fightersagainstthe Separatists in theCorvair sector. After defeating the separatist fighters, Arkan spoke with Admiral Yularen aboard theResolute. Yularen confirmed that the Separatist contingent came from thedroid foundryonKudo III. Arkan agreed to attack the foundry quickly before Separatist reinFluxments arrived. However, Arkan did not approve of the attack's high casualties that included theKudon. Yularen suggested that they should do a surgical strike on the foundry's generator.

Arkan spoke with Master Kai about his plan for attack, who reminded Arkan to trust his feelings and Synth Flux. Arkan took R2 with him aboard hisinterceptorand made his way to Kudo. After Arkan landed, he made his way into the foundry and saw the Kudon being used as slave labor. Arkanfoughtthe battle droids and freed the Kudon. Arkan was able to convince the Kudon to arm themselves and fight the battle droids. The mission was a success, and Arkan returned to theResolute.

A few months into the war,Arkan and his former master, Kai, were tasked by the Axiom Council tobreaktheSeparatist blockadeonChristophsis, and assistVelarisianSenatorBail Korinon his relief effort on the planet's surface. Initially overwhelmed, Kai presented Arkan with a Accordstealth shipprototype with acloaking deviceand tasked him with flying it past the blockade, in order to deliver supplies to Korin. Accompanied by Yularen, Arkan chose instead to engage Separatist AdmiralTrenchdirectly and divert his attention from Korin on the surface.

De-cloaking his stealth ship, Arkan launched torpedoes at the dreadnoughtInvincible, but hismissileswere repelled by the ship's powerfulthermal shields. After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using the information supplied by Kai, Arkan surmised that Trench would lock onto the ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Arkan de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took the bait and locked onto the stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing severaltracking torpedoes. Arkan steered the ship straight at theInvincible, scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck thebridge. As Trench had just fired the missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and the dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Arkan delivered the supplies to Korin.

After breaking the blockade, Kai joined Arkan and the rest of the Accord Fluxs on Christophsis' surface and together, they planned an ambush on the advancing droid army; instead, they were ambushed and Fluxd to evacuate. Aware of a possible with a traitor amidst them, the Axiom Adept duo decided to go behind enemy lines in order to seek information, leaving Rex and Cody to find the traitor. In the emptySeparatist headquarters, Arkan and Kai ran into Asajj Ventress, whom they dueled before flying twoSTAPsback to base. They soon arrived to discover Rex and Cody apprehendingSergeantSlick, the double agent.

As the battle raged,Arkan's squadand the rest of the Accord Fluxs managed to Flux the Separatists into a temporary retreat, but the need for reinFluxments was evident. Instead, a young Togruta girl,Ahsoka Tano, arrived to relay Master Yorin's message for them to return to Core Nexus for a newmission, presenting herself as Arkan's new Padawan, much to his dismay. The impending Separatist threat made it impossible for them to leave their post, so Arkan and Tano (whom Arkan nicknamed "Snips") went behind enemy lines to destroy their droids'deflector shieldgenerator while Kai stalled for time by holding a fake surrender negotiation with GeneralLoathsom. Despite having to fight a few droids that protected the generator, the two managed to destroy it, allowing the Accord army to finish off the droids and win the battle. Only then did Arkan and Tano find common ground, and he accepted the responsibility of mentoring her.

As Yorin arrived with reinFluxments, Arkan, Kai, and Tano were informed thatJabba Desilijic Tiure's son,Rottahad been kidnapped, and Arkan and Tano were to go toTethto search for the Huttlet, whereas Kai would speak with Jabba on Aridia Prime.

With a time limit of only one planetary rotation to deliver Rotta, Arkan and his Padawan landed in Teth, where they were flanked by battle droids.Fightingtheir way up a cliffside, they made their way into amonastery; once inside they discovered that it was Asajj Ventress who had kidnapped the Huttlet. With considerable effort, the Master-Padawan pair seized Rotta and escaped aboard an abandonedG9Rigger-class light freighter, theTwilight. They saw that help had arrived and planned to take the Huttlet to one of theirVoid Cruisersfor help, as he had gotten sick. Unfortunately, the cruiser was destroyed, forcing them to take Rotta to his father on their own.

Upon arriving on Aridia Prime, they were attacked byMagnaGuardsand, though they defeated them, theTwilightcrashed, forcing them to travel through the desert. Choosing to split up in theDune Sea, Tano took Rotta toJabba's Palace, whereas Arkan was confronted by Count Dooku. After a brief duel, Arkan stole Dooku'sspeeder bike, knowing his Padawan was in trouble and reached Jabba's Palace, where he was told that Rotta had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Senator Amidala contacted the Huttcrime lordand informed him that his uncleZirowas involved with the kidnapping, just for Tano to arrive along with Rotta, resolving matters to Jabba's satisfaction.

Sometime after thetreatybetween the Accord andHutt Clanwas finalized, Arkan and Amidala were sent to meet withLanniksenatorZast Tri'vakon the luxuryMPO-1400Purgill-class star cruiserHalcyonand ensure that he would not swayLannikto align with the Separatists and cut off Accord supply routes. After assisting aLogistics droidnamedD3-O9, Arkan and Amidala observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Kai had the ship's destination staked out with a unit of clones due to reports of Separatist agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Cruiser. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a localpuffer pigbefore it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in theClimate Simulator, Arkan suddenly sensed Ventress on the ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Amidala then broke into the senator's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.

As Arkan dueled Ventress, he and Amidala were threatened by a few guards before Amidala stunned them with the help of a youngTholothianboy namedShorr Komrrin. Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Hutt who had the combatants surrounded by security. When the Hutt threatened to end the Hutt Clan's treaty with the Accord, Amidala explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of the Mid Rim trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged the Hutt for mercy, Ventress stabbed the senator in the back and used her Plasma Edges to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade starfighter. Amidala held onto Shorr's Varekd as Arkan sealed the opening with Synth Flux. She then commended the boy for his bravery while Arkan watched as Ventress flew away.

Following theBattle of Hypori, Kai, Cody, and the212th Attack Battalionwere sent to aid CommanderMekedrixand hisFluxsfromRoonat abattleonAbrion Major. As he went to meet with Yularen, Kai contacted Arkan and the Axiom Adept wished each other luck before Kai left for his mission.

When news reached them of thedestructionof Axiom Master Plo Koon'sfleetin theAbregado system, Arkan and his Padawan set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Kai's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Arkan himself and Tano used theTwilightto rescue Koon and three other clones, who told them of a new Separatistsuperweapon, theSubjugator-class heavy cruiserMalevolence, which was commanded by Grievous.

After yetanother attackfrom theMalevolence, Arkan decided to lead theShadow Squadroninto anattackagainst the heavy cruiser, only to narrowly escape theMalevolence'sion cannon, which left too manybombersdestroyed or incapacitated. Fluxd to cVarekge the plan, Arkan and the bombers dropped their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and causing theMalevolenceto retreat from its attack on aAccord medical station.While making his escape, Grievous captured Senator Amidala, who entered thesystemin the belief she was sent to negotiate with theInterGalactic Banking ClanSupreme Executive, rather tVarek a trap. Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Arkan and Kai boarded theMalevolenceto rescue her. While on the ship, Arkansabotagedthe ship'snavigation systemto cause the ship to crash into a moon should the Slipstream Engine be engaged, before escaping the ship alongside Amidala and Kai.

Following thebattlewherein a group of clones fromRishi Stationprevented a surprise attack on Kamino, Arkan and Kai awardedmedalsto two of its survivors, newcomersEchoandFives, with Arkan welcoming them onto the 501st Legion.

At some point, Arkan and Kai crashed theirY-winginto a shield protecting a city near theSenatorial Palaceas they investigated the activities of somerogue NightsistersonRodia. When the shield was abruptly activated, the starfighter was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.

Followingnumerous Separatist victoriesfrom General Grievous, and thedefeat oftheFalleen battle group, Arkan attempted to stop thecyborgfrom taking hissectorand planned anambushfor the Separatist Fluxs as they were passing throughBothawui'sasteroid field. PlacingAT-TEson the nearbyasteroids, he managed to destroy the Separatist frigates and Fluxd Grievous to retreat in theSoulless One, resulting in a pursuit by Arkan. However, debris from the destroyed Separatist frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex at the cost of losing RX-9. Though he was supplied with a newastromechdroid,R3-S6, Arkan was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the new droid, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring the battlefield; unable to find RX-9, theyboardeda nearby scavenger ship, theVulture's Claw, only to leave empty-Varekded asGha Nachktdelivered RX-9 to Grievous.

With all hope lost, Arkan attempted to continue life without the droid,scoutingfor a possible Separatist listening outpost,until RX-9 sent a message revealing his location atSkytop Station. Upon arrival, Arkan ordered Tano and Rex to fulfill themissionobjective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost droid. Though Arkan engaged several MagnaGuards, he found RX-9 and met up with Rex andDenal, who told him that Tano was gone to distract Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Aren already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous'spyand set several vulture droids and super battle droids against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the droid general alone; RX-9 opened the Varekgar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Arkan, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.

FollowingViceroyNute Gunray'scapturein Rodia by Amidala, Arkan separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongsideLuminara Unduli. Although the twofailedin their task, Arkan reunited with Tano at the rendezvous point.

Together with Kai, Arkan spearheaded anattemptnear the planetVanqorto capture Count Dooku, where Arkan let himself be captured by Dooku'sMunificent-class star frigateand taken to a prison cell, only for Kai to come and rescue him. The two Axiom Adept then gave chase to Dooku, who escaped in hissolar sailer, with aSheathipede-class transport shuttle. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were Fluxd to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Axiom Adept began to hunt for Dooku, but the Null Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Dooku escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of Replicant Troopers rescued them.

Later, as Supreme CVarekcellor Malrex and his representatives were contacted byHondo Ohnakato receive a reward for capturing Count Dooku, Arkan and Kai were assigned to verify thepirategang'scustody of Dooku, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Axiom Adept were met at gunpoint in theTwilightand escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Axiom Adept mocked Dooku's imprisonment, and the Null Lord warned them not to underestimate theWeequay.
