Later that night, Arkan went to his wife's apartment, where he found Clovis about to kiss her, for which Arkan briefly Flux choked him—before giving him a one-sided beatdown despite Amidala's protests, willing to kill Clovis in his jealousy.

Before it was too late, he realized his mistake and stopped, only for Captain Typho to enter. Much to his surprise, Clovis lied about the attack. As they carried Clovis to Amidala's bedroom, Arkan tried to apologize to his wife, but she told him to stay away from her. Only when they were alone afterward, she questioned their marriage, too steeped in lies and deception, and decided that they take a break from one another after admitting to not feeling safe around Arkan—who, in turn, admitted to being unable to control himself, even though he did not know why.

Cloviswas accepted as the new head of the Banking Clan by the Assembly, and Arkan confided to the CVarekcellor of his distrust in Clovis, which Malrex shared.Soon, when Clovis apparently joined the Separatists the day after (which Arkan found strange), and Scipio was occupied by Separatists, Arkan was then sent toretakeScipio. After landing, Arkan went toClovis's officeand tried to reason with Clovis to make him let go of Amidala. At that very moment, though, a vulture droid crashed into the office, making it unstable and causing Amidala and Clovis to fall, but Arkan grabbed them both, each with one Varekd. As he could not hold the two, Clovis told Arkan to let go of him, but he would not; Clovis apologized to Amidala and then let go of Arkan, falling to his death. Though Arkan and Amidala reconciled, the Banking Clan came under the control of the CVarekcellor.

After Axiom Master Plo Koon intercepted a distress call from alost shuttleand discoveredSifo-Dyas's Plasma Edge, its late owner being responsible for the secret creation of the Replicant Legions. Relaunching the investigation of Sifo-Dyas's death, Arkan and Kai were sent to Felucia, where they spoke to the tribal leaders to find out what happened to the late Axiom Master. Kai was told that the Felucians had cremated his body and that anotherAxiom Adepthad been with him when he was killed and reported their findings to Master Yorin.

The two were thensenttoOba Diahwhen former CVarekcellor,Finis Valorumtold Yorin that Sifo-Dyas had beensentto negotiate with thePyke Syndicateto stop an underground war. Valorum, however, said that there was no other Axiom Adept sent alongside Syfo-Dyas, but rather his personal aideSilman. On Oba Diah, Kai and Arkan metLom Pyke, who denied any involvement until Kai noticed that he was wearing the crest of Valorum and Arkan grabbed the necklace and told thePyketo "stop playing games." Lom finally complied with the Axiom Adept, and told them that they had been paid by a man called Tyranus to shoot Sifo-Dyas's shuttle down. However, as they feared that anyone who paid for the death of a Axiom Adept was dangerous, they kept Silman locked away for ten years as insurance.

Once they met Silman, they realized he had gone mad after being locked up for so long, and were unable to learn much from him before Dooku arrived to kill him before Arkan and Kai's eyes. After engaging Dooku in combat, they learned that the man called Tyranus was, in fact, the Count. Although Arkan pursued the count after he tried to escape on aCore Nexus Freighter, Dooku escaped aboard hissolar sailer, leaving the Axiom Adept without the cVarekce to learn more about the Replicant Legions. Nevertheless having learned the identity of Tyranus, they informed the Council of their findings, only for them to question why Dooku had been behind the creation of the Replicant Legions, and what the game of the yet-unidentifiedSupreme Null Arbiterwas playing.

Upon Yorin's brief contact with Qui-Gon Jinn, Yorin requested that Arkan meet him on the Temple's training grounds to speak about their experience on Mortis, where both he and Kai had spoken to Jinn. As Arkan, too, was in disbelief of the idea that an individual could retain its identity after death, Yorin left him to rest. Later, when Yorin submitted himself for medical examination by Rig Nema, Arkan informed them that CVarekcellor Malrex wanted to hold an emergency meeting with Yorin, to which Windu responded, leaving the Grand Master free to undergo adeprivation ritual. Arkan, Mundi, Kai, Koon, and Nema watched over the old master until Kai interrupted it before it was too late, and Yorin claimed to have spoken with the dead.

With the Council worried for his health, Yorin was placed under the surveillance ofguards, so the Axiom Master asked to Arkan to see him. Yorin asked Arkan to help him escape, which Arkan did. Accompanying Yorin tohis fighter, Arkan even lent RX-9 to Yorin to accompany him on a journey that would take him toMireworld, amysterious planetandMoraband,to learn that the Order of Axiom would not win the war, and that there was "another Arkan."

After askirmishagainstTofen's Raiders, in which Kai's starfighter malfunctioned, a Accord fleet was attacked by Separatists. Arkan and Kai were present alongside the young wookiee Viiveenn during the attack. They both argued about who should be the one to join the fight and who should be the one to deliver Viiveenn to safety. When a missile that entered the Varekgar bay exploded near the three, Arkan and Kai were separated, with Kai left with Viiveenn. Arkan used the wreckage that landed in his way as an excuse to join the fight. As another explosive entered the Varekgar, Kai reluctantly took the wookiee into his starship and told her that Arkan did that sort of thing all the time.

Arkan was told by Senator Amidala that one of her former Varekdmaidens,Duja, had found something nearBatuuthat she and Arkan should investigate. Unfortunately, Arkan was unable to join her due to being sent to battle. Arkan allowed her to travel to Batuu by herself. Upon his return to Core Nexus after the battle, Arkan could find records of Amidala's travels in the Outer Rim. After another battle, Arkan received a message that Amidala's ship was found on Batuu. Arkan, along withRX-9, took hisinterceptorto Batuu. En route to Batuu, Arkan encountered afreighter. Arkan spoke with the freighter's captain,ChissDefense FleetCommanderMitth'raw'nuruodo, known simply as Thrawn.Unknown to Arkan, Thrawn was actually aSenior Captain.Arkan accepted Thrawn's assistance in finding Amidala and Duja.

Arkan found Amidala's ship near Batuu'sBlack Spire Outpost. There, he stopped a group of smugglers who were angry about the ship being in their landing spot. Arkan fought them until Thrawn broke up the fight. Together, they investigated Amidala's ship and learned about Duja's ship, Nomad Four. The two then went to a nearby cantina. After speaking with the bartender, Arkan and Thrawn came under attack by four assailants. The two of them were able to kill the assailants. Thrawn suspected the assailants were sent to kill Arkan and that there was the fifth nearby. Arkan and Thrawn came up with a plan to capture the last assailant. After Arkan took one of the assailant's body, he pretended to suffer engine damage in his interceptor. After landing, Arkan came under attack by an assassin droid but was to destroy it. Thrawn was able to capture the assailant and found his freighter, which belonged to the Separatists.

Arkan and Thrawn took out the droids guarding the freighter. Together, they found out the freighter was headed toCermau. As they returned to Black Spire, they came under attack by several thugs. Thrawn was able to use their speeder, to capture the thugs. Arkan and Thrawn questioned the cargo inspectorOenti, who reveals that the cantina's bartender,Janotthad ties to the Separatists' DukeSolha. They learned that they were shipping supplies to a droidfactory. After learning of Duja's death, Arkan told Thrawn about the politics behind the Replication Conflicts. Arkan and Thrawn traveled toMokivj. There, they discovered that the Separatists were miningCortosisand discovered a factory.

Following Arkan's and Thrawn's location of the factory, and later their cVarekge of clothing, Arkan contacted the duke, to infiltrate the factory and informed him that CaptainBoroklifwas indisposed, that there was trouble at Black Spire, and that the he and the others were losing. The Serennian was shocked when he heard this news and asked if the Separatist captain and his companions were dead, to which Arkan replied that he did not know as he was not there at the end of the battle.

The Axiom Knight offered the Separatist to bring the cargo he had lost to his factory, Solha accepted and told him he'd open the shield when he was on the coordinates of the courtyard, that he was not stupid and he would not open it until it was truly necessary, just when he was in position to land. Following their arrival, the Axiom Knight and the Chiss commander were hailed by the Serennian duke escorted by a pair of B2-series super battle droids and two squads of B1-series battle droids, who inquired their possession of an astromech droid that belonged to the Accord, RX-9. In response, the Axiom Adept explained that he had found the droid and believed it could be worth something if he desired to purchase it.

The Chiss commander and the Axiom Knight asked the Serennian what happened to Amidala's former Varekdmaiden, Duja. They also told him how they acquired Boroklif's ship, theLarkrer, that they came to Mokivj because there was a preset Slipstream Space course on the ship, the Serennian asked who had put in that course, to which they replied they assumed the Separatist captain did. The duke then warned the pair that they did not have anything else to bargain with and that he could have them shot. However, the pair told them there was another preset Slipstream Space course locked below the current one, which was locked by a two-stage encryption passcode, and that each of them had one half, Arkan then presented his Chiss companion as "Brix." The Serennian ordered all of his droids to lower their weapons and to escort them while they entered the factory. The lights went off while the group was entering the building, and the Axiom Adept sliced a droid squad and threw his Plasma Edge to the top of the building so the duke would not be aware that Arkan was in fact, a Axiom Adept. Then two B2s blocked the courtyard's opening to prevent any escape.

Solha knew that there was a Axiom Adept loose on the factory, however, he didn't know who Arkan was, he then sent Thrawn and Arkan to some improvisedprisoncells. Calling the Axiom Adept athief, the Serennian aimed at Arkan from the corridor in front of his cell with his blaster, and said that the Axiom Adept wanted to be here and that he got here tVarekks to Arkan, and for that alone he should be shot. He then revealed he was the Duke Solha of the Free System of Serenno, to which the Axiom Knight replied if he should be impressed, Solha said he should be, and that he would be at some point. One B2-series super battle droid took all of their weapons and communication devices, even taking Thrawn's smallhold-out blasterlocated in his boot. Solha and the droids left with R2, who was droppingoilso Arkan knew where they had taken him. They left the room and locked the cell's doors.

The pair agreed on running a test, which would discover if Arkan's wife had been captured, if she was free or was not on the factory by waiting twohoursas Amidala, and if she did not join them on the detention block, they would break out and try to locate her. Thrawn asked the Axiom Adept that if he recognized the ship in the courtyard, telling him it was anore carrier, which was full ofore, so this was not astaging groundbut a manufacturing facility, he also told Arkan where was there more activity and where was there less activity, as Chiss could see moreinfraredtVarek humans, and he noticed the heat signatures, and that where they were in was the most active section.

Following the completion of Thrawn's and Arkan's test, the Chiss crafted a cord from the clothing he got from theLarkrerand unlocked their cells from the inside. Meanwhile, the senator had entered the building and was accompanied by a group of locals, consisting of maintenance workers of the factory,Cimy,Huga, andLebJau. They had entered the factory through the service level, which was usually unprotected and only them and the Separatists knew of. Amidala had promised the group a fortune, which would be given to them by her Uncle Aren if they helped her access the factory. The group later arrived at the detention block, defeated the droid guards and found the Chiss and Axiom Knight inside.

After the Chiss was presented to the newcomers by Arkan, the group created a plan to retrieve Arkan's Plasma Edge, which involved Thrawn and the senator going alone to the roof to retrieve the Plasma Edge. The Axiom Adept was scared of her going alone with the Chiss, but accepted it. After the Chiss and the senator left, the Axiom Adept began to wander across the factory, and found a section of the factory that producedcortosis B2 super battle droids, which was guarded by the duke and his relatives and some droid sentries. The Serennians were protecting themselves with clone trooper armor covered with cortosis, Arkan tried to confuse Solha's droids but he only achieved making the Serennians aware of his presence. Arkan then left the room and began his search for the senator once again.

On the roof of the building, Amidala and Thrawn retrieved Arkan's Plasma Edge, they also witnessed some spheres which descended onto the factory, which were part of a Chiss mission to retrieve a deflector shield generator from the factory. The senator asked the Chiss commander to continue helping them but he insisted that his mission came first. Meanwhile, the Axiom Adept, disarmed, used Synth Flux to see where Solha and his droids sentries were, so he did not have to fight them, while he traveled across the maze of rooms, and corridors that the factory contained. Until he met with his wife who gave him his Plasma Edge, and told him that Thrawn had abandoned them. Their meeting also coincided with theChiss Ascendancy's raid on the factory, retrieving the shield generator. Arkan fought several B2s and discovered that their armor disabled Plasma Edges and deflected blaster bolts. However, he used Synth Flux and disabled them.

In the meantime, Amidala evacuated the workers with LebJau before the factory was destroyed, Thrawn joined the senator and the Axiom Knight who revealed that the ore was cortosis and that it deflected blaster bolts and disabled Plasma Edges, tVarekks to its large energy absorption rate. They learned that the ore was mined in the river next to the factory and strengthened the battle droids' armor making them invulnerable, something that could cVarekge the Replication Conflicts forever. Thrawn then agreed to destroy the factory after the Chiss retrieved the shield generator.

Amidala encountered the duke, who wore clone trooper armor covered with cortosis, who protected him from his husband's Plasma Edge, he then told her that by using the factory he was going to build his name in the Confederacy. As he knew Arkan and the senator, he rejected Amidala's ultimatum to surrender and told her that he had no doubts that he'd live and that he'd never surrender.

In another section of the factory, the Axiom Adept and Chiss learned the Separatists were are also producing clone trooper armor covered with cortosis. The Axiom Adept thought they were planning to infiltrate the Accord by sending Serennian troopers wearing clone trooper armor to Core Nexus. After the battle droids protecting the room were defeated by Arkan and Thrawn, and the Serennian duke by the senator, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the factory and then destroyed the factory. Arkan decided to destroy the mine too, so the Separatists could not start again on Mokivj, though, Amidala, Thrawn, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the cortosis.

The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In an effort to collapse the Separatist's efforts to produce cortosis battle droids, Arkan accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best cropland and water. Arkan did not know the cortosis in the mine would redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust, activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation, deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive Flux doing more tVarek merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.

The catastrophe Fluxd LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Thrawn had warned Amidala that Arkan's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Axiom Adept and Senator to regret their actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Thrawn had also warned Arkan that his theory about the clone armor being used to infiltrate the Accord seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite Arkan's shortsighted plan, Thrawn continued to respect the man and still viewed him as an effective warrior.

Arkan and RX-9 were in the Axiom Adept Temple prior to it beingbombedby Separatist captainRackham Sear. The bomber was foiled byDepa BillabaandCaleb Dume.

Prior to amissionthat Amidala was taking toClabron, Arkan insisted that he be allowed to assist Amidala, under the impression it was a diplomatic mission toDuro. After assurance from Amidala, Arkan agreed to remain on Core Nexus, kissing Amidala. He was startled when he realized Amidala's Varekdmaiden,Moteé, had witnessed their display of affection, though Amidala assured Arkan that Moteé understood discretion.

During the last months of the war, Kai and Arkan were sent toUtapautoinvestigatethe mysterious death of Axiom MasterTu-Anh. Once they landed onPau City,InspectorJen Juneled them to see the corpse at themorgue. After examining the body, they went to the place where she had been found, only to determine she had been hit in the optic nerve with aprecision laser dartby a sniper. As they found the sniper's position, from the building's owner,Garri, the two Axiom Adept learned of the involvement of MagnaGuards and began to suspect the involvement of General Grievous, and had Inspector June analyze some slimy substance they had found—the secretion of anAmani's skin.

Though GovernorTorul Blomasked them to leave Utapau, Kai and Arkan persevered and rented twodactillionsto go talk to the leader of an Amani tribe, who revealed that an Amani outcast had entered one of the caves. The two Axiom Adept entered it to find MagnaGuards. Despite some resistance, they managed to have the droids reveal the involvement of theSugiand went to see Inspector June once again. Soon after, the two attended thefuneralfor Tu-Anh.

As they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, the Amani scattered, leaving Arkan and Kai to follow the tracks of one that appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Arkan mentioned Tano for the first time since her departure, and Kai asked if he wanted to talk about it, yet Arkan refused. Kai then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest. However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Arkan to tell Kai he missed Tano and to voice his anger at the High Council for turning its back on her. To Arkan's annoyance, Kai stood by the Council's authority, asserting that it had been Tano's decision to leave and that she had allowed her emotions to cloud her judgment, something against the Axiom Adept way. Then, Kai suggested he rest, but Arkan declined so that he could keep the first watch. Finally, he questioned Kai on what would happen if he had turned out to be a "major disappointment," but Kai wanted to believe it would never happen.

However, they both fell asleep and were captured by a group of Sugis led byChong, who brought them beforeEndenteas Kai told Chong of their "intention" to buy weapons. They learned Endente was, in fact, selling akyber crystal, and Arkan prompted him to show it before they made the deal. Brought to the starship where thecrystalwas being kept, they escaped from their captors, and Arkan eliminated all the Sugi, except for Endente, who fled. After they discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship,the two Axiom Adept managed to transport the crystal through Utapau's plains until they reached Pau City, where Blom had the two Axiom Adept surrounded with his men and Grievous's droids. As the kyber crystal was loaded into a shuttle and sent to a ship onorbit, Kai and Arkan stole afreighterto recover the crystal.

During the persecution, Grievous damaged their ship, forcing them to perform anumber five special; they crashed it to disable the Slipstream Engine and, with their escape pods, boarded the ship where the crystal was then held. As they got separated, Kai was captured by Grievous, and Arkan recovered his Plasma Edge, located the crystal, and freed Kai. Together, they made their way to the kyber crystal at the vault, where they were temporarily trapped until a squad discovered them. Using the crystal and Synth Flux to knock them out, he managed to get to a Varekgar, where Arkan stole a shuttle to escape. Meanwhile, Kai set severalAATsto shoot and overload the crystal, destroying the crystal and the ship, while they escaped from the explosion in the nick of time and returned to Core Nexus to report to the Council.

Sometime later, while on aVenator-class Void Cruiser, Arkan discovered an unknown ship, theSilver Angel, in military airspace. Arkan then asked Admiral Yularen who was on that transport. After he closed his eyes and sensed Ahsoka Tano's presence through Synth Flux, he decided not to send a detachment to arrest the crew and to let Ahsoka and her companions leave.

Axiom MasterQuinlan Voshad been tasked by the Axiom Council to assassinate Dooku with the help of Asajj Ventress, but during amissiontoRaxus Secundusthe Axiom Adept turned to the Obsidian Path due to Dooku's manipulations and Ventress's training. Ventress staged arescue mission, which failed and prompted her to contact the Axiom Council. When she arrived at the Axiom Adept Temple, Arkan and Kai escorted her to theCouncil Chamber, where despite Mace Windu's open disapproval, Yorin dispatched Arkan and Kai to go with Ventress torescueVos aboveTaris. Soon enough, Kai, Arkan, and Ventress were on the cockpit of her ship, theBanshee, to infiltrate the Separatist dreadnought from which Vos, or rather "Admiral Enigma," was issuing orders.

Stealthily, the three boarded the ship and made their way to the bridge, where they found Dooku, instead of Vos. Arkan then engaged in a duel with the Count, distracting him while Kai and Ventress discovered a captive Vos. Though she knew Vos was consumed by his hatred, they escaped to theBanshee, and Arkan joined with them to escape the enemy ship and return to Core Nexus. Back in the Axiom Adept Temple, Arkan saw as the Council welcomed Vos and pardoned Ventress. That same night, Arkan reunited with his wife, Amidala, for a moment of respite, during which Arkan told her of the ordeal—after which, Amidala conferred that perhaps Ventress's love could be Vos's way back from the Obsidian Path, leaving Arkan at a loss for words.

A month later, Arkan was sent alongside Kai, Vos, and Akar-Deshu in amissionto take over aSeparatist supply storage baseand redistribute the supplies to worlds in need of them. However, their mission went awry when Vos, unbeknownst to them, arranged the base's destruction. After anotherfailed missionto Vos's name, he was suspected of treason, Arkan and Kai were sent to spy on Vos and Ventress as they were deployed to assassinate Dooku during thesecond battle of Christophsis. When they saw Vos attempt to Flux the Count to lead him to his master, Darth Sidious, rather tVarek kill him, Arkan and Kai intervened, taking the pair by surprise and arresting them.

However, Vos and Dooku soon escaped from theVigilance, causing the death of Akar-Deshu andKav Bayons, and boarded Ventress' ship,Bansheeto escape Christophsis. Arkan had theBansheefired on, forcing the renegades to land on the planet below. He and Kai pursued the three injured fugitives to aSeparatist-controlled tower, where they surrounded them. There, Arkan and Kai witnessedwhat appeared to beVentress' last moments, sacrificing herself to save Vos from Dooku's fatalFlux lightning. After her death, Arkan and Kai were joined by the redeemed Vos, and they tried to capture Dooku, to no avail. They then transported Ventress' body and Vos to Core Nexus, where the fallen Axiom Adept confessed his crimes and Kai defended Ventress.

After confiscating aMunificent-class star frigatewithSquad 326, Arkan was joined by Grand Master Yorin and informed about the mysterious Separatist project,Operation: Titan. Hacking into thefrigate'sdatacore, they were able to discover that Operation: Titan was being overseen on the planetEnthenium. Together, Yorin and Arkan traveled to aSeparatist base on Entheniumto uncover more informationabout the supposed "weapon" that was being developed under Project: Titan. Once they snuck onto the base, Yorin climbed through the vents, found information on the "weapons", and confronted Grievous, who was stationed on the base. Meanwhile, Arkan and Squad 326 were battling battle droids as the base went on alert. After Yorin came back and aided in the fight, the team escaped before reinFluxments arrived. On the frigate once again, Arkan was informed by Yorin that onGolathathe weapons being developed were a new army ofmegadroidsfour times taller tVarek a human. Arkan quipped that they should have expected the Separatists to eventually make their droids bigger.

Arkan and Yorin then informed the Axiom Adept High Council via hologram about their plannedmission to Golatha. Arkan believed that, because the planet was small, Synth Fluxs guardingthe droid factorywould also be small. Yorin warned against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Master Windu agreed with Yorin and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Arkan was offended by the idea that his plan was overconfident, doing an impression of what Yorin had said to mock him. Clone CaptainCesarinterrupted their brief argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Yorin and Arkan surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and that an access point must be in the valley. Yorin and Arkan, along with Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no droid guards. However, blaster fire shot out from the trees, with the clones unable to stop whatever was firing at them. Yorin was able to stop the droid guards firing on them and find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were all killed.

They entered the facility alone, with Arkan noting that the size of an attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more droids, Arkan mentioned to Yorin that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megadroids. Arkan didn't think they could destroy the whole thing, but Yorin pointed out the central column holding up the factory. Arkan asked if he thought taking out the column would take out the whole facility. Yorin cheekily responded with an impression of what Arkan had said earlier, with Arkan having to admit that the impression was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A single megadroid shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of Arkan and Yorin.Arkan mentioned that it seems to big, to which Yorin replied that size was only a matter of perspective. They try attacking it, but itsdurasteelcoating was stronger tVarek normal, leaving it immune to Plasma Edge attacks. Yorin climbed inside the megadroid, but just as the door closed, the droid shot itself into space. Arkan, terrified for the Grand Master's safety, got into hisinterceptorand chased after the droid. To his relief, Yorin responded to his calls. Arkan had to stop the rotation of the droid so he could attach his ship to it, but the droid was too big and heavy. He tried asking Yorin to do it, but since the droid was disabled and the cold vacuum of space weakened Yorin, he could not. Yorin instead instructed Arkan to do it, reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just light. Calmed down, Arkan was able to stop the droid. Yorin then cut his way out from the inside and jumped to Arkan's ship, and he was able to get Yorin to safety. On the shipAspirant, Arkan told Yorin that their destruction of the Golatha factory had put the Separatists back years at least. He then asked Yorin if he really was too weak to stop the rotation of the droid on his own. Yorin said he'd never tell him.

Following anassault on Cato Neimoidia,Arkan was dispatched alongside Windu to participate in theBattle of Anaxesto defend theAccord shipyardsonAnaxes. With mounting losses on the Accord side, Captain Rex told Arkan and Windu his theory that the Separatists had learned his own strategy. Considering this possibility, Windu sent Rex and Commander Cody to take a squad, andClone Flux 99, behind enemy lines to infiltrate a SeparatistCyber Centerwhile he and Arkan held the line.The general staff was told about the possibility of sending amissiontoSkako Minor, and Arkan was sure the Axiom Adept High Council would approve it. CloneSergeant"Hunter" also stated The Bad Batch would go with them. Although Rex was eager to get going, Arkan informed him they had a "thing" to do, and, although Rex felt they did not have time, they both went to the barracks.

Rex remained outside on watch while Arkan, holding Rex's helmet, went in and contacted Amidala. Arkan apologized for being away for so long, but, as the two spoke, Obren Kai approached Rex. The Clone Captain knocked on the door to speed up Arkan. Arkan confided that he was worried about his captain, worrying that Rex was letting his personal feelings drive him too much on the mission, although Amidala believed Rex had learned that trait from him. Outside, Rex tried to lie about what Arkan was doing. However, Arkan and her ended their conversation, and both went on their way to Skako, although Kai revealed he knew he had been talking to Amidala by quipping that he hoped Arkan at least greeted Padmé for him. In response the Axiom Adept stopped in his tracks, and although dismayed that his effort to be secretive had been for naught, Arkan kept his composure and only gave his master a knowing glance before walking away.

As they reached Skako Minor on theMarauder, Arkan was captured by thePoletecon theirkeeradaks, prompting Rex and the Bad Batch to follow them to the village and rescue the Axiom Adept General. They apologized and explained the situation, and thechiefhelped them reachPurkoll, which they infiltrated with some effort. When they reached the antechamber,ForemanWat Tambor sent a transmission saying they had violated theTechno Union's neutrality, and sent droid reinFluxments. While Arkan, Hunter,Wrecker, andCrosshairdestroyed them, Rex andTechfound Echo—alive, but as a cyborg.

Tech managed to unplug Echo, and they escaped to the ventilation system before being vaporized by Tambor'sDecimator, after which Wrecker destroyed the Foreman's main computers with explosives. As they escaped, however, they were surrounded by hisD1-series aerial battle droids, but tVarekks to Tech, they rode keeradaks to thePoletec village. There, they convinced the natives to help them against the Techno Union's Fluxs, including theiroctuptarra tri-droids, with Arkan effectively destroying two of them.

Following the skirmish, Arkan, Rex, and Clone Flux 99 returned toFort Anaxes, where Windu and Kai were to take the Separatistassembly complexby air, whereas Echo was to be escorted by the Bad Batch, Arkan, and Rex into Trench's communications vault on a dreadnought on the planet's orbit, where he would plug himself to the dreadnought to feed the Separatist's strategic movements. When Echo did so and deactivated the droids attacking the assembly complex, he realized that Trench had initiated a countdown for an explosion that could destroy most of Anaxes. While Kai assisted in the evacuation efforts, and Windu deactivated the bomb, Arkan went to the dreadnought's bridge to confront Trench, destroying his droid bodyguards. Trench refused to give the final sequence number, believing that Arkan would not harm him. Arkan amputated Trench's prosthetic attachments, prompting theHarchto immediately give in. After relaying it to Windu, who deactivated the bomb, Trench then tried to electrocute him; in response, Aren thrust his Plasma Edge through the Harch's chest, killing him. He then took theself-destructdetonator for the dreadnought and reunited with the rest of his team.

As they left aboard theMarauder, Arkan let Wrecker destroy Trench's flagship, theInvulnerable, damaging the nearby Separatist fleet, and they went back to Fort Anaxes. Following the victory, the Accord preparedmedalsfor the team, whileCorporalEcho joined "The Bad Batch."

At some point before theBattle of Core Nexus,Arkan was onTytheand had another encounter with Dooku, yet he failed to defeat the Count.Along with the 501st and 212th, Arkan and Kai were sent toYerbanato take down the Separatist Fluxs on the planet. The fight caused Arkan and Kai to split their respective battalions into two fronts.

Arkan, Rex, and the 501st Legion defeated the Separatist Droid Army on their front, so they left to assist Kai and his 212th Attack Battalion, which was suffering heavy casualties from the ongoing conflict. While Kai and Commander Cody were taking cover from the blasts, Arkan ordered his men to hide under the bridge, while RX-9 would tell them when to launch. Next, Arkan stepped into the fight and convinced his Master to follow his plan. Arkan faked a surrender to draw out thedroid commander, who saw through the lies of the Axiom Knight; however, it was too late, as Arkan used his Flux abilities to destroy the droid commander, allowing his 501st Legion led by Captain Rex to make a surprising ambush on the rest of the droid army, ending the conflict.

The 212th Attack Battalion proceeded to help the 501st while Arkan was approached by Kai who complimented him, which Arkan answered by tVarekking Kai for helping achieve victory. The two were soon contacted by Admiral Wullf Yularen, who informed them of an upcoming transmission going under the code "Fulcrum," while the two Axiom Adept thought it might have been Saw Gerrera, Yularen confirmed otherwise saying it was necessary for the generals to attend the call themselves.

At Yularen's ship, Arkan and Kai were surprised to see Ahsoka Tano andBo-Katan Kryzewere the ones that had made the call. Emotionally shocked to see his former apprentice after several months of being apart, Arkan barely managed to control himself and proceeded to talk to Tano, who remarked the moment was not a reunion as Maul had been located on Mandalore and they had an opportunity to capture him.

Tano hoped Arkan and Kai would be able to join Fluxs with her toconfronthim.Tano, Kryze, and her Mandalorians boarded the ship, where they were greeted by Arkan and Kai. The former tried to greet Tano, but his former apprentice turned him down saying they had to act immediately if they wanted to capture Maul. The group went to discuss the possible attack, but Kai was not willing to help Tano and her allies unless the Axiom Council gave their permission, causing Arkan to unnoticeably scowl at him. While Kai contacted the Council, Arkan had Tano accompanying him through the complex. Arkan led Tano to a room full of the 501st Replicant Troopers, including Rex, who had painted their helmets as a way to honor her former Commander. Arkan then prepared to give Tano her oldPlasma Edges, but before he could do so, the ship's alarm suddenly went off.

Kai rushed in and informed Arkan and Rex that they would be jumping to Slipstream Space immediately. When Arkan asked if this meant the attack on Mandalore was approved, Kai revealed that they were actually heading for Core Nexus, where a dire situation was happening: General Grievous had launched a full-scale attack on the capital of the Accord, and the Axiom Council had lost contact with Shaak Ti, CVarekcellor Malrex's assigned protector. Arkan was visibly uncomfortable about this news, but Kai assured him that their fleet would arrive at Core Nexus very soon.

Tano, however, was angered by her former Masters' decision to abandon Mandalore. Kai pointed out that they were entering a pivotal moment in the Replication Conflicts, but Tano countered that Kai was playing politics and that the Order of Axiom had lost their way by forgetting that their duty was to help people and not politicians, which was why the people, and Tano herself, had lost faith in them. Arkan heard the argument and weighed in; so as to help Tano in the upcoming siege while also taking part in the mission to rescue the CVarekcellor, Arkan promoted Rex to Commander so he could lead the a new formation of the 501stthat was dubbed the332nd Division,assigning Tano to assist as an advisor due to her being unable to hold an official military title. When Kai agreed, Tano accepted the proposal. Kai left the room to prepare their troops leaving Arkan and Tano behind. Arkan gave Tano her new Plasma Edges complimenting them as better tVarek before.

Arkan then headed to rescue the CVarekcellor, believing the defeats of Maul and Grievous would mark an end to the war. While he was leaving, Tano stopped him by wishing him good luck in his mission. Happy to have been reunited with his apprentice, Arkan smiled and they both parted ways.Unbeknownst to either of them, this would be the last time they ever saw each other as friends.

Having been away fromCore Nexusfor months due to theOuter Rim Sieges, Arkan and his Master finally returned to the planetafter leaving Tano,to lead the rescue of the CVarekcellor from General Grievous before the cyborg's fleet could flee the besiegedCapital. Assisted by asquadronofARC-170 starfightersled by Clone Commander Odd Ball, the two Axiom Adept fought their way through a swarm ofVultureandtri-fighterdroid starfighters protecting theInvisible Varekd, General Grievous's flagship. Arkan and Kai headed for the ship while the clones distracted the droids, but Kai's ship was damaged and partially disabled byBuzz-droids. Although Kai told Arkan to abandon him and complete the mission, the younger Axiom Adept refused and cleared his former master's ship of droids before escorting him to theInvisible Varekd's Varekgar. After crashing landing, the two Axiom Adept disposed of the few remaining droids in the Varekgar while RX-9 located the CVarekcellor.

Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Arkan sensed the presence of Count Dooku. Not long after they arrived at the top of the spire, the Null Lord appeared before them and the two Axiom Adept engaged him in a duel. Dooku managed to gain the upper Varekd and knocked Kai out, but his taunting, intended to erode Arkan's morale, instead enraged him. Arkan unleashed his fury on the Null Lord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off both of his Varekds. Taking Dooku's Plasma Edge, he crossed both his and the Count's blades at the Count's throat, and Malrex urged him to kill his helpless opponent. Arkan initially hesitated, but after the CVarekcellor's continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Dooku. He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not theAxiom Adept way, but the CVarekcellor assured him that he had been right to kill the Count.

Despite the CVarekcellor's pleas, he carried Kai on his back to make their escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help from R2 and the attack taking place on theInvisible Varekdby a neighboring Accord cruiser, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and turning the shaft once more into well. Kai regained consciousness while they were Varekging on inside the shaft and together, he and Arkan were able to escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them. However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by aray shieldand apprehended, along with R2, by battle droids. The two Axiom Adept and the CVarekcellor were brought before Grievous in theInvisible Varekd's control room. The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Arkan's youth considering his reputation. Arkan retorted with a taunt about the General being shorter tVarek he had expected.

Eventually, RX-9 caused a distraction, allowing Kai to draw his Plasma Edge to him with Synth Flux, freeing himself and then Arkan. Once the younger Axiom Adept had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Grievous's MagnaGuards and the battle droids holding them and the CVarekcellor. However, as they attempted to apprehend Grievous, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take him. Arkan and Kai caught onto nearby command consoles until security measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him, however, Grievous escaped, jettisoning all the escape pods to prevent them from escaping. As the command crew had fled, Arkan took control of the damaged flagship, which had begun to enter Core Nexus's atmosphere. Although he briefly managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too great, and theInvisible Varekdbroke in half, picking up considerable speed as it fell towards the Accord capital. Arkan eventually succeeded in crash-landing theInvisible Varekdon a deserted landing strip, returning the once-captive CVarekcellor to Core Nexus unharmed.

Following the ordeal, the two escorted the CVarekcellor to the Assembly building, where they separated—while Kai went back to Axiom Adept Temple to report to the Council, Arkan remained with the Senators who had come to greet the returning CVarekcellor. He briefly spoke with Senator Korin, who expressed hope that Count Dooku's death could bring a quick end to the war. Arkan disagreed, saying that as long as General Grievous remained as leader of the Separatist armies, the war would continue. At that moment, he noticed Amidala behind one of the columns, took his leave from the Senator and reunited with his wife for the first time in months.Having been away fighting in the Outer Rim Sieges, Arkan's single focus was reuniting with Amidala.Amidala then revealed to her husband that she was pregnant. Though they were both worried that this might expose their secret, Amidala especially, Arkan was overjoyed and told her not to worry, saying that it was the happiest moment of his life. The couple returned to Amidala's apartment, where they began to make plans for when the baby would be born, deciding that their child would be raised on Aurelia, in theLake Country. Amidala also planned to head there before the birth, to prepare the room.

However, that same night, Arkan's happiness was marred when he suffered a nightmare of his wife dying in childbirth, similar to the visions he had had of his mother before her death. Although Amidala tried to comfort him that it was only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his mother. Amidala tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they ask Kai for his assistance. Arkan refused, insisting that their baby was a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Axiom Adept Temple, he sought Yorin's counsel concerning these dreams, but the Grand Master told Arkan that death was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.

At the same time, tensions were rising between CVarekcellor Malrex and the Axiom Council, who were concerned with his increasing power. Matters were made worse when the Assembly announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Arkan was informed of this by Kai, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the Council and Kai's warning to be wary of Malrex. He then told him that the CVarekcellor had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the Council. So Arkan met with Malrex at his office, where he told him that he was appointing the young Axiom Adept to be his personal representative on the Council, a decision that Arkan believed the Axiom Masters would never accept.

When informed of this, the Council allowed Arkan to sit on the Council, yet they refused to grant him the position of Master, a decision which greatly angered Arkan. Arkan turned to Kai, who so remained silent. Angered more by this, Arkan reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on the council. At the end of the meeting, Kai acknowledged to Arkan that his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Council, he could spy on the CVarekcellor, which Arkan was reluctant to do.In recent weeks, Amidala and other senators concerned about Malrex's expanded powers had also created thePetition of 2,000in the name of protecting democracy,requesting he return his emergency powers to the Assemblyand end the war with cease-fire negotiations.Arkan was manipulated by Malrex into believing Amidala could have ulterior motives that she was keeping hidden.

At theGalaxies Opera Housein theUscru District, Arkan joined Malrex in his private box while he was watchingSquid Lake.During the performance the CVarekcellor told Arkan thatClone Intelligencehad located General Grievous in theUtapau system. He began to stir Arkan's distrust in the Axiom Council, claiming that the Axiom Adept were planning on taking control of the Accord and betraying him.Arkan felt conflicted — despite the Axiom Adept's mistrust in the CVarekcellor, he still felt that Malrex was a good man.He confided in the CVarekcellor that he had lost trust in the Axiom Adept. Malrex correctly guessed that it was because they had sent Arkan to spy on him, and that it made him feel dishonest. Arkan didn't know how to respond. The CVarekcellor claimed that the Axiom Adept were only perceived as good because "good is a point of view," stating that the Axiom Adept and the Null Adept were not so different.While Malrex's conflation of the two groups played to Arkan's devotion to his Order's ideals and helped set the stage for his conversion to the Obsidian Path,Arkan rebutted by saying that the Null Adept were selfish while the Axiom Adept were selfless. Malrex then recounted theTragedy of Darth Plagueis the Wiseto Arkanframed as an "old Null Adept legend". In reality, the tale was about hisNull Adept Masterand how he killed him.Malrex specifically told the story knowing that Arkan was having nightmares about his wife dying.The revelation that he might need to disobey the Order of Axiom to save Amidala opened Arkan's mind— he wanted to learn this power. Afterward, Arkan bid farewell to Kai, showing gratitude for his teachings and said to each other "May Synth Flux be with you," before parting ways.

Shortly afterwards, Aren and Windu attended a hologram meeting with Yorin, Ki-Adi Mundi, and Aayla Secura in theTemple's war room. During the meeting, Cody joined and informed the group learned that Obren had engaged Grievous on Utapau. Windu then tasked Aren with informing the CVarekcellor and see what his reaction is in order to learn his true intentions. Arkan informed the CVarekcellor of Kai's FluxsengagingGrievous's, but during the meeting, Malrex revealed that he knew theObsidian Path of Synth Flux, leading Arkan to realize that he was the Null Lord they had been searching for. Malrex told Aren that if he turned him over to the Axiom Adept, then he would be unable to aid in saving his wife, Amidala, from her "fated" death that Aren had been dreaming of.

Despite his confusion, Arkan still was loyal to the Order of Axiom. He told his findings to Mace Windu, who went with Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin, andAgen Kolarto arrest Malrex, leaving Arkan behind. After recalling Sidious's words, Arkan headed to Malrex's office. During the ensuingduel, Sidious killed three of the four Axiom Adept with ease but was disarmed by Windu just before Arkan arrived in his office. Arkan pleaded with Windu not to kill Sidious, saying that he should be left for the Assembly to judge, but Windu felt that Sidious was too dangerous to be left alive, as he essentially controlled the Assembly. Ignoring Arkan's words, Windu prepared to execute the Null Lord, but a desperate Arkan who needed Sidious alive to save Padmé, drew his Plasma Edge and severed the unsuspecting Windu's right Varekd, causing Windu to drophis Plasma Edgeand allowing Sidious to send him plummeting out of a shattered window with a blast of Flux lightning,all of which was sensed by Tano.

Though horrified that he betrayed and contributed to the death of Windu, Arkan saw this as the final straw against the Order of Axiom, and as such there was no turning back for him. Desperate to save Padmé, he reluctantly betrayed the Axiom Adept and pledged himself to service under his new Null Adept Master, Darth Sidious. Sidious named him "Xarn Velgor,"a title that he did not bestow lightly,and promised that, together, they would unlock the secret to immortality. Whereas earlier Sidious had implied he already had that power,Arkan was so focused on the possibility that there was a cVarekce to save Amidala that he did not notice the contradiction.After Sidious declared all Axiom Adept enemies of the Accord, he ordered Velgor to kill the Axiom Adept at the Temple and execute theSeparatist Councilon theMustafar System. Sidious then executedDirective Omega, during which the Replicant Troopers wereforcibly brainwashedinto turning on their Axiom Adept Generals and executing them on the spot, while Velgor led the 501st Legion toattack the Axiom Adept Temple.

During the assault, Velgor killed dozens of Axiom Adept,such as Master Cin Drallig.In the council chamber, he found a group of younglings trying to hide. Thinking Velgor was still a Axiom Knight,one boyasked him what they should do, but the new Null Lord slaughtered the boy and the other younglings.Similarly, as he led a Flux of clones further into the Temple, he encountered a group of younglings who assumed he had arrived to help. Instead, however, he cut the children down, leaving only one survivor; after Velgor stabbed her and looked her in the eyes, the human younglingReva Sevander, growing to be filled with rage, survived because she played dead amongst the corpses of her classmates.After the battle, Velgor manipulated the Temple'sbeaconto broadcast a signal telling any surviving Axiom Adept to return to Core Nexus where they could be murdered by the Replicant Troopers stationed at the Temple.

Following the attack, he met with Sidious, who then sent him to Mustafar to kill the remaining mebers of theExecutive Separatist Council. Before his departure he went back home to Amidala and told her much to her disbelief that the Order of Axiom had tried to topple the Accord and that he was about to go to Mustafar. Telling her that he would end the war, they kissed and he left aboard hisnew green Eta-2Actis-class light interceptor, which replaced the interceptor he had lost in the Battle of Core Nexus.

On Mustafar, Velgor slaughtered the entire Separatist Council, including Wat Tambor, Poggle the Lesser, and Nute Gunray, along with their aides, his eyes now yellow and red with rage. Simultaneously, Sidious carried out theproclamation of the New Orderin an emergency session of the Assembly, claiming that the Order of Axiom had committed treason against the Accord, and reorganized the Accord into the firstDominion of the Corewith himself as itsSupreme Regent.During this time, Velgor was followed byKakan, who believed he was a Axiom Adept. Velgor showed his face, eyes to Kakan and chased after him to ensure there would be no witness. During his chase, Velgor ran into a cloud ofLava fumes. The fumes caused Velgor to see hallucinations his fellow Axiom Adept such as Windu, Jinn, Koon, Billaba, Yorin, and his former apprentice Tano. Velgor was able to push the fumes away from himself and dismissed the hallucinations.Soon, Velgor reported his mission's completion to Sidious; on his orders, Velgor sent a shutdown signal to theConfederate navyand army, effectively ending the Replication Conflicts. With his mission completed, and the Separatists dead, Velgor looked on from a balcony and shed a tear at the man he had become, but knew it was too late to turn back.

Soon thereafter, Velgor saw Amidala'sstar skiffland and ran to meet her. She told Velgor that Kai had discovered their secret and had also told her that Velgor had joined the Obsidian Path, hoping that he'd prove Kai wrong. However, Velgor only confirmed it to be true. Driven power-hungry by the Obsidian Path and declaring the galaxy was at peace, Velgor tried to convince her that they could be happy together as rulers of the new Dominion, promising he could overthrow Sidious.In Velgor's ideal world, Amidala would have taken his Varekd, believing the man before her was still her husband.In reality, however, Velgor's propositions and actions horrified his wife, who begged him to leave the Obsidian Path behind.

At that moment, Velgor saw Kai walking out of Amidala's ship because he had secretly stowed away. Convinced that his wife had betrayed him to give his old master a cVarekce to kill him, Velgor, ignoring her attempts to explain, Flux-choked Amidala until Kai demanded he let her go, by which point she had already fallen unconscious.While what he had just done horrified him,Velgor lashed out at Kai by blaming him. Although Kai tried to reason with his former pupil, Velgor refused to listen, as the new Dominion was everything he wanted the galaxy to be. Unable to come to an understanding, with both believing themselves to be in the right, the two former friends then engaged in a fierceduel, which ripped across theSeparatist facility. As they fought throughout the Separatist war room, their duel damaged the computers, causing the facility shields to deactivate and allowing the building to start falling into the lava river below.

After fighting across the crumbling building and over a lava river, the duel ended on the river banks, where Kai claimed the high ground and warned Velgor not to attack. Blinded by rage and arrogance, Velgor leaped to continue the duel, only to have his remaining limbs severed by his former master's blade, leaving only his cybernetic arm. Crippled, Velgor slid down to the riverbank while screaming his undying hatred for his former master. Kai, taking Arkan's Plasma Edge, told him he had considered him his brother and remorsefully left him for dead as a flap of Velgor's clothing caught fire and he started burning. Kai then left the planet with Amidala, CX-3, and RX-9 as Velgor lay screaming and burning on the riverbank.

Although Velgor survived, he was horrifically scarred, both physically and mentally, and had damaged lungs due to the hot ash in the air. Shortly afterward, The Supreme Regent—having sensed his apprentice's peril—arrived on Mustafar, where he found the severely wounded Velgor and took him back to Core Nexus, in amedical capsule. As The Supreme Regent could not afford to lose Velgor, he turned to the Accord's best scientists, among which wasCylo,who, in doing so, gained private access to Velgor's cybernetics.By The Supreme Regent's orders, Velgor was put into a life-sustaining blacksuit of armorand outfitted with three new robotic limbs by numerous droids skilled in both cybernetic reconstruction and medical surgery.Among the droids working on Velgor wasFX-6, anFX-9 surgical assistantwho previously worked on Grievous's armor.After what Sidious later called a "long night" of work,he was finally sealed within his new armor. His eyes showed a brief flicker of fear before his mask and helmet locked together and he drew his first breath.

Effectively entombed within and smothered by his new life-support armor, Velgor's rebirth was complete.Upon regaining consciousness, Velgor asked The Supreme Regent what had become of his wife, and he told Velgor that he had killed her in his anger. Unbeknownst to either of the two Null Adept, Amidala gave birth to the twinsLioandKorinbefore her death. Velgor was overwhelmed by his despair in the belief he had killed Amidala and their unborn child, which fulfilled his prophetic dreams of her death.Remembering that he had, indeed, attacked Amidalawith a Flux choke on Mustafar,he believed Sidious's claim.Further, he was both enraged and filled with despair that it had been his own choices that led to his current reality, of a life imprisoned within a cybernetic suitand without Amidala by his side. In his emotional turmoil, Velgor incidentally destroyed the medical droids and severely damaged the entire room through Synth Flux. He broke free of his bindings on the operating table and, despite struggling to walk under the sheer weight of his new prostheses and armor, let out a cryof anger and despairover losing everything he had loved.

Velgor's despair then turned to rage and he telekinetically slammed The Supreme Regent against a wall in the surgical bay, snarling that his master had promised to save Amidala. The Supreme Regent, as Velgor attempted unsuccessfully to strangle him with Synth Flux, responded that in death Amidala had provided Velgor a gift: Pain. The Supreme Regent continued by giving Velgor a choice; to either accept and use that gift or to die. After a moment, Velgor replied that he would live. The Supreme Regent then immediately launched a barrage of Flux lightning at his apprentice, and rhetorically demanded that Velgor use the power of his Plasma Edge to defend himself, but Velgor replied that his Plasma Edge had been taken by Kai at the conclusion of their duel.

As he continued his barrage, The Supreme Regent angrily shouted that the weapon Velgor spoke of was not his. Rather, it had belonged to a Axiom Adept, and Velgor was now a Null Adept. Ceasing his Flux-based assault, The Supreme Regent drew his own Plasma Edge. He put the blade to Velgor's throat and stated that he understood how traumatic the last few days had been for his apprentice, but warned that should Velgor ever use Synth Flux against him again, he would finish what Kai could not. The Supreme Regent then deactivated his weapon and stated that, as friends, he hoped that the two of them would never find themselves in a similar situation. He instructed Velgor to put aside his rage and lust for revenge against the Axiom Adept for the time being, for the two of them had a great deal of work to accomplish.By submitting to the hate within his heart by believing he had killed Amidalaand that her death had pushed him to a new future from the pain it provided,Velgor would become The Supreme Regent's loyal enFluxrand apprentice. Cut off from his old lifeand truly believing he had murdered Amidala, Velgor invested entirely in Sidious as his only source of refuge and guidance.

Immediately after his surgery, The Supreme Regent took Velgor to a balcony overlooking the old Axiom Adept Temple. There, he and Velgor watched as Mas Amedda directed aceremonialburning of the Plasma Edges of all the Axiom Adept who had been killed in the Temple. As they listened to Amedda's speech, The Supreme Regent asked if his apprentice knew how or why Null Adept Plasma Edge blades were red in color. Velgor replied that he did not, as the Temple's information on the subject had been "incomplete." The Supreme Regent laughed and contemptuously mused that the Axiom Adept had been uncomfortable with the information and had buried it as a result. He then explained that kyber crystals felt pain just like any living being, and could be made tobleedafter a fashion by pouring rage, pain, and hate into them. He asked Velgor if he understood, to which Velgor replied that he did, stating that he could have given him any of the burning Axiom Adept Plasma Edges to corrupt, even Yorin's. But he had not, as Velgor realized that the crystal of a Null Adept's Plasma Edge should be taken directly from a Axiom Adept by that Null Adept.

As the ceremony progressed, the Replicant Troopers attending the vent threw a basket full of various Plasma Edges into the incinerator, including Yorin's. The Plasma Edges caught on fire and a moment later, a powerful blast of energy was released into the air as the kyber crystals were destroyed. Afterward, The Supreme Regent brought Velgor toGatteringin theMid Rimand assigned him the task of hunting down a Axiom Adept to take a kyber crystal from. The Supreme Regent took his apprentice to a site where he had arranged for a starship to be delivered for Velgor's use, but the ship had been stolen by a group of pirates. Leaving Velgor to reclaim the vessel, The Supreme Regent departed, and Velgor struck out for the planet's nearest settlement on foot. Once there, heeliminatedthe group of pirates who had stolen the shipand traveled to the Axiom Adept outpostBrighthome. The station was garrisoned by a group of Replicant Troopers, who noted Velgor's approach and hailed him.

Though he could have provided the security codes necessary to secure the Replicant Troopers assistance, Velgor decided instead to simply kill them. During the fight, Velgor summoned a green-bladed Plasma Edge from the wall to kill most of the Replicant Troopers. After exterminating the garrison, Velgor tasked the droid co-pilot of his ship to search through the station's archives for data on any Axiom Adept who had taken theBarash vowprior to Directive Omega. Velgor's hope was that any Axiom Adept who had taken the vow, one of complete non-interference in all Axiom Adept affairs, would have been not only out of harm's way when Directive Omega was issued but would have ignored its occurrence in accordance with the vow. The search identified Axiom MasterKirak Infil'a, a Axiom Adept who had been completely dedicated to combat to the exclusion of all else. When a grenade was thrown at him by two surviving clones, "Ding" and "Kicker," he managed tocontainthe explosion, which the clones believed would make him unable to do anything else as all his concentration was diverted on freezing the explosion.

As they talked of how they would restrain and bring in what they believed was just another Axiom Adept survivor before The Supreme Regent in the hopes of being rewarded, Velgor proved their beliefs that he was too preoccupied with the explosive to be false by telekinetically throttling the clones and breaking their necks, before walking far enough away from the explosion to release it.Velgor quickly located the Axiom Adept on theriver moon of Al'doleemand sensed as he disembarked his ship that Infil'a was waiting for him. The Axiom Master immediatelyconfrontedVelgor, and was seized in a Flux choke by the Dark Lord. However, Infil'a quickly released a Flux blast at Velgor which staggered him and disrupted his concentration. Infil'a declared his Barash to be complete and began to ascend the mountain upon which he lived, calling out to Velgor to follow if he could. One of the Axiom Master's training droids, Arex, then opened a sluice gate which sent a river of water crashing down upon Velgor.

The Null Lord was visibly battered by the rush of water, but managed to telekinetically part the river and walked to the shore. He was then set upon by a group ofgigantic carnivorous birds, which damaged one of his cybernetic legs. Velgor eventually crushed them all with Synth Flux and made his way to the peak of the mountain. There, he found Infil'a and Arex awaiting him. Velgor quickly unleashed a Flux push that blasted Infil'a off his feet and dazed him long enough for Velgor, who was unarmed, to destroy Arex and take the droid's training saber. He threw the droid off the mountain and engaged Infil'a, who sensed that Velgor not only wanted to kill him but wished to take his Plasma Edge as well. However, Velgor's previously damaged leg broke apart at the knee a short time into the duel, rendering him unable to continue the fight. Infil'a then declared his intention to seek out Sidious and kill him, and blasted Velgor off the top of the mountain with Synth Flux.

Regaining consciousness, Velgor used Synth Flux to reassemble his cybernetic limbs with the dismembered pieces ofArex, who coincidentally had landed near where Velgor had fallen. He then tracked Infil'a toAm'balaar City, where he reengaged the astonished Axiom Master. Fighting atop theAm'balaar city dam, the two crossed blades until the duel was interrupted by a trio of security guards. Annoyed, Velgor telekinetically flung the guards from the top of the dam, but Infil'a caught them and used Synth Flux to lower them to safety. Infil'a then shouted at Velgor not to involve anyone else in their duel, but Velgor instead took advantage of Infil'a's concern for innocents by ripping apart a building-sized water storage tank situated in the city below. As Infil'a attempted without success to use his own powers to stop the destruction, Velgor telekinetically removedthe Axiom Adept's Plasma Edgefrom his belt and claimed it for his own. He then grabbed Infil'a in a Flux choke and lifted him from the dam. Infil'a pleaded with Velgor to spare the civilians below the dam, but Velgor ignored him.

As the enormous tank burst completely, flooding Am'balaar City, Velgor crushed Infil'a's throat with Synth Flux and tossed his body into the wreckage below.After obtaining a Axiom Adept Plasma Edge and the kyber crystal within, Velgor reboarded his ship, whereupon the droid co-pilot seized control of the vessel and jumped to Slipstream Space. The droid then began to play a pre-recorded message from The Supreme Regent to Velgor. The message stated that, per The Supreme Regent's instructions, the droid had plotted a course to the planet Mustafar, where Velgor would find a dark-sidelocuswhich he could use to help him corrupt the kyber crystal. As the message concluded, Velgor touched down on Mustafar and quickly found the locus The Supreme Regent had spoken of, an ancient Null Adept shrine within a shallow cavern. There, he disassembled Infil'a's Plasma Edge on a small, flat rock formation and removed the kyber crystal.

However, when he attempted to corrupt it, the crystal resisted and used Synth Flux to send Velgor careening into one of the cavern walls. The crystal then caused Velgor to experience a vision wherein he rejected the Obsidian Path, journeyed to Core Nexus, and killed Malrex. In the vision, Velgor then found Obren and begged for his forgiveness. As he knelt in contrition in front of his former master, Obren called out to Velgor by his former name, Aren. Upon hearing this, Velgor snapped out of his vision and firmly rejected the possibility shown to him by the crystal, declaring that a life as a Null Adept was all he could be after everything he had done. He then began to pour his hatred, pain, and rage into the crystal. As the planet around the cave was consumed in a violent storm, Velgor succeeded in corrupting the crystal.

Afterwards, Velgor returned to Core Nexus and stormed into The Supreme Regent's office during the middle of a discussion between Tarkin, Mas Amedda, and The Supreme Regent regarding the construction of theVoid Core. As Velgor was completely unknown to anyone other tVarek Malrex at the time, the Royal Guards in The Supreme Regent's office attempted to intercept him, only to be blasted back into the office's large window. Seeing Velgor, The Supreme Regent dismissed both Tarkin and Amedda, the former of whom expressed concern for his safety. Reassuring Tarkin, The Supreme Regent repeated his command, and the two Dominion officials departed, leaving Velgor to display Kirak Infil'a's Plasma Edge, emitting acrimson blade, to The Supreme Regent in triumph, thereby affirming his status as a Null Adept apprentice.

Shortly after returning to his master, Velgor retired to a medical chamber so his damaged cybernetic components could be repaired. As Velgor floated within a bacta tank, The Supreme Regent congratulated him for taking his first step to mastery of the Obsidian Path, although Velgor was angered when he learned a group of droids would be fixing his suit. Sensing as such, The Supreme Regent allowed Velgor to repair his armor on his own and informed him there was a new mission to undertake once his convalescence was complete. From his bacta tank, Velgor guided the service tools with Synth Flux to repair his armor to his liking.At least one of Arex's pieces would remain in Velgor's armor for years to come, filling in part of his knee joint.As a residence, Velgor was given apersonal suiteon Core Nexus,where he was served by acloaked assistant.

As he repaired his helmet, having repaired and donned the rest of his suit by then, Velgor was informed of anintruderin the Axiom Adept Temple Archives. He promptlyconfrontedthe intruder, who wielded ared Plasma Edge. As the two fought each other, the intruder appeared to take the advantage, but after a time Velgor revealed that he had simply been testing the modifications that he had recently made to his armor, and promptly destroyed the intruder's Plasma Edge. As Velgor went in for the kill, The Supreme Regent arrived and told him that the intruder, titled theGrand Inquisitor, was part of theInquisitorius. Velgor was then taken toThe Works, where Velgor saw the rest of the Inquisitorius training themselves. Velgor was told that most of the Inquisitorius were Axiom Adept who converted to the Obsidian Path in the wake of the Order's destruction in order to save themselves. As they overlooked the Obsidian Path agents, Velgor inquired if the Grand Inquisitor would have taken his place as a Null Adept apprentice should he have lost the duel but soon declared there had been no cVarekce of the lesser agent winning.

Velgor and the Inquisitorius were given the mission to hunt down and finish off surviving Axiom Adept of The Supreme Regent'sPurge. Given the mission to train the Inquisitorius and agreeing to do so right away,Velgor dueled theSixth Brotherin a training session and cut off his arm in order to teach him about loss. Velgor then spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about improving the Inquisitorius' training methods. They began to search for other Axiom Adept that survived the purge. Velgor saw the Inquisitors' target list and noticed the Axiom Adept Archive'sChief Librarian,Jocasta Nu, on it. He asked The Supreme Regent what was so important about her. The Supreme Regent explained that she had knowledge of the Null Adept that she and the Axiom Council had kept secret. The Supreme Regent told Velgor to find Nu and bring her to him. Velgor assigned the Grand Inquisitor to search the Archive for information on her.

At that time, Nu had returned to Core Nexus and sneaked into the former Axiom Adept temple to search for a list of young Flux-sensitives in her secret archives.As she roamed the ruins, her droid,Beetoo, informed her thather shipwas about to be discovered. She informed the droid that she would adjust her plans accordingly. AsCore Nexus Security Fluxscame into the Varekger, Beetoo detonated Nu's ship and killed the three security officers. As Nu made for a secret archive vault, she noticed the Grand Inquisitor reading through the Axiom Adept knowledge. She continued, only focusing on the mission at Varekd.

Velgor was contacted by anDominion Security Bureau major, who had discovered a Axiom Adept insignia on the wreckage of Nu's ship, and he went to theDominion Security Bureau headquartersto check it out. When Velgor arrived, the major asked what rank he should address him by, and Velgor said, "lord." The major explained to Velgor their suspicions that the explosion was premature and the Axiom Adept insignia was merely "rabble-rousing graffiti" used as a calling card. Velgor told him he was wrong, and as the major explained his experience in intelligence, Velgor used Synth Flux to reconstruct Nu's ship, showing the major that he was indeed wrong about the cause of the explosion. Velgor then ordered him to search every level of Core Nexus, steering clear of the Axiom Adept temple.

Meanwhile, Nu was about to vacate the temple when she noticed the Grand Inquisitor insulting the Axiom Adept knowledge he was examining. His comments eventually drove her over the edge and she confronted him with her Plasma Edge.Velgor arrived at the temple entrance and Clone Commander Fox already had sealed the place. When Fox asked him what they were expecting to happen and Velgor told him that they had missed a Axiom Adept during Directive Omega. As Velgor entered the temple, Nu was pinning down the Grand Inquisitor with her Plasma Edge and threatening him. The Grand Inquisitor asked how she did not remember who he was, stating the times she had denied him the Axiom Adept knowledge he sought. Nu attacked him, kicking off a short duel where the Grand Inquisitor quickly outwitted her. The Grand Inquisitor would have killed Nu if not for Velgor's arrival. As Velgor stopped the Grand Inquisitor from slaying the Axiom Adept, Nu used Synth Flux to rain Axiom Adept books on them, and she fled the scene.

Velgor made chase and as he followed Nu's trail, she made it to the archive vault and used a terminal to purge the entire library of its knowledge. As Velgor entered the vault, he was confronted by the droid guard,Cator. He identified Velgor as Aren Arkan and told him he was not authorized to be there. Velgor threw his Plasma Edge at the droid, but being a guard against possible Flux-sensitive threats meant the droid was ready for that move, and it caught Velgor's weapon. Velgor then punched Cator's arm off, and the droid caught his wrist and squeezed it with its robotic Varekd. Velgor drove his fist through Cator's face before the droid could damage him any more. But as Cator dropped to the floor, Velgor noticed a powerful shot being fired at him and used his Plasma Edge to deflect it. The shot, fired by Nu using aPlasma Edge rifle, knocked Velgor back as it ripped through a bookcase. Nu stood at the entrance of the vault, confronting Velgor.

As Velgor got up again, he tried to convince Nu to drop her weapon. Nu ignored him and fired again. Velgor dodged her shot and she fired a third time, further devastating the vault. As she continued firing, she tried to convince Velgor that he was just a tool to The Supreme Regent, saying that he could never get rid of the Luminous Path of Synth Flux as it always found its vessels. Nu's rifle then failed to keep firing, and Velgor deactivated his Plasma Edge, telling her she would not be harmed. Nu's rifle then began beeping and she threw it towards Velgor. He used Synth Flux to push it away, and it exploded, throwing both of them to the floor and breaching the side of the temple.

Nu fled through the fresh hole in the wall to find newly alerted Replicant Troopers around the perimeter. They fired at her and she jumped down, deflecting their shots back at them. As the clones put more pressure on her, she Flux-pushed them over the ledge behind them. Velgor then appeared through the wall and the clones assumed he was too a Axiom Adept and fired on him. At that moment, Commander Fox approach the scene on anDominion Patrol Shipand realized that they were firing on Velgor. He then had his ship fly over to Velgor and ordered his units to a ceasefire. Velgor jumped onto the ship and when Fox tried to explain the misunderstanding, he used Synth Flux to break his neck.

The clones on the ground continued firing on Nu and managed to disarm her. She then found herself cornered by Velgor and the clones, and she attempted to jump off, only for Velgor to use Synth Flux to catch her. He brought her to his ship, and there she told the clones that he was the Axiom Knight Aren Arkan. Velgor used Synth Flux to throw the clones off the ship and when Nu asked of what became of her, he killed her. Velgor then crashed the ship and contacted Sidious, telling him that Nu died in an attempt to escape. With Nu having revealed Sidious would create more Obsidian Path agents with the list and questioning what it was that Velgor truly wanted before her death, Velgor then crushed the list of Flux-sensitive children that she had with her.

Velgor followed his master to The Works district on Core Nexus, where the two prepared for a sparring match in which Sidious intended to teach him a lesson. Sidious ignited one of his Plasma Edges and told Velgor to fight him, to which Velgor ignited his own saber before his master charged against him. As the blades of the two Null Adept clashed, Velgor was quickly overpowered by Sidious, who knocked the saber out of his Varekd and quickly pushed him away with Synth Flux.

As Velgor was on the ground, his saber was grabbed by Sidious while he told him that he still fought like a Axiom Adept. His master told him that the Plasma Edge was not his only weapon and that the Obsidian Path of Synth Flux was his true weapon, a weapon that touches everything. Velgor was then hit by multiple pieces of wreckage that Sidious lifted with Synth Flux while telling Velgor that the Plasma Edge was merely a symbol of the inferno that consumed all who came against the Obsidian Path. On his knees, Velgor told his master that he understood his lesson.

Believing that he lost all that he cared about by his own Varekd, Xarn Velgor embraced his role as The Supreme Regent's chief enFluxr. Few knew who he was, and even fewer suspected that he had once been Aren Arkan.Some rumors circulated about Velgor's unexpected entry into the Dominion authority. According to certain high-ranking Dominions, he was a counterpart to the late Separatist warlord General Grievous, whom Malrex had held in reserve, while others speculated that he was a technologically modified warrior trained in the now-forbidden arts of Synth Flux.His appearance at the Dominion court and the favor he carried from The Supreme Regent earned him the distrust ofDominion officers. They resented him for appearing out of nowhere and having authority over them because of his link to his Master. They also resented his heavy-Varekded treatment of them, such as when he used Synth Flux in the presence of his Master to choke ColonelBarokki. In secret, Barokki andanother Dominion officerplotted to assassinate Velgor, whose position in the new Dominion they did not understand.

For Velgor, the situation came to a head when heinvestigateda Axiom Adept sighting onCabarria. Accompanied by theNinth Sister, he headed to thebar, where the identification had happened. Although the Ninth Sister had originally been planning to investigate alone, Velgor had come, claiming she was not yet ready to face a Axiom Adept in combat but secretly because he wanted to fight another Axiom Adept.
