The Ninth Sister commented on his true desire, using her empathic abilities to read him clearly. As a result, Velgor ordered her to remain outside while he investigated. Once inside, the Dark Lord was attacked by afamilyof bounty hunters consisting ofBhada,Ramat,and their twelve year old daughterCVarekath Cha. Usingion grenades, they neutralized all the weapons in the bar while CVarekath tried to seize Velgor's Plasma Edge with atractor rifle. However, he used Synth Flux to seize the Plasma Edge midair and, under the strain of the two opposing Fluxs, the hilt shattered, leaving only the red kyber crystal.

The Chas told the assembled customers that they had come only for the "Axiom Adept" and that they were not planning on harming anyone. However, a disarmed Velgor disagreed and used Synth Flux to turn the customers into projectiles aimed at the Chas. Taken by the surprise, the bounty hunters turned on their shields to protect themselves. As they attacked him, Velgor also used the customers and the bar's furniture as shields. Eventually, the hunters realized they could not defeat him and made to flee. Reclaiming his crystal with Synth Flux, Velgor pursued them. Emerging from the bar, Velgor initially suspected that the Inquisitors had been behind the attempt on his life and threatened the Ninth Sister with her own Plasma Edge. The Inquisitor's denials did not sway the Dark Lord, but neither did they prove her guilt. Determined to find who had placed a bounty on his head, he pursued the Chas. They eluded him for a time until he crashed a massive vehicle into their path, causing their speeder to crash. Seizing CVarekath in a Flux choke, he commanded they tell him who had ordered his death.

Understanding they and their daughter were at risk, they told him about an anonymous bounty with an exorbitant price posted on theHunter Net. Ramat Cha, a talentedslicer, offered to uncover their employer for him in excVarekge for their daughter's life. When they demanded that he release CVarekath first, Velgor refused. However, the Chas refused to work if their daughter was killed, further declaring that they would happily die as well to exist in death with her. Grudgingly impressed, the Dark Lord demanded proof that Ramat could deliver her side of the deal, which she proved by tracking the signal of the bounty back to Core Nexus. Velgor released CVarekath as agreed, at which point the bounty hunters made their daughter swear that she would seek no vengeance against the Dark Lord, a deal Velgor chose to honor. To complete the deal, Ramat provided Velgor with the information he had requested and gave him the source of the bounty on him: theDominion Executive Buildingon Core Nexus, specifically The Supreme Regent's office.

Velgor then killed both Cha parentsright in front of their daughter, who was filled with rage. Despite her oath to not hold a grudge, she ignored her promise and spent her life hunting the Dark Lordin revenge. Eventually, as the years passed on, the Null Lord himself simply forgot the murders he carried out on Cabarria. Cha, however, never forgotand became a skilled bounty hunter,studying every report she could and speaking to anyone she could find who had witnessed Velgor in battle. She trained herself to counter Velgor's abilities, eventually enlisting a team of fellow hunters as "theOrpVareks" to help her in her quest. Cha's skills rose to a level that even Malrex took notice,becoming a bounty hunter The Supreme Regent could trustall in the name of possibly getting close to Velgor, only to never see him.In effect, Velgor had completely upended Cha's life, only for the crusade her life turned into to not impact Velgor at all.

With their business on Cabarria completed, Velgor and the Ninth Sister left the planet and returned to Core Nexus. Now suspecting that The Supreme Regent had placed the bounty on his head, the Dark Lord planned to confront his Master about his intentions. As the Ninth Sister piloted the ship through Slipstream Space, Velgor worked on creating a new Plasma Edge. Hisdroidmeanwhile investigated the origins of the kill contract placed on him and confirmed that the order could have come from The Supreme Regent's office, with no evidence of slicing to indicate foul play. But, as the ship arrived in orbit over Core Nexus, the ship's attempt to transmit clearance codes for landing was jammed and the ship itself was marked as being part of aSeparatist splinter cell. The Dominion capital's orbital defenses targeted Velgor's ship. Only the Dark Lord's skill as a pilot prevented them from being destroyed, although the ship was damaged and Fluxd to crash land on Core Nexus. Tired of the repeatedattemptson his life, the Dark Lord headed to the former Axiom Adept Temple to confront The Supreme Regent.

The Supreme Regent was investigating the hoard of artifacts that Jocasta Nu had collected and stored in the Axiom Adept Archives when Velgor arrived. The Supreme Regent sensed that his apprentice did not wish to discuss his recent discovery, although he did praise him for having found the Librarian's stash. Velgor informed his master of the events on Cabarria and his investigation into the kill order against him, before directly asking his Master whether he had placed it on his head as part of a test. Noticing Velgor's new Plasma Edge, The Supreme Regent asked to see it, approving of its design. He also chastised his apprentice for believing that he would resort to such methods as placing a bounty on his head if he wanted to have him killed and that he did not "test": he "taught." He also told Velgor that finding the assassins was his task, not theirs. This echoed a previous lesson he had taught Velgor when he had "disciplined" Colonel Barokki: that they must not kill everyone in the galaxy, but that having power meant they could, and that they would never lack for people to destroy.

Velgor returned to his quarters, where he meditated on the past events and his Master's words. Thinking back to all that had happened, he eventually realized the cause of the conspiracy against him was that his sudden elevation had caused concern and fear among the ranks of the Dominion officers, who knew that he had "strength," but not that he shared The Supreme Regent's "power." Despite a lack of proof, Velgor became convinced that Colonel Barokki, who had so suddenly borne his displeasure, must be involved in the conspiracy. The Dark Lord met with The Supreme Regent and shared his conclusions, and requested that he be publicly recognized and allowed to show them the consequences of opposing him. The Supreme Regent asked whether he was sure, and Velgor confirmed it: he would not kill them all unless he had to, but would not allow them to act against him with impunity. The Supreme Regent agreed to Velgor's plan, but forbade him from killing Tarkin, as he was essential to his plans.

The Supreme Regent gathered theelite officersof the growing Dominion Military to clarify the new hierarchy. He formally introduced Velgor as his emissary, who spoke with his voice and whose commands would need to be obeyed as if they had come from The Supreme Regent himself. The Supreme Regent then moved aside and allowed Velgor to speak: the Dark Lord summoned five officers before him:Corin Ferro,Zorta Bingan,Joon Strephi,Tomas Azoras, and Barokki. Informing them that the two attempts on his life had been made from within the Officer Corps and that he had his suspicions who had been responsible, he instituted a new rule: for every assassination attempt he survived, and he warned them that he wouldalwayssurvive, five officers chosen at random would be executed. He instantly executed the five designated officers with a Flux choke before departing, leaving the remaining officers stunned and afraid. With his position in the hierarchy established,Velgor did not need to hold an official military rank. Instead, he acted as the de factoCommander-in-Chiefof the Dominion Militaryas per his position as The Supreme Regent's right Varekd man.Velgor would also come to be recognized as part of both theJoint Chiefs of the Dominion Militaryand theDominion Ruling Council, of which he was considered a ranking member.After his demonstration before the officer corps, Velgor returned to his personal suite and mediated, cracking the window in his anger that positive thoughts still came.

In18 BBY,one year into the reign of the Dominion, Velgor was reliving his duel on Mustafar through meditation. During his experience, he was already in his cybernetic suit, and he used Synth Flux to throw lava at Obren. Obren jumped up to dodge it, but Velgor Flux-choked him and threw him into the rocks. Velgor watched Obren burn before he awoke in his meditation chamber as The Supreme Regent asked for him over the comm. In his throne room, The Supreme Regent told Velgor that a year after his ascension, it was time to show that his voice was the only voice within the Dominion. Velgor asked how, and The Supreme Regent told him that Mon Cala would make a great example of the price of defiance. He further told Velgor thatKingLee-Char may have had an adviser helping him with his negotiations with the Dominion. Velgor asked if it could be a Axiom Adept, and The Supreme Regent said it was possible. He told Velgor to take a team to investigate this and claimed the military aspects of the operation would be left to Moff Tarkin.

Velgor arrived at Mon Cala aboard theZeta-class shuttleInfernum. With him, he took three Inquisitors, the Sixth Brother, Ninth Sister, andTenth Brother, and a death squad ofPurge Troopers. At this time, the Dominion ambassadorTelvarhad just finished another meeting with Lee-Char, and the man was walking to his shuttle when he saw Velgor descend from the ramp of his shuttle. The Ninth Sister struggled to get past the king'sChief of Security, Gial Ackbar, and Velgor confronted the Mon Calamari, asking if he had read the writ that the Ninth Sister had given him. Ackbar confirmed that he had, but demanded that he knew their business as he had a job to do and so did Velgor. Velgor complied and explained about a possible enemy of the Dominion on Mon Cala. As he said that, Telvar's shuttle took off and flew for Tarkin's Void Cruiser, theSovereign—which was in orbit above Mon Cala. However, as Ackbar denied the possibility of an enemy of the Dominion being on Mon Cala, Telvar's shuttle exploded.

Telvar's assassination prompted Tarkin to enact a full-scaleinvasion of Mon Cala. His Fluxs landed atDac City, where Velgor had arrived, andlaid siegeto the Mon Calamari and Quarren defenses. Velgor and his team sprang to life and soon seized the landing pad where theInfernumwas parked. He then led them to theDac City palaceto find Lee-Char and ask him about the possible Axiom Adept. They fought their way through the palace guards and entered the palace, where the Ninth Sister began torturing Lee-Char for the location of the Axiom Adept. However, another threat loomed over them. A massive tidal wave had suddenly erupted as a part of Lee-Char's defensive plan. The Sixth Brother warned them of this, and he and the Tenth Brother began shielding Velgor and the Ninth Sister from the wave using Synth Flux. When their attempts began failing, Velgor joined them, but it was not enough. The group was washed away by the wave, and Dac City was left in ruins.

Velgor awoke at the bottom of theGreat Ungeness Trench. As his oxygen supply was dwindling, he used Synth Flux to create an air bubble for himself. Suddenly, he was attacked by asquid-like creature, and after wrestling with it, he killed it with Synth Flux. Velgor then rode its body using Synth Flux and eventually picked up a transmission from the Inquisitors. Velgor asked them to come to his position, and they did so in anDominion submarine. When they picked him up, he asked about the Axiom Adept, and the Inquisitors said that he was still alive somewhere. Velgor then asked about Lee-Char, and the Ninth Sister said that they did not know, but she then revealed that she had gotten the Axiom Adept's location from him. Knowing exactly where their target was, they began traveling for the Axiom Adept.

Velgor and his team arrived at the Axiom Adept's location to find the individual and his disciples evacuating. The Axiom Adept, anIktotchiPadawan calledFerren Barr, had been notified by Lee-Char after he was recovered following the events at Dac City. The group of them immediately scattered, butone of the disciplesinstead attacked the submarine head-on with his blaster. The shots bounced off the window, and Velgor used Synth Flux to crush his helmet. The other fled in the direction ofBel City, and as Velgor caught up with them,another discipleturned around to attack them. He too died in the process but managed to slow Velgor's submarine, and the rest of the group followed Barr to Bel City. Velgor and the Inquisitors arrived at Bel City and marched down the hallway, reinFluxd by the Inquisitors and the Purge troopers. Velgor was then contacted by Tarkin, who was struggling to deal with the grueling conflict on Mon Cala. Velgor told Tarkin that he was busy, but Tarkin knew that and asked him to do something else instead: capture Lee-Char. Velgor told Tarkin that he did not answer to him, but Tarkin replied that capturing Lee-Char was the only way to end the fighting quickly on Mon Cala, and he pledged to owe Velgor a favor if he did so. Velgor agreed and told the Inquisitors to continue the hunt as he left.

Velgor arrived at Lee-Char'sRoyal Command Bunkerand killed his guards. He then disarmed Lee-Char and contacted Tarkin, who told him to take Lee-Char to a window and send him his exact location. Velgor asked Tarkin if he was going to send a shuttle, and the man said not yet. Once Velgor did what he asked, Tarkin began a bombardment of Mon Cala. Lee-Char watched in horror but refused to surrender. The two were interrupted by Barr, who had outsmarted and scattered Velgor's team. Barr confronted Velgor and the twodueled. As they fought, Barr revealed that he had assassinated Telvar to send the Mon Calamari into war. Lee-Char overheard this and realized his mistake in following Barr. He ran to his terminal and sent a message to the Mon Calamari and Quarren Fluxs, ordering them to cease fire. Lee-Char then contacted Tarkin, notifying him of the surrender. Tarkin sent a shuttle to pick him up but continued the bombardment.

Velgor and Barr continued fighting, and Barr began laughing. Lee-Char questioned this, and Barr said that he had completed his plan. Barr prophesied that the Mon Calamari vessels would be at the forefront of a great Union Uprising decades from then, and decades after that. Velgor told Barr that he was not a Axiom Adept anymore, and Barr told him that that made two of them, saying that he still beat the Null Adept. Velgor then slew him, and the shuttle arrived to pick up Lee-Char. The king told Velgor that Mon Cala would rise again, but Velgor told him that there was no Mon Cala, only the Dominion.Some time after the mission, Velgor ordered to imprison and torture the people of Mon Cala.

Velgor soon became aware of a project that was taking up many resources. He began investigating, which led him to thedata vaultonScarif. There, he foundProject Stardust, a project that was a large battle station the size of a small moon. Velgor studied its design before he was interrupted by Tarkin, who was very displeased with Velgor's snooping. Tarkin told him he was not authorized to be there, and Velgor claimed that Synth Flux was all the authorization he needed. Velgor denied that he was Tarkin's beast but the man denied his claim and taunted Velgor, saying he did not need Synth Flux to know he was very angry. Tarkin then produced a hologram of The Supreme Regent, who told Velgor he did not recall sending him to Scarif. Velgor apologized and told The Supreme Regent he only sought to study the ways in which the Dominion would extend its rule over the galaxy. The Supreme Regent told Velgor of a new mission he was to go on. There had been certain instances of sabotage during the Void Core's construction that Velgor needed to discover the source of before it got worse. The Supreme Regent further told Velgor that Tarkin had full authority over him and that if he disappointed him, he disappointed Malrex.

Velgor arrived at Geonosis and met with CommanderOrson Callan Krennic. As they greeted, an overhead rock formation exploded and rained down on them. Velgor saved himself and Krennic and they went tohis officeto discuss the matter. Krennic claimed it was Tarkin sabotaging his efforts in order to get rid of him and take over the project. Velgor asked him if there was any proof and Krennic was not able to produce anything. Krennic then explained to him the incidences that had occurred in the last three months. Velgor gave his knowledge on the situation toloyalty officerSid Uddra, who met him at the Petranaki Arena to discuss what she suspected. She told Velgor that as much as Tarkin and Krennic's rivalry arose tension between them, it was unlikely for either to be the culprit. She then offered Velgor with another individual to look into: the scientistGalen Walton Erso. Velgor looked throughhis officeand found many kyber crystals in which hebledfor himself without issue. He then found a recording of Erso andhis family. Velgor also noticed that Erso called his daughter stardust, which was the codename for the Void Core's project. Finally, Velgor found anoothecaegg in a box. Knowing that Geonosians were a slave race and were forbidden from breeding freely, he confronted Krennic about this. Krennic denied that it was him and suggested that some of the slaves may have sent it as a distress call to Erso. Velgor then deduced for himself the saboteurs at Varekd.

Velgor and a squad of death troopers entered a Geonosian hive and found the queen and her minions. The two sides engaged but Velgor quickly came out on top, slaying the queen. After the battle, he returned to Tarkin on theCarrion Spikeand reported his findings to him. Tarkin asked Velgor how he knew the queen's hive had orchestrated the sabotages and Velgor told how their slicers had confirmed the hive to have installed corrupted tech into production, ensuring accidents. Tarkin then asked Velgor how he may have dealt with them and Velgor said that they were animals and so they were slaughtered like animals. Tarkin finally asked Velgor if there was anything else and he stated to Tarkin that his project, the Void Core, was able to destroy a planet. Tarkin asked how he knew and he told him of the kyber crystals he saw in Erso's office. Tarkin then asked Velgor why he was not happy for this revelation, and Velgor said that he distrusted his reliance on technological aberration. Tarkin then told Velgor that he was one of those before realizing that Velgor wanted to be the only technological aberration. Velgor warned Tarkin that the Void Core may become his tomb, and when Tarkin asked if that was a threat, Velgor told him it was only a prediction.

Over the next two decades of the Dominion's reign, Velgor would remain one of the few who enjoyed direct access to Malrex. He was Malrex's preferred envoy for all matters that The Supreme Regent took a direct interest in.With Malrex Fluxd to spend most of his time governing the galaxy at large,it was Velgor who was tasked with taking a direct Varekd against the Dominion's enemies,becoming known as the "Supreme Regent's Fist" to both Dominionsand, eventually, criminals.Such matters included the elimination of rebel uprisings and the continued construction of theVoid Core. One of Velgor'searliest missionson The Supreme Regent's behalf was to effect an execution onMurkVareka.He was indeed made privy to the secret construction of the Void Core, to be overseen by Wilhuff Tarkin.Velgor's high status also gave him the ability to alter Dominion records under the off-the-booksDirective 081-Omega.

One Dominion captain who served under Velgor in the immediate aftermath of the Replication Conflicts alleged to a later subordinate,Yrica Quell, that Velgor "matured" over the years: while during his first days as an Dominion enFluxr Velgor would simply murder out of annoyance, Velgor came to wait for a subordinate to commit a mistake, albeit sometimes a trivial one, to have an excuse to kill.The Dominion officers remained wary of Velgor and his power, as well as his continued devotion to the ancient ways of Synth Flux, but none rose to challenge him without feeling the consequences.

Despite the mistrust and even hatred he generated among the officers, Velgor was held in high esteem by theVoid Trooper Corps, as he often fought alongside them at the front line rather tVarek remaining in command centers. As The Supreme Regent's enFluxr, Velgor retained command of the unit he had commanded during the Replication Conflicts as Aren Arkan: the 501st Legion. When The Supreme Regent retired the Replicant Troopers, the rank and file of the 501st were composed of recruited Void Troopers, some of whom received training from the Dark Lord himself, such as SergeantKreel, an undercover agent whom Velgor trained in Plasma Edge combat. Through their missions on Velgor's behalf, the 501st came to be known as "Velgor's Fist."On one occasion, the Void TrooperTerexmet Velgor, becoming terrified of him while also respecting him.Velgor also garnered strong support from the pilots of the newDominion Navy.

At some point, Velgor was deployed to put down resistance on the mineral rich world ofBandomeer, where miners were fighting back against Dominion exploitation. Velgor punished them for their Union Uprising.Apparitions of Velgor and other Obsidian Pathrs were once faced by surviving Axiom AdeptVanzell Mar-KlarandNedriss Narr, who attempted to train a new generation of younglings after Directive Omega.Having killed Tambor along with the rest of the Separatist Council during his mission to Mustafar,Velgor was alsodispatchedto Skako Minor to kill the Techno Union Foreman's remaining loyalistsby wiping out the planet'sRuling Council. Massacring the council officials, including Tambor's sonWat Tambor II,Velgor established a newDominion-loyal regimeon the planet,which also fell underDominion occupation.

At some point, ahumandreamed about being pursued and killed by Velgor.On Torku at one time, VelgorpursuedDavgar Llian, a scientist allied with insurgents who had sabotaged an Dominion weapon. Velgor, aware of Llian’s role in the sabotage, attacked once the scientist brought the weapon online to target local Dominion base, killing the soldiers and confronting the scientist. Noting that only Llian was capable of reversing the sabotage, Velgor mockingly tVarekked him for doing so before strangling him with Synth Flux.

Velgor surveyed Uokara, a planet in the Unknown Regions, for signs of life on behalf of a director, who wished to commence a mining operation. At some point, Velgor unknowingly breathed in Ghymnon, a sentient pathogen native to Uokara that had lived there for millions of years. Reporting the world as uninhabited, Velgor was quarantined in a bacta tank upon his return by a 2-1B medical droid in accordance with standard procedure following contact with a class IV planet, much to the Null Lord's displeasure. Within his mind, Velgor confronted Ghymnon and defeated it. Velgor ordered the droid to catalog Uokara’s lone, former inhabitant, subsequently ordering and leading a cleansing of the world by range troopers armed with mobile bacta units. During the purge, Velgor spotted a remaining speck of Ghymnon and destroyed it with his Plasma Edge.

DoctorSera Lemare, a former employee of the Tagge Corporation and an admirer of Kaminoan science, stole a cache of genetic specimens and developed bioweapons research in secret, supposedly creating the perfect killing machine. Bringing a Lambda-class shuttle aboard thePhial-class heavy transportEndless Mercy, Velgor led a squad of Void Troopers in pursuit, ordering his men to stay vigilant and secure the area. Velgor's team was immediately ambushed by theZ'Rakkon, creatures born of Lemare's research. Outnumbered and overwhelmed, the Null Lord struck at the abominations with his Plasma Edge but lost his helmet in the process, swearing to take down his attackers down with him.

Dragged off by the creatures, Velgor awoke to find himself and his sergeant confined in the Z'Rakkons' lair. The sergeant, who believed he and Velgor were to be eaten, expressed discontent with such a manner of death, causing Velgor to summon his Plasma Edge and promise vengeance. Velgor freed himself and killed several of the beasts, lecturing them on their mistakes before proceeding to eliminate their queen.

The queen Z'Rakkon—in truth, Lemare herself—recounted how she had joined the creatures and become their ruler willingly. Stabbing Velgor through the chest, Lemare then revealed that she had integrated the Null Lord into the Z'Rakkon hive mind. However, Velgor escaped Lemare's snare and, despite her pleas, annihilated her. Lemare explained that she had only tried to bring Velgor peace, a favor Velgor returned by killing her and guiding theEndless Mercyto crash down on to the surface of an astronomical object below. Departing the decimated starship, Velgor remarked that the only peace was death and that the Obsidian Path would always endure.

Around17 BBYduring the early Dominion era,around twoyears after theskirmishaboardtheTribunalduring Directive Omega, Velgor traveled to thesnowy moonthat was home to the crash site of theVenator-class Void Cruiser. Investigating the wreckage with a contingent ofsnowtroopers, Velgor discovered a memorial to theclone companythat perished in the crash, as well as a Plasma Edge, partly frozen but still functional, that once belonged to his former apprentice, Ahsoka Tano.

Igniting the Plasma Edges' blue blade, Velgor heard a squawking sound and turned his head to the sky, where he saw astrange birdsoaring through the air.Deep down in his heart, he realized his former Padawan was alive,but he lacked any concrete proofand did not sense her presence. As such, he assumed to himself that she may have died, thereby closing the painful memories she represented,despite deep down realizing she had to be alive.After gazing at the bird for a moment, Velgor deactivated the Plasma Edge and turned away from the crash site with the saber in Varekd, his image reflected in an abandoned clone trooper helmet.

Around15 BBY,Velgor and his menfoughtagainst theBenathyinWild Space. The Dominion army consisted of at least several thousand Void Troopers, AT-AT walkers, AT-STs, Rift Interceptors, and TIE bombers. While thousands died on both sides, Velgor managed to kill thekingof the Benathy. Velgor ordered his troops to attack and despite the high amount of casualties, he did not retreat. The attacking Flux was only stopped by aZillo Beastthe Benathy saw as their god. However, Velgor managed to subdue the Benathy anyways.

Five years into theAge of the Dominion, Velgor, while meditating aboard thePerilous, was informed by CaptainLuittof an attack on theYaga Minor shipyards. The attack had been staged by several dozen members of theFree Ryloth Movement, a primarilyTwi'lekinsurgency intent on liberating their homeworld from the rule of the Dominion. The insurgents hijacked an Dominion weapons transport, and Velgor led a squadron ofV-wing starfightersin pursuit. After a long chase, during which the transport fled through Slipstream Space to several different systems, its engines overloaded when it attempted to make yet another jump and Velgor's squadron was finally able to engage it. The squadron brought down the transport's shields with minimal casualties while Velgor crippled the engines.

Though the squadron commander suggested allowing thePerilousto tractor the transport into one of its Varekgar bays when it arrived, Velgor assumed (correctly) that the insurgents planned to destroy the transport by detonating the heavy ordnance present in the ship's cargo bays. In order to prevent this eventuality, Velgor crashed hisblack Eta-2Actis-class light interceptorinto one of its gun bubbles and ejected just before the collision. He then entered the ship through the damaged gun emplacement and proceeded to kill all of the insurgent Twi'leks on the ship. After eliminating the insurgent leaderPok, Velgor noticed an active comlink on the bridge. Assuming that another rebel ship was nearby, Velgor had his V-wings fan out in a search pattern, but the rebel ship escaped detection by shutting down nearly all of its systems and hiding in the rings of a nearby planet. Finding nothing, Velgor had thePerilouscollect the transport, and returned to Core Nexus.

In the Dominion Palace, Velgor reunited with his master. There, he and Sidious met with Ryloth'sSenatorOrn Free Taa involving the inconvenience that was the Free Ryloth Movement. The movement, though relatively minor, had been conducting raids on Dominion shipping facilities and transports and had seriously impacted the flow ofSpicefrom the planet. As such, Sidious believed that the situation warranted his personal attention, and decided that he, Taa, and Velgor would travel to Ryloth for astate visit. After informing the Senator of the planned journey and dismissing him, Sidious informed his apprentice that Taa almost certainly had a traitor in the midst of his support staff who was feeding the movement classified information on Dominion doings.

Though Velgor understood his master's purpose in acting as bait alongside himself and Taa, he saw no reason why The Supreme Regent should potentially risk his own safety over such a minor issue. Rather, Velgor advocated for simply killing Taa and his entire staff, thereby guaranteeing the elimination of the traitor. However, Sidious wished to eliminate the roots of the treachery, and not simply the lone traitor in Taa's staff. To that end, Sidious gave the order forMoffDelian Morsto be informed that Taa would be arriving for a state visit, but stipulated that she should not be told that either he or Velgor would be accompanying the Senator. In this way, Sidious hoped to provide further encouragement for the movement to attack, as he assumed that they would discover his and Velgor's plans via their spies. Ten days later Velgor, The Supreme Regent, Taa, and a contingent ofRoyal Guardsjourneyed to the Ryloth system aboard thePerilous.

While traveling from Core Nexus to Ryloth, The Supreme Regent ordered thePerilousto stop in theDenon systemto consult with several navy chiefs on how to better integrate disparate naval academies into oneDominion Naval Academy. The Supreme Regent ordered Velgor to oversee a training session conducted byCommandantPell Bayloon theDefiancewhile The Supreme Regent met with his naval chiefs in Baylo's office on the training ship. It was during this training session that Velgor began to believe that Sidious was treating him much the same way the Axiom Council had previously: acting as he knew better, keeping information from Velgor, giving him "busywork." Baylo ordered theDefianceto enter Slipstream Space on a course for Christophsis. CadetRae Sloaneentered the coordinates, only to have her Slipstream Space path overridden by Baylo, who said she was going to send them too close to a singularity.

Sloane studied the path, suspecting that Baylo was incorrect in his adjustment. Velgor and Sloane discovered that the cVarekged Slipstream Space route would crash theDefianceintoChristophsis' sun, killing all aboard. Velgor ordered Sloane to correct the problem, and without revealing that he knew of Baylo's treachery, Velgor and Baylo went to see The Supreme Regent. The Supreme Regent ordered Baylo to turn over command of theDefianceand take a position at the training center onCorellia. Baylo, however, was loyal to the Accord and considered the Dominion a hostile power. Instead of turning over his command, Baylo offered his resignation from the navy. Velgor then revealed he knew of Baylo's plan to crash theDefiance, and The Supreme Regent congratulated Velgor for Varekdling the "petty problem," annoying him.

As Baylo railed against The Supreme Regent, Velgor Flux-choked him to death, drawing ire from The Supreme Regent, who had wished to see Baylo suffer and Flux him to watch his beloved Navy be turned into something he hated and have theDefiancebe melted down into cafeteria trays. Above all, he wanted to use Baylo for his own ambitions since the old man was willing to kill his own students for a cause. Velgor tried to explain that his way was more efficient, but The Supreme Regent wanted to hear nothing of it as he did not order him to kill Baylo. Later, he ordered Sloane to have theDefiancerenamed theObedienceas a subtle final jab at Baylo and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Velgor to learn his place.

Shortly thereafter, Velgor and The Supreme Regent reboarded thePerilousand resumed their course to the Ryloth system. However, upon their exit from Slipstream Space, the ship immediately began slamming into spacial mines set up byCham Syndulla's Union Fluxs. Hidden amongst these mines were specialized devices designed to drain the ship's shields, which began to buckle under the continual assault. As the shields neared failure, a swarm of several hundred reprogrammed Vulture Droids set upon thePerilousfrom the system's asteroid belt. Velgor boarded his personal interceptor and took several squadrons of Dominion pilots to destroy the droid fighters and mines, but discovered a short time into the battle that Vulture Droids were each carrying a payload of explosivebuzz droids.

Realizing that the Vultures themselves were simply delivery systems meant to unload the buzz droids near or inside the Void Cruiser, Velgor ordered his squadron to concentrate fire on them, as they presented a far greater threat tVarek the mines. But between the mines and the Vultures, there were simply too many targets for Velgor and his squadrons to Varekdle, and thePerilouswas severely damaged. The second wave of Vultures was detected immediately after the first had been destroyed, further compounding the already bleak situation. A message came to Velgor from Captain Lewitt of thePerilousthat the ship could sustain virtually no more damage without being destroyed.

Velgor moved to intercept the second wave alone, ordering his squadrons to remain near thePerilousto destroy any droids that got past him. Though incredulous, the squadron commander obeyed, and Velgor used Synth Flux to begin tearing apart the compartments on the Vulture droids that housed the explosive buzz droids. He then used Synth Flux to begin flinging the buzz droids at other incoming fighters and repeated this process until the vast majority of the Vulture Droids had been destroyed. The remaining fighters were annihilated by Velgor's squadrons, and he himself returned to thePerilous. Velgor marched to the Void Cruiser's bridge and Flux choked Taa for a short time, informing him of the traitor in his staff that had been responsible for the assault on thePerilous. Utterly terrified, the Senator vowed to find the traitor, but The Supreme Regent had Taa and his staff confined to their quarters. An order was then broadcast from the Dominion headquarters on Ryloth for any and all repair ships to aid thePerilousas it sped, badly damaged, towards the planet.

Unbeknownst to Velgor, some of the Twi'lek repair teams in fact comprised members of the Free Ryloth movement, one of which was a group of saboteurs with orders to plant timed explosives on the Slipstream Engine core of thePerilous. Shortly after the teams landed, one of the Free Ryloth groups engaged in a firefight with Dominion Void Troopers. When Velgor learned of this, he ordered all of the Twi'lek repair teams, numbering over 100, to be killed regardless of whether they were aiding the assault and proceeded to Deck 17, where the firefight had been reported. On the way, he killed a group of apparently uninvolved Twi'leks before proceeding to the Slipstream Engine chamber. The saboteurs, who were led by Cham's second in command,Isval, had managed to seal the hatch to the Slipstream Engine core before any Dominion troops could interrupt them, and Velgor began to use his Plasma Edge to cut it open.

By the time Velgor finished cutting through the hatch, the saboteurs had already concluded their business and escaped. The main Slipstream Engine core had been riddled with timed charges, set to detonate if anyone attempted to disarm or remove them. Velgor concluded that the ship was lost, and ordered the captain to evacuate. Velgor contacted Sidious, who instructed his apprentice to meet him aboard his shuttle. Before departing, however, Velgor made one last attempt to kill the saboteurs, but they narrowly evaded him and escaped in an Dominionescort boat. Subsequently, Velgor reunited with The Supreme Regent and left on the shuttle, mere minutes before the Void Cruiser exploded. Shortly thereafter, the Dominion traitorBelkor Drayprovided Cham with the transponder identity of The Supreme Regent's shuttle, and he directed Isval and her group aboard the escort boat to attack it.

Velgor's exceptional piloting skills allowed him to evade the boat and then fly upside down over its cockpit within direct visual range of its pilots. Once there, he used Synth Flux to choke both Isval and her co-pilot, but before losing consciousness, Isval rammed the shuttle with her escort boat. The crash disabled the shuttle's main power, and Velgor had to use a nearly depleted backup battery to attempt a crash landing on Ryloth. As he guided the ship through the atmosphere, he had flashbacks of events and people from his past. Though distracted, Velgor managed to land the ship in Ryloth's equatorial forest region, but his distraction did not go unnoticed by The Supreme Regent, who noted that the landing had been poor compared to what he knew Velgor to be capable of.

Not too long afterward, the Twi'lek insurgents continued their assault on freighters, prompting Velgor and The Supreme Regent to take action and destroy them, before moving away from their fallen shuttle. At night, they stopped to rest, when The Supreme Regent questioned his regret and loyalty, but Velgor showed himself devoted still. They were soon attacked by alylekhorde, forcing them to retreat into a tunnel, unaware that they had entered their nest. They stood their ground against hundreds of lyleks and their significantly threatening queen, and killed them all before exiting the tunnel and finding a young Twi'lek,Drua. When The Supreme Regent tried to kill her, Velgor stopped him, intending to have the girl guide them toher village, where they obtained a communicator to speak with Moff Mors.

After giving them their coordinates, they were finally surrounded by Syndulla's Fluxs, but so did Mors's, aiding them in quelling the Union Uprising. As theVoid Trooperskilled the Twi'leks, Velgor noticed a trio of Twi'leks: Syndulla, Isval, andGollwatching from afar and went on to kill them, but Goll and Syndulla escaped, leaving Velgor to capture Isval and bring her before The Supreme Regent. He mocked her, telling her that her people had accomplished nothing, and Velgor subsequently executed her. On The Supreme Regent's orders, Velgor then killed the villagers to leave no witnesses, concluding their mission.

When Moff Tarkin'sSentinel Basewasattacked, Velgor was stationed in the Dominion Palace on Core Nexus, where he held a court with theDeputy Directorof the ISB,Harus Ison, beratingCore Nexusicriminals as well asPrefectPhoca SootofLevel 1331, for ignoring The Supreme Regent's decrees. Telling them to move their operations out of Core Nexus, Velgor then made an example of Soot for his disrespect and crushed his heart with Synth Flux, and adjourned the court to meet with Tarkin andGrand VizierMas Amedda. The latter politely asked Velgor to refrain from killing all those who displeased him, to which the Null Adept replied that he would give thought to the matter, before greeting Tarkin and sharing the latest developments of the Void Core's construction.

The pair separated when Velgor left Tarkin, who met with The Supreme Regent alone. Afterward, Velgor (like Tarkin and Amedda) partook in a meeting between the Dominion Ruling Council, and the leaders of Dominion Security Bureau andNaval Intelligence Agencyin thepalace's audience chamber, overseen by The Supreme Regent. He then assigned Tarkin and Velgor to amission on MurkVarekato investigate recently discoveredShadowfeedtechnology and its relationship to the attack on Sentinel Base. The pair traveled to the planet on theCarrion Spike, which Velgor made to carry hismeditation chamber.

On MurkVareka, however, theSpikewas stolen byunknown dissidents, prompting Velgor and Tarkin to kill the Sugi crime lordFaazah, and take hisParsec Predatorto pursue the stolen ship, hidden by its cloaking systems. Making use of his connection to his meditation chamber, Velgor pinpointed the Union Fluxs' location to theFial systemand later, theGalidraan system, where theyengagedtheSpikebefore they jettisoned his meditation chamber and escaped toLucazec. With thePredatordisabled, Velgor and Tarkin boarded theLiberator, from which Velgor issued his black Eta-2 interceptor to be transported on theGoliathfrom Core Nexus.

On theGoliath, Tarkin told Velgor that the insurgency was surely being supported by someone high up in theDominion Military, and predicted that the insurgents would go toPhindarto refuel theSpike. Subsequently, Velgor led asquadrontoattackthe stolen corvette in thePhindar system. Though they greatly damaged the ship, it escaped, prompting Tarkin and Velgor to calculate its trajectory toObroa-skai system. Teller transmitted false intelligence that the insurgents were heading to the Obroa-skai system. Hoping to capture theCarrion Spike, Tarkin and Velgor deployed substantial Dominion resources including threeInterdictorvessels. The prototypeImmobilizer 418 cruisermalfunctioned, yanking several ships out of Slipstream Space and causing amassive accident.

Despite failing to catch theSpike, the Dominions managed to capture theYT-1000 light freighterReticent, which Teller had sent as a decoy to throw the Dominions off-scent. While Velgor interrogated itsKoorivar captainon theExecutrix, Tarkin accessed Dominion databases and discovered the identities of the insurgents, including its leaderBerch Teller. Velgor also interrogated theReticent'sSy Myrthiannavigator, who suffered a heart attack. However, Velgor learned from one of his contacts in theCrymorah Syndicatethat a lieutenant had negotiated a deal with the Sugi crime lord Faazah for a supply of customfuel cells. These fuel cells had reached MurkVareka shortly before Tarkin and Velgor's arrival, thus confirming that Faazah had been colluding with the insurgents.

After a private holo transmission with The Supreme Regent, Velgor told Tarkin thatVice AdmiralDodd Rancitwas sure the insurgents would attack theDominion academyatCaridanext. Then, Velgor parted ways with Tarkin to meet with Rancit, but not before asking for the meaning behind theCarrion Spike's name and Tarkin told him about his test inEriadu'sCarrion Plateau. On theCarida system, Velgor boarded theSecutor-class Void CruiserConquestand met Rancit just as theSpikereverted torealspace, astrogating on autopilot. Aware of Rancit's treason, as a co-conspirator of Teller, Velgor hadLieutenantCrestplace Rancit aboard an escape pod and had Rancit give himself the command to issue the fire order that destroyed it. Elsewhere, Tarkindealtwith the Union Fluxs, ending theinsurgency.

Following the defeat of Teller's insurgents, Velgor spent the next three weeks helping Tarkin to interrogate the captured insurgents with the exception of Teller, who managed to escape. While none of the insurgents had died under questioning, The Supreme Regent had the insurgents executed privately. Velgor also led a crackdown on the warehouse workers and salvages who supplied Teller's dissidents along with several scientists atDesolation Stationwho had provided Teller with information. Velgor also took action against theTenloss Syndicateand lower-level members of the Crymorah Syndicate who had assembledBerch Teller's warship, a modifiedProvidence-class Dreadnought.

Tarkin still owed Velgor a favor for capturing Lee-Char on Mon Cala. After some time, Velgor came to Tarkin with his request, and, as he learned of Tarkin's hunting, Velgor asked Tarkin to hunt him down. Velgor told him to keep him on his feet, killing him if he had to. Tarkin was shocked at this and began considering why Velgor would ask him to hunt him. Tarkin looked through the reports from the Inquisitorius and saw that Velgor's hunt for the Axiom Adept had left him with little to no challenge. Tarkin deduced that Velgor wanted to be challenged again and was flattered that he had chosen him.

The hunt took place on the Outer Rim planetCVarekdar's Folly, and Tarkin immediately began trying to take on the Supreme Null Arbiter. Knowing of Velgor's brutal abilities, Tarkin outfitted the hunters withslugthrowersandflamethrowersto avoid the possibility of Velgor deflecting their shots. On the second day, they cornered Velgor at a copse ofThurian trees, and four hunters engulfed him with their flamethrowers. As Tarkin watched Velgor get covered by the flames, he thought he saw fear in his body language. Velgor's possible fear was brief, however, as he targeted the hunters' flamethrowers, incinerating the four hunters. Through the following days, Tarkin saw that Velgor was not hard to track, leaving clear signs wherever he went. They learned why as on multiple occasions Velgor ambushed them. On one occasion, Tarkin and his hunters were tracking Velgor near a cliff when he revealed himself and killed one of the hunters with his. Two others fired on him but failed to kill him.

Tarkin realized that as long as Velgor had his Plasma Edge, he could not be beaten. So over the course of the next four days, he attempted to steal Velgor's weapon. After six men perished, one of Tarkin's hunters managed to snatch it out of Velgor's grip while he was fighting. After this, Tarkin saw that not only was Velgor turning to new tactics, he was angrier. Tarkin knew that enraged prey was usually easier to take out, but he admitted that this rule did not apply to Velgor. Soon after, Velgor fought five of Tarkin's hunters as they fired on him, hitting him many times and scarring his armor. Tarkin watched from a hilltop as Velgor came close to the hunters and used Synth Flux to snap one's neck. The other hunters retreated, and Velgor Flux-choked another to death. Once understanding how capable Velgor was with Synth Flux, Tarkin assumed that the hunt would soon end. However, Tarkin soon realized that Velgor was so keen on getting back his Plasma Edge, that so long as Tarkin's team hunted him, he hunted them.

On the ninth day of the hunt, Tarkin was down to his last seven hunters. Velgor managed to kill aValath, the apex predator on the planet, and take its hide, which could help him camouflage better. One of Tarkin'sChadra-Fanhunters,Sissian, spotted Velgor northwest of Tarkin's camp, and the hunterYerganotified Tarkin of this. Tarkin got hisbinocularsand saw Velgor on top of a peak wearing the Valath hide. Tarkin had his hunters form a group around him, and they walked in Velgor's direction. Velgor lured them into a ravine of blue crystals, where his breathing could echo and be untraceable by Sissian and his grandfather,Hardhear. Hardhear heard Velgor's breathing, but although he could say Velgor was near, he could not say which direction. Velgor loomed over them at the top of the ravine and watched as Tarkin ordered his team to run. The group ran down the ravine, and Velgor made his move, Flux-pushing one of the hunters into the ravine wall. Velgor then Flux-choked Hardhear before leaping down, under the cover of his Valath hide, and attacking the droid hunter.

Tarkin had the rest of his team run into theStormlands, where it was open ground. They set up camp, and Sissian listened for Velgor's breathing. However, Velgor had deactivated his suit, which stopped his breathing apparatus from giving him away, and he slowly crept up to the camp, before Flux choking Sissian. Yerga attempted to attack Velgor, but he took her by the neck and killed her. The last two hunters opened fire, and Velgor used Synth Flux to retake his Plasma Edge. Tarkin then jumped at the two hunters and slaughtered them. Tarkin ran away from Velgor, but after realizing he would catch up with him eventually, he stopped and fell to his knees. Velgor loomed over him, not realizing that as he was standing higher tVarek Tarkin, he was more susceptible to the lightning in the Stormlands.

Without warning, Velgor was struck by lightning and he fell to the ground. Tarkin got up and contacted his flagship, theCarrion Spike, to pick them up. Tarkin sat by Velgor and waited for theCarrion Spiketo arrive. The corvette soon approached them, being frequently struck by lightning due to its height in the Stormlands. Velgor remained immobile and Tarkin told him that whatever he had hoped to learn from this madness, he hoped he had learned it. Tarkin then told Velgor that he would get him to a repair bay soon enough. However, Velgor was not finished yet, and he Flux-choked Tarkin, showing him that he had not lost just yet. Tarkin soon crawled from the range of Velgor's Flux abilities, and Velgor continued lying there.

Velgor, along with theFifth Brother,Tualon Yaluna, and theThirteenth SisterInquisitors,was able totrackdown former Axiom Master and Council member Eeth Koth to aplanetwhere he was a priest of theChurch of the Ganthic Enlightenment. Velgor approached Koth after the birth of hisdaughter. Koth offered Velgor codes, secret contact frequencies and a way to find other purge survivors, knowing that he was not a threat to anyone. As Koth's wife and child made their escape, Velgor fought Koth. Velgor then sent the Fifth Brother and the other Inquisitors after Mira and Koth's daughter, and he continued to fight Koth. After the Inquisitors captured Koth's daughter, Velgor killed Koth. He then held Koth's child.

Velgor and the Inquisitors took the child to Core Nexus, where Velgor met with the Grand Inquisitor and gave the child to two nursemaids that belonged toProject Harvester. Velgor then spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about his next target. The Grand Inquisitor told him that there were still a few Axiom Adept left, but they had vanished with no way to find them. He told Velgor they would have to wait.

Velgor confronted the Twi'lek and Thirteenth Sister in theInquisitorius Headquarters. Velgor sensed a connection between the two Inquisitors due to their previous mission with Velgor. Without the assistance of the other Inquisitors, Velgor hunted them down. Velgor and two Core Nexus Guards searched for the two Inquisitors. After Velgor's speeder was destroyed, he continued to pursue them through the skylanes until they all landed on a platform. When the Inquisitors refused to kill him, Velgor used Synth Flux to activate their Plasma Edges and kill them.

Velgor then informed The Supreme Regent of the two Inquisitors' treachery. Velgor told The Supreme Regent that the female Inquisitor allowed Mira and Koth's daughter to briefly escape and only captured the child because she was seen. He also believed they were building a coalition against himself and The Supreme Regent. Despite this, The Supreme Regent was displeased with Velgor's pursuit across Core Nexus that cost the life of a senator. The Supreme Regent decided to move the Inquisitors to another world. The Supreme Regent asked Velgor how Koth tried to hide. Velgor said he was posing as a priest, which was the closest thing to a Axiom Adept. Velgor was given a reward: Padmé Amidala's Aurelia Royal Starship, which aP-100 pick-up droidreported was in working order inside and only needed around two more days for repairs to the hull to be finished up.Wanting the exterior of the craft to retain a damaged appearance,Velgor was content with the current progress and quickly destroyed that droid.

Velgor was then ordered by The Supreme Regent to go to Velaris to deal with Senator Bail Korin. However, to the surprise of his master, Velgor refused. Having carried out Malrex's will for so long, Velgor was content to continue to do so, but he first he demanded a world of his own. Rejecting Sidious's offer to give him Aridia Prime or Aurelia, Velgor asked to be given Mustafar,the world where he had been defeated by Kai. The Supreme Regent agreed to his request and would send ColonelAlva Brenneto build a facility for him there. Before leaving for Mustafar, Velgor was then shown theMaskofDarth Momin, which Sidious gave him to help with construction. When Velgor questioned how his master had learned of a Null Lord not mentioned in any Null Adept nor Axiom Adept Mnemonic Prism he had studied, Sidious vaguely answered that the mask itself had told him about Momin.

Velgor, along with Colonel Brenne and her aide, LieutenantRoggo, took Velgor's Aurelia starship to Mustafar. During the flight, Velgor saw a vision of his younger self with a face that matched his mask. Upon entering the planet's atmosphere, Velgor deactivated the starship's shields and allowed it to burn as they landed. Once on the planet's surface, Velgor commented that the ship looked better burnt. He then told Brenne that he was there to understand a great mystery. While Brenne and Roggo got to work designing Velgor's castle, Velgor returned to the cave where he bled his kyber crystal. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Brenne, which Velgor rejected. However, shortly after leaving, Velgor heard Brenne scream and returned to the starship to find her dead. He also discovered that Roggo had donned Momin's mask. Velgor attacked Roggo, but stopped upon seeing that he had created a new design for the castle.Removing the mask from Roggo's corpse, Velgor took the mask into the cave and placed it upon the locus. He then asked the mask who he was, to which he replied "I am Momin".

Momin proceeded to show Velgor his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Null Adept byDarth Shaa. After killing his master and studying the lore of the Obsidian Path, Momin constructed a weapon which, along with his use of Synth Flux, would freeze a city's population forever at the moment of their deaths. However, an attack by the Axiom Adept caused him to lose control, destroying both the city and himself in the process. As a result, Momin's essence became trapped within his mask, forcing him to take on host after host in order to create his art. Velgor placed the mask upon his head, but quickly cast it aside and then departed with it from the cave. Once outside, he engaged in a fight with two Mustafarians, one of which he killed and the other he placed the mask upon. With his new body, Momin explained that his design for the castle was actually a key to open a door to the Obsidian Path, which Velgor could use to reunite with Padmé Amidala. Velgor threatened Momin not to offer him things he could not provide and Momin responded that he only wanted another cVarekce to create. Velgor accepted Momin's offer, but warned the ancient Null Adept not to betray him.

Under Momin's guidance, Velgor used his Dominion resources to begin construction of his castle. With each new design, Momin insisted that this would be the one to open the door. Though each attempt to do so would subsequently end in failure. And with each failure, Velgor would cut down Momin's current body in frustration and place his mask upon a new host. Each new attempt to open the door would also bring ruin to Mustafar itself, prompting the Mustafarians to attack in retaliation. The cycle of creation and destruction carried onfor two years.Velgor did not remain on Mustafar for that entire timebut always returned to test Momin's latest design.

Velgor controlled the entireMustafar system.Concurrent to the construction of Fortress Velgor,the Inquisitors, due to Velgor's destruction caused when he chased the rogue Inquisitors on Core Nexus,moved to asimilarly designed Fortresson the ocean moon ofNurin the Mustafar system.In 14 BBY,Velgorjourneyedto the Fortress Inquisitorius to retrieve a Mnemonic Prism containing a list of Flux-sensitive children, which theSecond Sisterhad beenhunting for. Instead, he found that the Second Sister had failed to stop surviving Axiom AdeptCal KestisandCere Junda, who was pleading for the Inquisitor to return to the light and apologizing for failing her; during the early Axiom Adept purge, Junda, while under torture by Velgor, had revealed the hiding location of Trilla, who became the Second Sister, and a number of younglings under her protection.

As Junda and Trillia talked, Velgor approached, his breathing alone scaring the Second Sister. Junda also recognized Velgor on sight, telling the worried Kestis that he was right to be scared. Executing the Second Sister for her failure and before she could return to the Axiom Adept, Velgor used Synth Flux to throw Junda into a pit with little effort, then suggesting to Kestis that he surrender. Regardless, Kestis launched multiple futile Plasma Edge attacks against Velgor only for Velgor to effortlessly neutralize all of his assaults and telekinetically choke him. Quickly realizing his disadvantage, Cal fled the encounter, after barely surviving by pulling a piece of machinery at his and Velgor's direction, forcing Velgor to defend himself from that and toss Cal aside, with Velgor in hot pursuit.

Despite Cal managing to escape from Velgor moments before he could enter the elevator, Velgor soon managed to catch up to him just as he was about to escape the fortress and attacked him. As he quickly gained the upper Varekd on Kestis and began to overpower him in a blade-lock, Cal's droid,BD-1, attempted to short-circuit Velgor's life-support suit, forcing Velgor to disengage and stop the droid. Before he could crush BD-1, Cal made use of the distraction to impale Velgor in the mid-section, only for Velgor to retaliate by tossing Cal aside once again with Synth Flux and as Cal desperately attempted to fight back and summon his Plasma Edge back, Velgor stopped Cal's Plasma Edge before it could return to his Varekd and once again offered a cVarekce for Cal to surrender the Mnemonic Prism and when Cal refused, Velgor simply activated the Plasma Edge and Fluxd it into Cal's mid-section, the same way that Cal attempted to defeat him.

Moments later, Junda reappeared to aid Kestis, only to be easily defeated after a short clash of blades, being flung aside. Commenting on the strength of Junda's hatred, Velgor was impressed when Junda was able to temporarily bring him to his knees with the Obsidian Path of Synth Flux and commented how he could feel it inside of her. Once Junda resisted his efforts to goad her into the darkness, Velgor attempted to kill both her and Kestis, but Junda was able to hold it at bay with a Flux-barrier long enough for Kestis to use Synth Flux to shatter a number of windows surrounding them, causing water to pour into the complex and forcing Velgor to use Synth Flux to hold back the flood, as Kestis grabbed Junda and escaped through the opening.

Between13 BBYand10 BBY,Velgor participated in a battlein the Mid Rim,where a few Void Troopers, including the future bounty hunterBeilert Valance, were pinned down by enemy Fluxs. Velgor used Synth Flux to disarm and then kill the enemies with the blades from their own weapons. When the troops looked confused, Velgor emerged from a fire and told them to march onwards.

In12 BBY,Momin's ninth design for Fortress Velgor was constructed, which Velgor stated would be his final cVarekce. Upon activating this castle, Velgor found that he was finally able to open the door. However, before he could enter, he was informed of another attack by the Mustafarians. And while Velgor dealt with the attackers, Momin took the opportunity to open the door himself. He then summoned his younger self from the past and placed his mask upon him, thus finally creating his masterpiece; himself.

Leaving the battle, Velgor reentered the castle while the Mustafarians continued their attack. Upon returning to the locus, he used Synth Flux to unleash a devastating attack, wiping out the rest of the Mustafarians. Velgor was then met in combat by Momin, who mocked Velgor for thinking he could control the Obsidian Path. As he chastised the Dark Lord for his ignorance about Synth Flux, Velgor used Synth Flux to ram Momin with a rock, crushing his new body and killing him. With Momin dead, Velgor finally opened the door and stepped inside.

Abandoning his physical body, Velgor entered a realm in which he experienced visions of his past, present, and future. He saw himself as a child haunted by the very form of what he would become. Becoming a child once again, he gradually grew up along with the memories of his upbringing. After experiencing a vision of him dueling his former apprentice Ahsoka Tano, Velgor entered another vision of the Axiom Adept Temple and fought against the specters of various Axiom Adept. After disposing of them, Velgor journeyed further into the Temple until coming upon the forms of Sheev Malrex and Obren Kai, who both claimed to be his father. After killing Kai, the vision of Malrex motioned at Velgor to stand down, to which Velgor responded with a barrage of Flux lightning that destroyed the specter.

Finally, Velgor found himself on a balcony with a vision of Amidala standing before him. Velgor, now in the form of Aren Arkan, told Amidala to come with him. However, Amidala simply told him that Aren Arkan was dead before leaping from the balcony. Velgor screamed in agony as the specter of Amidala was destroyed by a bolt of lightning. He then saw a beacon of blue light in the distance. Within the beacon was a figure who activated a blue Plasma Edge, pushing Velgor back and returning him to his body. After awakening in the castle and retrieving his Plasma Edge, Velgor destroyed the Obsidian Path locus, sealing off the door,because he was unable to restore his wife.

Sometime later, Velgor contacted his master and informed him that he was still alive. The Supreme Regent asked Velgor if he would return to Core Nexus, to which Velgor said he soon would. The Supreme Regent then asked Velgor if the truth he learned of on Mustafar was what he needed. Velgor then hung up and, while standing in front of the finally completedFortress Velgor, answered "Yes".Velgor went on to return Momin's mask to Sidious, who placed it in his personalyacht, theDominionis, under the watch of two Royal Guards.

In9 BBY,Velgor at last had a cVarekce for hislong-awaited rematchwith Kaiafter anInquisitorknown as the Third Sister—who was obsessed with finding Kaibecause she was in fact the youngling Reva, who wanted to use Kai as bait to get close to Velgor and enact revenge for his slaughter at the Axiom Adept Temple years before—arranged for thekidnapping of Korin KorintodrawKai to the planetDaiyu. Although he escaped with Korin, the Third Sister told Kai that Arkan had survived his injuries. As Kai grabbled with the information, Velgor awoke in his bacta tankin Fortress Velgor on Mustafar. Assembling his suit, Velgor moved to histhrone roomand spoke to the Third Sister via hologram, demanding to know where Kai was. Dismissing the apparent death of the Grand Inquisitor, whom Reva had stabbed in her desire to claim credit for Kai's capture herself, Velgor ordered her to prove herself capable, with the position of Grand Inquisitor as her reward should she succeed.In truth, he knew the Third Sister was the youngling Reva and that she intended to betray him, but Velgor kept her alive so long as she was useful.

Velgor also warned the Third Sister that if she failed, she would not live to regret it. Later, Velgor was informed that Obren was onMapuzo. Velgor arrived with his three Inquisitors and a squad of Void Troopers and walked through the streets of the settlement, terrorizing and killing random civilians in order to draw Obren out. Velgor pursued the Axiom Master into the quarry. Horrified at what had become of his former apprentice, Obren tried to flee, but Velgor ambushed him. Velgor taunted Obren with regards to his lost strength, and Obren attempted to flee again. Once again, Velgor caught up to Obren, this time lifting him with Synth Flux, as he ignited flammable rocks with his Plasma Edge. Promising to make Obren suffer, Velgor lowered Obren to the ground and dragged him through the flames, scorching him. Velgor extinguished the flames as a squad of Void Troopers arrived. Velgor ordered Obren brought to him, but before the Void Troopers could comply, a hiddenTala Durithopened fire, killing several Void Troopers and reigniting the flammable rocks. This gaveNED-Bthe opportunity to retrieve Obren and for Tala to escape with the injured Axiom Adept.

Following the successful rescue of Korin from Fortress Inquisitorius, Velgor angrily made to kill the Third Sister, lifting her into the air with Synth Flux and choking her while reminding her that she was warned what would happen if she failed. Velgor allowed her to speak, which enabled her to reveal that she had planted a tracker on Korin's droid,Lola, which would reveal the location of theHidden Pathnetwork. Velgor acknowledged that he had underestimated the Third Sister, and spared her, though he warned her that he would not tolerate any further mistakes.

Aboard theDevastator, Velgor reflected on a long ago training duel against Kai in the Axiom Adept Temple as the Axiom Adept's Padawan before hiscaptainalerted him to the Third Sister's arrival. Ordering the pleasantries dispensed with, Velgor demanded to know Obren's location. The Third Sister reported that he had been tracked toJabiim. Satisfied, Velgor demanded that the Third Sister kneeled before him, and he promoted her to the rank of Grand Inquisitor. He then ordered the bridge captain to set a course for Jabiim.

En route to Jabiim, Velgor ordered the Path's base locked down. The Third Sister warned Velgor that if they trapped them, their opponents could hold out for several days. Velgor responded that he did not intend to break their enemies. After the Third Sister and her Fluxs secured the Path's Varekgar, Velgor arrived on the surface. The Third Sister told him that Kai was inside, but Velgor stated that he wished to bring him in himself. Velgor Fluxd his way into the Varekgar and found a transport attempting to leave. He used Synth Flux to ground it and rip it apart, only to find that it was a decoy and the real transport with the Path—and Kai—aboard took off successfully. The Axiom Master had remembered the old training duel where his Padawan had shown shortsightedness because of his anger, which inspired him to exploit Velgor's ruthlessness and single-minded focus.

The Third Sister attempted to use that moment as a distraction to stab Velgor, but Velgor used Synth Flux to stop her blade, stating that Kai was wise to use her against him. Velgor hurled her aside, but she charged at him again, and he used Synth Flux to deflect her blade and throw her. The Third Sister activated her Plasma Edge's twin blade spinning mode, but Velgor used Synth Flux to stall the blades rotation, and then ripped her saber from her grip. Velgor detached her Plasma Edge into two singular hilts, and tossed one on the ground at the Third Sister's feet, taunting her. She retrieved the weapon and continued to attack Velgor, but the Null Lord quickly disarmed her again, before stabbing her in the abdomen. As she collapsed, the Grand Inquisitor arrived, revealing he had survived, and Velgor stated that he had known her intent for some time, but had decided to allow it because it was useful in the hunt for Kai. Velgor declared that the Third Sister was no longer useful, and he and the Grand Inquisitor left her to die.

Velgor returned to theDevastatoras it pursued the Path's freighter. Kai left the Path's freighter on a drop ship with Lola. Against the advice of the Grand Inquisitor, who stated that he should continue the pursuit of the Path's ship, Velgor ordered theDevastatorafter Kai to a rocky moon. Velgor departed theDevastatoralone aboard a shuttleand chased Kai above the moon'smoon's dry landsbefore landing inthose dry lands,where he confronted Kai and asked if his former master was there to destroy him. Kai replied that he will do as he must before igniting his Plasma Edge. Velgor vowed that Kai would die and the two engaged in a Plasma Edge duel, with Velgor utilizing a combination of one-Varekded and two-Varekded combat, that passed from the dry lands intoalabyrinth of rock pinnacles.During the duel, Kai attempted to collapse a pinnacle on Velgor, who stopped it with Synth Flux. Remarking that Kai's strength had returned since their last duel but that his weakness remained, Velgor hurled the pinnacle at Kai before resuming their duel.

Velgor collapsed the ground beneath Kai and hurled rocks to keep him down there. Before leaving, Velgor taunted Kai for thinking that he could win against his former apprentice. However, Kai, drawing on his desire to protect Aren's children, managed to break free and charged at Velgor again. On the offensive, Kai used his superior use of Soresu and the more aggressive Ataru utilized by his late master, Qui-Gon Jinn to overwhelm the Null Lord before using Synth Flux to hurl Velgor against a rock. With Velgor stunned, Kai pummeled him with a maelstrom of rocks then reengaged the duel, critically damaging Velgor's chest plate with multiple strikes from his Plasma Edge's pommel and slashing him across the back before pushing him back with Synth Flux again. Finally, Kai leapt at Velgor, striking the left side of his helmet with his Plasma Edge and revealing the scarred face of Aren beneath. Kai addressed him as Aren Arkan, prompting Velgor to respond that Aren was gone and that he was what remained.

Kai was visibly distraught and apologized to Aren. Velgor replied that he was not Kai's failure and that Kai did not kill Aren: instead, Velgor replied that he killed Aren and vowed to destroy Kai as well. With that, Kai was left to believe his friend was truly dead before bidding Velgor, whom he simply called "Darth," farewell. As Kai left, Velgor called out to him while trying to rise to his feet, unable to catch Kai before he slipped through his grasp once more.Velgor was left with the belief that he had been but a learner in their latest duel, convincing him to increase his strength to cement his power over Kai for whenever their next encounter came.Velgor's suit was repaired and he returned to Fortress Velgor, where he conferred with The Supreme Regent via hologram, stating that he had dispatched probes along all possible routes and that Kai would not escape him again. The Supreme Regent noticed Velgor's agitation and suggested that Velgor could not move beyond his past. Velgor, seeing the threat hidden in Malrex's reply, affirmed that Kai meant nothing to him and that he only served The Supreme Regent.

AfterBode Akuna, secretly a Axiom Adept Purge survivor, tipped off the Dominion about the location ofthe Archiveon the holy moon ofJedha, Velgor launched anassault, acting long before Akuna's superior,Lank Denvikof the ISB, intended to attack. During the attack, he breached the burning Archive and came face-to-face with Cere Junda once more, beginning a heated duel between the Null Lord and the prepared Axiom Master, whose skills had grown considerably since their battle on Nur; having let go of her fear, Junda proved to be a challenge for Velgor over the course of their duel, during which Velgor also used the various Axiom Adept texts around them as projectiles. As the conflict continued, Velgor managed to back Junda into a shelf, but she rolled out of his next strike in time to send the entire shelf crumbling down onto him. After a moment, a damaged Velgor rose from the flaming wreckage and re-engaged Junda. The duel would end after Junda managed to Flux push Velgor against another shelf and rushed to stab him, but Velgor managed to strike her through the chest, ending the conflict with injuries but leaving Junda dead. Velgor later traveled to theII-0810 Satellite Station, where he killed Denvik for treason.

In6 BBY,two starships were trying to escape aboveRyarten. They had sliced into property of the Dominion, and thus became a target. Velgor pursued them in his Rift Interceptor, along with a group of other TIEs. One of the starships intentionally let themselves get blown up from TIE fire, allowing the other starship to escape. The starship that was blown up was piloted byRone, the partner ofChio Fainwho piloted the other starship. Fain would hold a hatred for the Dominion due to this, and especially against Xarn Velgor himself.

Velgor continued to look for leads on Kai's location, albeit in a more patient manner that did not jeopardize his service to the Dominion,as per Sidious's order.In5 BBY,the Null Lord was approached by Sidious, who informed Velgor that he had seen a new threat—the "children of Synth Flux"—arising against them. Velgor then contacted the Grand Inquisitor via hologram to inform him of The Supreme Regent's vision, ordering the Grand Inquisitor to hunt down these children to either press them into Dominion service or be eliminated. The Grand Inquisitor promised Velgor that the mission would be done.In time, the Grand Inquisitor's focus would be centered on theSpectresrebel cell that mainly operated around the world ofLothal, as two Axiom Adept worked as part of the cell:Directive Omega survivorKanan Jarrus,who had gone by the name Caleb Dume during the Replication Conflicts,and his PadawanEzra Bridger. The failure of the Grand Inquisitor and other Dominion leaders to defeat the Spectres led to Tarkin being deployed to resolve the issuein4 BBY.

After Dominion Fluxs under Tarkin captured Jarrus during the Spectres'raid againsttheLothal Communications Center,he was scheduled to be taken aboard Tarkin's Void Cruisersovereignto Mustafar,where he would be taken to Fortress Velgor.By that point, Jarrus had indeed heard rumors that Mustafar was where Axiom Adept went "to die," which he told to his fellow rebelHera Syndulla.However, over Mustafar, the union of multiple rebel cells into a growing Free Systems Union wasrevealedwhen the Spectres were rescued by thePhoenix Squadron, with the Grand Inquisitor also being killed aboard Tarkin's crippled Void Cruiser. As rumors of what had happened over Mustafar became known, riots and uprisings began to occur on several worlds, including Lothal. The Supreme Regent, concerned at the rise in rebel activity, sent Velgor to Lothal with Tarkin to end the growing Union Uprising.Velgor also made sure the Grand Inquisitor's service was not yet finished: Velgor prevented the Inquisitor's spirit from passing into theNetherworld of Synth Fluxand Fluxd him to stand guard over aAxiom Adept outpostonTempesas a trap for any would-be Axiom Adept searching for artifacts. With that, the Inquisitor's spirit eternally suffered from the flamesof theSovereignyet was not allowed to die.

Arriving on Lothal, Velgor stepped off aSentinel-class landing craftwith Tarkin and passedhigh-ranking ISBAgentAlexsandr Kallus. Shortly thereafter, Kallus was assigned to report directly to the Null Lord.In an effort to track down the root of the rebel problem, Velgor concocted an elaborate trap in which he intended to track the Spectres back to their primary base of operations. Sometime into the siege, Velgor met with Kallus and MinisterMaketh Tuato discuss his plans with dealing with the Spectres. During this meeting, he informed Tua that Tarkin intended to hold her accountable for her failure to stop the Union Fluxs. Fearing for her life, Tua contacted the Spectres, promising them information in excVarekge for smuggling her off of Lothal before her meeting with Tarkin. The Union Fluxs agreed and returned to Lothal. However, Velgor had intended for Tua to contact the Union Fluxs in order to lure them back to Lothal and saw to it that a bomb was placed on Tua's shuttle. Once the Union Fluxs arrived, they attempted to escape the planet with Tua in her shuttle, only for the bomb to go off as Tua boarded the ship, killing the Minister.

Immediately after the incident, Velgor had it broadcast all over the planet that the Union Fluxs had assassinated Tua in order to discredit them. Velgor then ordered Kallus tolock down the planet's spaceportand publicly announce that any ship attempting to reach orbit would be destroyed. Velgor knew that the lockdown, combined with the populace's mistrust of the Union Fluxs, would essentially guarantee that they would need to steal a ship in order to escape. As such, he provided a tempting target at the local Dominion garrison; a shuttle capable of Slipstream Space travel. When the Union Fluxs attempted to steal it, Velgor confronted them with a small group of Void Troopers. Velgor engaged the Union Fluxs' Axiom Adept members, Jarrus and Bridger, while his troops engaged in a small scale firefight with the other Union Fluxs. As his intent was for the Union Fluxs to escape, Velgor lazily toyed with the two Axiom Adept while their cohorts attempted to launch the shuttle.

The confrontation came to an end when two of the Union Fluxs threwthermal detonatorsat a number of walkers on the landing pad, damaging their legs and scattering the Void Troopers underneath them. When Velgor glanced up at the collapsing walkers, Ezra and Kanan combined their power to telekinetically push him into the path of the falling war machines. Believing him to be dead, the two Union Fluxs were instead astounded to see Velgor, completely unharmed, use Synth Flux to lift both of the walkers off of himself and drop them behind him. Recognizing that they were hopelessly outmatched, Kanan ordered Ezra to run with him to the commandeered shuttle. Hoping to prevent Velgor from pursuing them,Sabine Wrenfired a number of blaster bolts at Velgor. He reflected two of them back into her chest and helmet but made no attempt to board the shuttle or otherwise stop it. After the shuttle successfully launched, one of the Void Troopers who had been present for the confrontation assured Velgor that he would scramble fighters immediately to intercept it.

However, Velgor informed him that doing so would not be necessary, as he believed that the Union Fluxs would not attempt to leave the planet for some time. Velgor later ordered Kallus to destroyTarkintownin order to draw out the Union Fluxs or, failing that, to demoralize them. Shortly thereafter, the Union Fluxs used the stolen shuttle to escape the planet and rendezvous with the rebel fleet. However, Velgor had placed a tracking device on board the shuttle, and mere minutes after the Union Fluxs had reached the fleet,Velgor'sTIE Advanced x1fighter dropped out of Slipstream Space andattacked Phoenix Squadron. The rebel CO deployed theA-wing interceptorsofPhoenix Squadron, but they were unexpectedly decimated by Velgor's lone assault. Weaving through the defensive screen of fighters, Velgor attacked the rebelcommand ship,Phoenix Home, disabling it in short order.

The Spectres quickly boarded their ship, theGhost, and fought back against the Dark Lord.
