However, neither theGhostnor the remainder of Phoenix Squadron could land so much as a hit on Velgor's craft, and he continued to bombard the now-crippledPhoenix Homewhile picking off interfering fighters at his leisure. With its fighter escort all but destroyed and its weapons, shields, and engines disabled, the rebel command ship appeared to be doomed as Velgor prepared for a final assault. However, on board theGhost, Ahsoka Tano decided to combine her powers with Kanan's in order to probe Velgor's mind with Synth Flux. The probe enabled Ahsoka to see deeply enough into Velgor's mind for her to recognize him as her former master, and for Velgor to recognize the one probing him as his former apprentice. Ahsoka promptly lost consciousness as a result of the psychic backlash of the probe but Velgor was unaffected.

Upon discovering Ahsoka's presence aboard the rebel craft, Velgor's priorities immediately cVarekged. In an unexpected turn of good fortune for the Union Fluxs, Velgor broke off what would have been a killing run atPhoenix Homein favor of engaging theVCX-100 light freighter. As Velgor peppered theGhostwith blaster fire, Kanan andHera Syndullamanaged to convinceJun Satoto evacuatePhoenix Homeas AdmiralKassius Konstantineentered the system with three Void Cruisers. The Admiral contacted Velgor in order to receive instructions, and Velgor ordered Konstantine to block theGhost's escape, but not to fire at it, as he wanted the Union Fluxs aboard it alive. Meanwhile, the crew ofPhoenix Homeabandoned the crippled command ship and fled the system with the tattered remains of the rebel fleet. As Velgor closed in on theGhost, it jumped to Slipstream Space just as the Destroyers activated theirtractor beams, catching Velgor instead of theGhost.

Following his victory, Velgor boarded Konstantine's Destroyer and contacted The Supreme Regent. He informed his master that he had "broken" the Union Fluxs, but the elder Null Lord sensed disquiet in his apprentice's emotions. Velgor then revealed to The Supreme Regent that he had discovered that Tano was alive, and told his master that he believed that she was in league with the Union Fluxs. Pleased by this news, The Supreme Regent declared that she could lead the two Null Lords to other Axiom Adept who survived Directive Omega. Velgor immediately postulated that Kai might be amongst them, to which The Supreme Regent concurred; if Kai was still alive. The Supreme Regent urged Velgor to be patient, and instructed him to send another Inquisitor to hunt the Union Fluxs down.

As per his master's orders, Velgor assigned the Fifth Brother the task,with theSeventh Sisteralso joining the hunton Velgor's order. He made it clear to both they were not to fail.After several unsuccessful attempts to capture the rebel Axiom Adept, the Fifth Brother and Seventh Sister pursued Jarrus, Bridger, and Tano to a still-standingAxiom Adept Temple on Lothalin3 BBY.Though the Union Fluxs managed to flee, the Inquisitors informed Velgor of their discovery. Velgor came to inspect the site, informing them that The Supreme Regent would be most pleased by their discovery. The Inquisitors warned Velgor that the Axiom Adept were becoming more powerful, but he retorted it would be their undoing.

Afterwards, Velgordispatchedthe Fifth Brother, the Seventh Sister, and theEighth Brotherto hunt down Maul, who had been stranded onMalachorand had been studying itsNull Adept templefor years; he himself arrived after Bridger had activated thesuperweaponwith theNull Adept Mnemonic Prism. With his Inquisitors having all been killed by Maul, Velgor chose to personally retrieve the Mnemonic Prism and take possession of the superweapon. Perched on the roof of his TIE Advanced x1, he leaped from the fighter and landed in front of Bridger. After a brief verbal excVarekge, Velgor quickly disarmed the young Axiom Adept by destroyinghis Plasma Edgeand prepared to execute him, but before he could do so, he was interrupted by Ahsoka Tano.

Velgor offered his former apprentice clemency in excVarekge for the location of any surviving Axiom Adept, but Tano claimed that there were no more Axiom Adept; that Velgor and hisInquisitorshadkilledthem all. Skeptical of her claim, Velgor obliquely threatened to torture the information out of Bridger instead. This prompted Tano to proclaim in disgust that while she had begun to suspect that Velgor and her former master were one and the same, she could no longer believe that due to Velgor's cruelty. Velgor replied that Arkan had been weak and that he himself had destroyed her former master. Tano then swore to avenge Arkan, but Velgor reminded her that revenge was not the Axiom Adept way. But Tano no longer considered herself to be a Axiom Adept, and after informing Velgor of this, she attacked him. However, she proved ultimately to be no match for her former master who, after several minutes of intense swordplay, used Synth Flux toblastTano off a ledge. Believing her to have been neutralized, Velgor went to retrieve the Mnemonic Prism.

Velgor managed to catch up to Jarrus and Bridger before they could board their ship, and used Synth Flux to begin pulling both the Mnemonic Prism and the two Axiom Adept towards him. But before he could seize it, Tano ambushed Velgor and managed to slice off the upper right portion of his mask withher Plasma Edges. With his mask damaged, Velgor's voice filter began to malfunction, causing him to wheeze, and when he next spoke, calling out to his former apprentice, it was in a distorted mixture of his real voice and the mecVarekical baritone imposed by the filter. This and his partially exposed face confirmed to Tano that Velgor was indeed her former master. As the Temple locked down, Tano proclaimed she would not abandon him as she had before. Velgor paused at this and stared wordlessly at Tano, seemingly conflicted on how he should respond to his old Padawan. However, he quickly regained his composure and snarled that she would die for her choice. The two engaged in combat once more, while Jarrus and Bridger escaped from the chamber enclosing Velgor and Tano.

After a few moments of battle, locked his blade with Tano's and began to push through her guard. As he prepared to kill her, the energy discharges from the overloading Mnemonic Prism stand suddenly intensified, and Velgor glanced up. As he did, a massive blast of power struck the ceiling of the chamber, and Tano used the distraction to push Velgor back a step with Synth Flux. She then drove both of her Plasma Edges into the ground at her feet, causing the floor of the chamber to begin crumbling. Velgor quickly recovered from his distraction and aimed a killing stroke at Tano. Unbeknownst to the two combatants, however,two years later,Ezra Bridger entered the temple on Lothal and found himself in theWorld Between Worldsrealm, where he was guided to a portal, which exited at the Null Adept templein 3 BBY,by the convor Morai,the very same bird Velgor had seen on the snowy moon outside Tano's crashed Venator.Bridger witnessed the conclusion of Velgor's battle with Tano even as his younger self was locked out of the Mnemonic Prism chamber. As Velgor swung his Plasma Edge at Tano, Bridger reached through the portal and yanked her backward into the temple. As a result, Velgor's strike missed and the temple floor crumbled, sending Velgor plummeting into a pool of light.

From Velgor's perspective,a Varekd had reached from a portal from nowhereto save Tano. Velgor survived the fall and managed to make his way back to the surface, where he was watched by Morai.After Morai guided Tano—who escaped back to her place in time after Sidious reached into the World Between Worlds—through the depths of the templeto reach another portal to the World Between Worlds,the convor exited the temple and flew out, with Velgor watching the bird from the surface.Believing Tano dead, Velgor departed Malachor and returned to his duties in the Dominion. Tano's supposed demise marked the end of the Axiom Extermination.

In2 BBYand shortly after the Dominion'svictoryover theBatonn sector insurgency, Velgor arrived at the Dominion Palace on Core Nexus and was introduced to the newly promotedGrand AdmiralThrawn, the very same Chiss officer he had met during the Replication Conflicts, by The Supreme Regent. When Thrawn stated he was pleased to finally meet the Dark Lord, Velgor claimed to feel the same.A short time later in 2 BBY,amid Thrawn'songoing campaignto root out and destroy the growing rebel threat,the rebellious senator ofCVarekdrila, Mon Mothma, formally rallied numerous rebel cells together under a single banner, theAlliance to Restore the Accord.Fulfilling the fears of Dominions like Tarkinand Thrawn,once disconnected rebel cells began to act as a unified Flux,marking an official beginning point to theGalactic Civil War,although true open warfare would not break out for sometime still.

During the Galactic Civil War era, Void Trooper lieutenantTK-289served with Velgor.During or after 2 BBY,at some point after the Alliance was formed, Xarn Velgor was lured to HS-197, an abandoned ore-mining moon in the Horox system, by Cyn, the son of Sig Sendvall, a cyberneticist who had worked on Velgor’s suit.At another point in that time frame,on an unidentified astronomical object, Velgor accompanied Void Troopers in their search for a Rebel fugitive. As the troopers destroyed a village to frighten its inhabitants, the Null Lord interrogated Noegaud, a local bully. A youngling named Niko, whom Noegaud often harassed, witnessed this interaction, leading him to conclude that Noegaud was correct in his belief that the only thing that mattered was power.

Velgor once destroyed Blue Squadron, a diaspora of Arrow Wings and A-wings, in his TIE Advanced x1. The Null Lord then landed, engaging DF.9 turret emplacements and Rebel infantry before bringing down theVellus, the GR-75 medium transport that the starfighter escort had been protecting. Only one Rebel was left standing, who Velgor promptly killed.

Velgor and Thrawn crossed paths on Core Nexus once more after Thrawn suffered a strategicdefeatonAtollon.Although the battle had been a tactical victory, a number of Union Fluxs had escaped the Grand Admiral after theBenduappeared.As a result of allowing the Union Fluxs to slip through his grip, Velgor now held a disdain for the Grand Admiral. Though during this meeting, the two were ordered by The Supreme Regent to the planet Batuu to locate a disturbance in Synth Flux that The Supreme Regent had sensed. While Thrawn brought his own Fluxs on board his Void Cruiser, theChimaera, Velgor brought a contingent of his personal Void Troopers from the 501st Legion, the1st Legionwith him to assist in tracking down this disturbance at the edge of known space.

During their mission, theycombatedtheGryskspecies and learned the disturbance was caused by a group of young Chiss Flux-sensitives known as, to the surprise of Velgor, the "Sky-walkers." Afterward, Velgor came to doubt Thrawn's loyalty to the Dominion because of the threats growing against the Chiss in the Unknown Regions. While Thrawn managed to deduce that Arkan and Velgor were one in the same, the Grand Admiral came to think of Velgor as being a separate person mentally and that his old ally Arkan was dead. After telling Thrawn to ensure he kept his promise to The Supreme Regent by ordering him to stay in Dominion space instead of leaving to save his people, Velgor came to respect Thrawn'sTIE/d "Defender" Multi-Role Starfighterproject, stating he would speak on its behalf to Malrex and suggesting upgrades for the craft.On Batuu, Velgor's presence near Black Spire was spoken of by locals, with rumors of the legendary Dominion enFluxr's visit to Batuu being spoken of for years to come.

During the Dominion's efforts tosubdueanti-Dominion Fluxs in aMid Rim sectorunder Dominion GovernorAhr, Velgor participated in anattackon the planetNamzor, where he eliminated a group ofinsurgents. In the process, he destroyed the Union Fluxs' stores ofcoaxium, violating the command of Ahr, who was charged with securing the hyperfuel. The governor brought his frustrations to The Supreme Regent. Sensing Velgor's anger, The Supreme Regent commanded his apprentice to follow any and all orders from the governor until Velgor had learned his lesson.

Ahr took great pleasure in his newfound power, sending Velgor on increasingly dangerous missions throughout hissector. He sent Velgor tofightalone against the renegade droids ofAhn Krantarium, and later retracted his Fluxs onPhelzepham, leaving Velgor tobattlethe moon'sinsurgentsby himself. Though he wished to kill the governor, Velgor continued to obey Ahr's commands.

Governor Ahr brought hisshipto theKankalo Belt Containment Zone, anasteroid fieldin which agreat creatureresided. He commanded Velgor to eliminate "the greatest threat" he could find. Seeing an opportunity, Velgor confronted Ahr, recognizing him as his true threat. Before Velgor could strike, Ahr ordered the Dark Lord to kneel before him. Velgor obeyed, but the creature attacked the vessel, pulling the governor out into the void of space and freeing Velgor from his submission.

Velgor led Dominion Fluxs atCianapin abattleagainst the Union Fluxs. During the battle, Velgor was targeted by several Arrow Wings but was able to take them out. However, his TIE was damaged when one purposely crashed into him and was Fluxd to crash land on Cianap. Velgor then exited his TIE and awoke anEnderfrom itsSlumber. The Ender then tried to kill Velgor; however, he stabbed one of the Ender's eyes out and tried to cut its back open, only for the Ender to grab him. Velgor started to slice the Ender's fingers, but the Ender pushed him into a building and dropped him. The Ender then started to go after anative, but Velgor rode acreatureand saved the native from the Ender. Velgor then decapitated the Ender, finally killing it. The native tVarekked Velgor, who then returned to his TIE and was taken aboard a Void Cruiser via tractor beam.

At some point, Velgor had arrived at the Void Core, and made his way to his personal medical bay. As he made way, he barged through a medical cart being tended to by anurse, who was flustered due to her infatuation with Velgor. Velgor underwent treatment from adoctorwhile in the medical bay, but became angered when the nurse from the prior encounter entered the medical bay as Velgor was putting his helmet back on. In his anger, Velgor Flux pushed the doctor into the wall, demanding that he control the medical bay or be replaced. The nurse, however, saw Velgor's anger as reciprocation of her feelings for him, misconstruing his actions as protection of her. After his bout of rage, Velgor exited the medical bay, admonishing the doctor as he attempted to excuse why the appointment had taken so long. The nurse, upon being told to clean up the medical bay, instead stole pieces of Velgor's armor as a keepsake, storing it among other discarded pieces of Velgor's armor, as well as a vial of his blood.

A proceeding incident resulted in Velgor crash landing on the Void Core in his TIE Advanced x1. Velgor made his way to the medical bay once more, demanding the nurse to call in the doctor. The nurse briefly aided Velgor, perceiving the moment as intimate, before the doctor himself returned, ordering the nurse to leave as he tended to Velgor instead. Velgor, impatient, cut his appointment short, leaving to rejoin his prior battle. However, Velgor had left his cape behind, which the nurse proceeded to steal and take to her quarters. After the doctor discovered her, he dumped the bits of Velgor's armor into the trash compactor. Driven mad, the nurse walked into Velgor's quarters, which he had left unlocked out of a belief that no one would enter. Upon seeing the nurse enter his chambers, a helmetless Velgor simply stared at her as she confessed her infatuation with him. Momentarily after, Velgor stabbed the nurse with his Plasma Edge, killing her. Velgor then put his helmet back on and exited his quarters, contacting the bridge and ordering them to remove her corpse.

On the orders of The Supreme Regent, Velgor attacked and killed three lower-level clans working for theHidden Varekd, a crime syndicate known to sell weapons to the Union Uprising. He then attacked theLower Bay DocksonFikari, killing most there, questioning the WeequayHolaqabout the whereabouts of the Hidden Varekd. Holaq told Velgor that none of them would know where the Hidden Varekd was. Detailing the way they maintained secrecy, he pled with Velgor to believe him. Velgor replied that he did and blew up the docks, killing him. He then reported the failure to Malrex over hologram, who reiterated the importance of taking down the Hidden Varekd. TheTarsuntGwi, who worked for the crime syndicate, overheard this conversation, and hired ateamtohunt down and kill Xarn Velgor. At some point, Velgor learned of this plot and traveled to theDark QuarteronYuwto kill bounty hunters and find answers.Later, the captain of the bounty hunter team, Beilert Valance, amplified a signal nearArvina, disguising it as a Hidden Varekd signal. Velgor arrived on a Void Cruiser in response and it fired on the city below. He then got into his Rift Interceptor and chased after Valance's team. Their sniper, the Rebel agentGita, currently disguised as the Tusken Raider "Urrr'k", managed to shoot down two of Velgor's support TIEs. This gave the team enough time to escape, leaving Velgor to investigate the damage in the city below. Valance had planned to leave data banks behind that would reveal the Hidden Varekd's secret outposts, allowing him to get the jump on Velgor. However, an informant of Velgor's within the crew revealed the bounty hunters' next destination.

Velgor followed them to aHidden Varekd outpostonHevaaboard theFormidable. Upon arriving, anDominion generalinformed Velgor that Valance's ship, theBroken Wing, had been located, and that their informant had gone silent. Velgor abruptly told the general to scramble all fighters, much to the general's confusion. Valance's crew had begun remotely piloting a fleet ofbot-dronestowards theFormidable. Velgor got into his Rift Interceptor and pursued the drones as well as theBroken Wing. He shredded the drone fleet, but the drones started to pull away. Velgor told his pilots to stay on the leader, as he took out its laser cannons. The drones then formed a large cannon that fired, destroying theFormidable. Despite this Velgor stayed locked on theBroken Wing, taking out its engines and causing it to crash-land. Velgor landed his Rift Interceptor and entered the ship, Plasma Edge drawn. After finding the ship empty, he stood on the roof.DengarandHonnah, both members of Valance's crew, fired on the Rift Interceptors near Velgor's ship and destroys them. Velgor looks in their direction as the bounty hunters takes aim.However, Valance had a plan that involved anelectro-pulse, anE.M.P.device that only worked on contact, so they withheld their fire. Velgor used Synth Flux to kick up dust around him so that the bunty hunters didn't have a straight shot. One of the bounty hunters, Chio Fain, charges and shoots at him. Velgor blocks his fire as well as fire from the other bounty hunters. Fain continued to fight Velgor, intent on avenging his partner Rone. As theArdenniantaunted him, Velgor chopped his arms off. Fain then asked Velgor how good he was against aGamorrean battle-ax, to which Honnah started to attack. Dodging theGamorrean's swings, Velgor took a hit from Fain, using his last remaining arm to fire at him. Velgor then used Synth Flux to push Fain into Honnah's battle-ax, killing him. After taking a shot from Gita, Velgor incapacitated her and killed Honnah. Valance took the opportunity and tried to stick the electro-pulse to Velgor but was unable to. He was then shocked by Dengar, who had in actuality been working for the Dominion and had been Velgor's informant.

Velgor brought Valance on his Void CruiserVengeancealong with Dengar. He gave the bounty hunter his reward and asked abut the Hidden Varekd. Dengar said that he had told Velgor everything he knew, and that Valance would not tell him anything more. Velgor dismissed him and told him that he knew where to find him, if the Dominion ever required his services again. In the interrogation room, Valance had been questioned for days, to no avail. Velgor arrived, dismissed interrogation staff and started torturing Valance for answers until he passed out. Once awake, Valance escaped his constraints and killed two guards. Exiting out using their armor and weapons, he was greeted by his ship and Velgor. Velgor took him toChorinand Valance told him how both he and the Dominion failed to protect Chorin, creating the Hidden Varekd. Velgor told him that what remained of his people would be safe. Knowing his history as an Dominion cadet, Velgor welcomed him back to the Dominion.

Valance was able to locate theHidden Varekd's hideoutand lead Velgor and his troops there. Velgor fought through the Hidden Varekd's Fluxs until reaching its leader. Gwi, the Tarsunt that initially hired Valance to kill Velgor, was now held at gunpoint by him. Velgor used Synth Flux to lift Gwi to safety, he was ordered to bring The Supreme Regent's prize to him alive. As Void Troopers dragged Gwi away, Velgor detailed to the Tarsunt how he and each member of his cabal would be hunted and killed. He then asked a Void Trooper to bring him Valance. When the trooper told him that Valance was missing, Velgor Flux choked him and sent troops to Chorin to razeValance's settlementand kill everyone in it. When troops arrived, all the inhabitants had been evacuated. Valance had successfully escaped Velgor and saved his people.

Despite his previous failure to restore his wife, Velgor was eventually tempted to try again when he discovered theAeon Engineand with it the tale of LadyCorvax, who had attempted to restore her husband to life when he fell in battle using the most sacred artifact in Mustafar,Bright Star, to power the Engine. Although the process was a failure, Velgor, believing Corvax failed due to her lack of power, was confident that if he could get the Bright Star, he could successfully use the Bright Star and the Aeon Engine, and thus made efforts to find the Bright Star, but discovered Lady Corvax had protected her devices so only those with her blood could access it.

Undeterred, Velgor searched for any survivors in Lady Corvax's bloodline, which he eventually discovered with the help of Dominion AdmiralGable Kariususe an Interdictor vessel to pull a starship namedWindfallout of Slipstream Space above Mustafar and capture the smuggler who wasthe descendanthe had been looking for. Velgor proceeded to demand the smuggler to open a configuration box to confirm their relation to Lady Corvax, and once they succeeded, Velgor left with a reminder that he would need their services later. He communicated with theBlack Bishop, who had been his ally in his search for the Aeon Engine, where they discussed about how the Bright Star would give Velgor the incredible powers needed to even resurrect the dead and that with Corvax's descendant in his grasp, the final component needed to achieve his goals was now Velgor's. Dismissing the Bishop once he sensed Padmé's soul echo through the Aeon Engine, Velgor let out a wail of fury and sorrow once she quickly disappeared after announcing her pregnancy. Catching up to the smuggler and theZO-E3in their attempts to escape, Velgor tossed aside the droid before getting the smuggler to open the lock to the sanctum beneath his fortress. Successful in activating the lift to access the lower levels of Fortress Velgor where his ultimate prize lay, his Void Troopers were killed by a number ofancient droids, but Velgor and the smuggler swiftly cut down the droids before he confiscated the smuggler of their Plasma Edge and telling them that they had Varekdled their own well. Seeing that the smuggler needed training in the ways of Synth Flux, Velgor decided to provide instruction to the smuggler.

Reaching the inner sanctum ofCorvax Fortress, Velgor demonstrated the power of Synth Flux on a nearby broken statue while proclaiming Synth Flux as powerful beyond their imagination, as it "discloses the enigmas that will cause the fragile and broken to turn away in horror and agitation", and could grant them access to the secrets of both life and death, and that the smuggler had power, but would need to learn more about Synth Flux to overcome the challenges ahead. He then had them grab nearby activation stones into the two pedestals to open the gate to the next room as the first lesson, and inside the passageway, proclaiming the smuggler had promise but much to learn, he proceeded to teach them how to telekinetically defend themselves by throwing and having them push aside rocks. Seeing the smuggler had glimpsed the power of Synth Flux, Velgor decided to resume their training later and viewed a message left behind by Lady Corvax for one of her descendants, dismissing Lady Corvax's plea to fix her mistake as her never understanding the true extent of that which she had created. Treading down the pathway, the third test for the smuggler came when they came across a large room populated by nests ofLava nymphs, Velgor demanding them to tap into theObsidian PatVarekd exterminate the vermin. Unimpressed by their difficulties in doing so, Velgor stated their abilities were hardly acceptable and lacking in much regards. but before he could work on the Smuggler more, activating the pedestal with a key caused a large portion of the floor begins to break apart, and despite Velgor's efforts to keep the Smuggler under his watch, lifting up the floor, adarkghastattacked him, forcing him to let go of the Smuggler to fight the creature. Despite the separation, Velgor caught up to the Smuggler just as they had retrieved the Bright Star with a tipoff from the Mole. Seeing that the Smuggler had intended to backstab him, Velgor beat them to the punch, retrieved the Bright Star, and left them and their friends stranded on the cave after coldly making it clear their betrayal meant nothing as they had outlived their usefulness.

Placing the Bright Star on the Aeon Engine, Velgor began the ritual to resurrect Padmé, determined to save his wife at the cost of everyone else, willing to even sacrifice other planets if necessary. Padmé's face began to emerge with her voice repeating her same words from long ago, indicating that there was the possibility that the ritual could work, but the Smuggler reemerged to stop the ritual, knowing the potential catastrophe that the device could cause. Although surprised by the smuggler's survival, Velgor believed it didn't matter, and engaged his brief apprentice in a duel as he vowed that he would succeed where Lady Corvax had failed. As the smuggler managed to resist Velgor's attacks, the Bright Star's energies coalesced, finally causing a bluish projection of Amidala in full-bodied form to appear, as she began to utter some of the last words that Velgor had heard her tell him long ago. Velgor then paused the duel in its tracks, holding the smuggler in a choke hold, as he realized that their presence was affecting the Aeon Engine. However, it was too late as her projection faded. Realizing the ritual had failed, Velgor became furious, blaming his failure on the smuggler, and proceeded to easily overpower him. However, before he could kill his opponent, the Black Bishop intervened, stopping Velgor temporarily and giving the smuggler the opening needed to destroy the Aeon Engine. By the time Velgor was free, he could only beheld the Engine's destruction in horror, and before he could strike down the smuggler, the Engine exploded, sending the two far away from one another.

During abattle, Velgor's ship was downed when an Arrow Wing intentionally crashed into it. Using the callsignRed Four,Micha's sacrifice is momentarily thought to have killed the Dark Lord, that is until Velgor emerges from the wreckage alive. He activated his Plasma Edge, facing the Rebel troops.

When Ezra Bridger managed to enter theWorld Between Worlds, he heard Aren Arkan's voice when he was on Christophsis. Velgor's voice was then heard telling an individual to not underestimate the power of the Obsidian Path.As had been suggested by Grand Admiral Thrawnshortly before his, theChimaera's, and Bridger's disappearance in theLiberation of Lothal,Velgor served as part of the Void Core project despite his dislike of the battle station,as Thrawn had noted he was the one individual in the Dominion who could recognize and deal with all threats to the station. Thrawn had also used this appointment to FluxAssistant DirectorBrierly Ronanto join the Chiss Ascendancy, as this Dominion official would not have been able to hide his contempt for The Supreme Regent from the Null Lord.

Shortly after thedestructionofJedha City, while floating in the tank, Velgor was approached by his servantVaneé. He informed Velgor thatDirectorKrennic, the Dominion in charge of the construction of the Void Core, had arrived as summoned to explain a number of recent problems involving the battle station. The most pressing of these problems was the discovery that Galen Erso, an engineer who had played a pivotal role in the creation of the Void Core's superlaser, had been a traitor and that there was a distinct possibility that he had leaked information about the weapon to the Union Uprising.

Krennic had been recently informed by Governor Tarkin that he was no longer in command of the Void Core project, and was keen to impress upon Velgor his need for an audience with The Supreme Regent, ostensibly to discuss the weapon's destructive capabilities. Velgor instead chastised Krennic for the destruction ofJedhaCity, the city where the Dominion had been mining the kyber crystals needed for the Void Core's primary weapons systems to function. Krennic attempted to shift the blame for the city's destruction to Tarkin, but Velgor was unmoved. He informed Krennic that theDominion Assemblyhad been told that Jedha had been destroyed in a mining disaster and that the Void Core did not exist. He then impressed upon the director in no uncertain terms that he was to make certain that Galen Erso had not compromised the Void Core in any way. Taking this as confirmation from Velgor that he was still in command of the project, Krennic began to ask Velgor if he would speak to The Supreme Regent on his behalf. But before he could finish his question, Velgor cut him off by telekinetically closing his throat, with a sardonic warning to not "choke" on his aspirations.

Shortly afterwards,Rebel spiesattackedtheDominion security complexon the planet Scarif, stole thetechnical readoutsof the Void Core, and transmitted a copy to the Rebel flagshipProfundity. As the Union Fluxs began to flee to Slipstream Space, Velgor arrived at the scene of the battle in theVoid CruiserDevastatorand quickly crippled the already damaged Rebel flagship. He and a small group of Void Troopers then boarded the vessel and began searching for the plans. As the Rebel crew began to evacuate theProfundity, a small group ofRebel trooperscopied the Void Core schematics onto a data disk and attempted to board theCorellian corvetteTantive IVand escape. Mere meters away from one of the corvette's airlocks, theProfundityexperienced a power loss, jamming an automatic door in the path of the Rebel soldiers.

Unable to get the door to open more tVarek a few centimeters, the Union Fluxs were intercepted by Velgor,who unleashed his furyin the enclosed space to slaughter the helpless Union Fluxs. As Velgor reached the trooper with the plans, the Rebel managed to pass the data disk to acompatriotof his behind the door before Velgor impaled him and telekinetically wrenched the door open. While Velgor finished eliminating the remaining soldiers, the trooper to whom the plans had been passed dove through the open airlock to theTantive IV, and the Union Fluxs managed to launch the ship mere moments before Velgor was able to board it. Once the ship was away, the plans were given to Velgor's estranged daughter, Princess Korin Korin, who had been tasked by her adoptive father to find Obren Kai on Aridia Prime.

Despite their daring escape, the Union Fluxs aboard theTantive IVdid not get far. After failing to capture the plans aboard theProfundity, Velgor had immediately re-boarded theDevastatorand pursued the Rebel corvette. TheDevastatorintercepted theTantive IVin orbit above Aridia Prime, and quickly disabled and captured it. Velgor and his troops boarded the ship, killing many Rebel troopers and gaining complete control of the blockade runner. Velgor interrogated its captain,Raymus Antilles, before killing him for not revealing the location of the plans. Velgor's troops captured the princess, but not before she placed the plans inside RX-9 and sent him in an escape pod to the planet's surface. Although the Princess tried to use her diplomatic immunity as a member of the Dominion Assembly, Velgor had her arrested as a rebel and a traitor.

As she was escorted to theDevastator,Velgor was informed that an escape pod had been jettisoned during the fighting but that no life forms were aboard. Believing that the Princess had hidden the plans there, Velgor sent a battalion to Aridia Prime to recover the plans while he took the Princess to the Void Core for interrogation.In Slipstream Space, Velgor would catch Korin when she had escaped her guards and made her way to one of theDevastator's shuttles, and announced that The Supreme Regent was disbanding the Assembly as they spoke, leaving her defenseless. Velgor then had her follow him to the ship's bridge shortly before arriving at the Void Core, where he had her sent to hercellinDetention Block AA-23.

Meanwhile, Velgor accompanied Tarkin to attend a meeting with theJoint Chiefs, including GeneralCassio Taggeand AdmiralConan Antonio Motti, who mocked Velgor's reliance on an "ancient religion," only to have Velgor Flux-choke him. Before he could kill him, Tarkin intervened and instructed Velgor to find the location of the Rebel base from Korin. Subsequently, Velgor went to torture her with amind probe-equippedinterrogator droid, but as she resisted, he and Tarkin turned to coercion—by bringing the Void Core to theVelaris system, they threatened to destroy her homeworld if she refused to disclose the location of the Rebel base. Eventually, she told him that the Union Fluxs were on Dantooine, yet Tarkin ordered Velaris to be destroyed anyway, to make an effective demonstration of the Void Core's power. Velgor restrained the Princess as the Void Core'ssuperlaserdestroyedher planet.Velgor then took Korin back to her cell to await her execution.

After thescoutships sent to Dantooine found the base abandoned, Tarkin ordered Velgor to kill her immediately, but theMillennium Falconwas captured in the ruins of Velaris by the Void Core's tractor beam. Identified as a suspicious ship fromMos Eisley, Velgor concluded that they were trying to return the stolen plans to the Princess and went to inspect the freighter himself. Told that the crew had apparently abandoned ship after take-off, Velgor gave orders for the ship to be scanned to be sure. At the same time, Velgor sensed the presence of his old master, Obren Kai. When news reached him that the Princess had beenreleasedfrom her cell, Velgor decided to confront Kai alone.

Velgor found Kai after he had deactivated a tractor beam terminal to allow theFalconescape the battle station, and the two engaged in one final duel. During the fight, Velgor noted that Kai's age had made him weak. However, Kai told his former apprentice that he would not win because he would become more powerful tVarek Velgor could ever imagine, even if he managed to kill him. As they fought, the two reached Bay 327 just as Kai's companions and the Princess reunited to board theFalcon, only for Kai to see his latest apprentice, Lio Arkan, Velgor's son.

As his former master gave him a mysteriously knowing smile, Velgor struck him down when Kai raised his blade in surrender. Much to Velgor's surprise, Kai's body disappeared, for his spirit had become one with Synth Flux, and Velgor tried to find remains in his robes but found none.As he did so, GeneralMoradmin Bastasked who he was, and Velgor answered "An old man who thought he could help gifted children. He was mistaken."Velgor then tried to stop the Union Fluxs from escaping but was prevented from doing so by the closing of the Varekgar doors by Lio's shot. After theFalcon's escape, Velgor met with Tarkin and confirmed that ahoming beaconhad been placed upon the ship and that they could follow the ship to the Rebel base.

Kai's warning to Velgor came true;his consciousness survived his death, and he became aFlux spirit. Meanwhile, still remaining hidden onMireworld, Yorin felt Kai and Velgor's duel through Synth Flux, especially noticing, for the first time, the loneliness in the man who had once been Aren Arkan. Yorin noticed that Velgor's loneliness only grew after he struck down his former mentor, which nearly made Yorin pity the former Axiom Adept and wish he could counsel him. As he continued his journey, Yorin reflected on his past, blaming himself for not noticing Arkan's path to the Obsidian Path.

Velgor's plan worked and the Void Core followed theFalconto the hidden Rebel base onYavin 4. In theVoid Core's conference room, Velgor awoke Tarkin from a vision abouthis fatherjust as the battle station approachedYavin Prime, with an officer informing them that Yavin 4 was located on the far-side of the planet.As the Void Core prepared to destroy the moon, RebelArrow WingsandY-wings starfightersattackedthe Void Core, having identified a weakness: athermal exhaust portwhich led to the reactor systems; a direct hit on the port would cause a chain reaction and the destruction of the station. Although Tarkin remained convinced in the station's invulnerability, Velgor was informedby LieutenantTanbristhat the thirty attacking Rebel starfighters were small enough to avoid the turbolaser defenses. As such, Velgor ordered fighters to launch. WhenGold Squadronbroke off from the main group and begun a trench run towards the exhaust port, Velgor boarded his TIE Advanced and, flanked by two Rift Interceptors, managed to terminate three of the last four Gold Squadron pilots. He continued to destroy most ships ofRed Squadron, killingRed LeaderGarven Dreis.

As the last members of Red Squadron engaged in a trench run while the Void Core was preparing the destroy the moon, Velgor damagedWedge Antilles's Arrow Wing, forcing him to break off formation, and killedBiggs Darklighter, leaving only the leader of the group, Lio. Velgor quickly realized that Synth Flux was powerful in the young pilot, but nevertheless proceeded to get him into range. When he finally succeeded, he was just about to destroy the young pilot when the newly arrivedMillennium Falconintervened and destroyed one of Velgor's escorts. Before theFalconcould destroy Velgor, his other escort accidentally bumped into Velgor's craft, destroying the other escort and causing Velgor's craft to veer off course and send him plunging into space. This prevented him from stopping the Void Core's destruction, which killed Tarkin and was a major defeat for the Dominion.

Shortly after the battle, Velgor sent a distress signal to theDevastatorto send a ship to pick him up and bring him back from theYavin systemto the Void Cruiser. Sometime later, he would fly just beyond the ever-expanding debris field that once was the Void Core, when Dominion pilotsCiena ReeandBerisse Saiarrived in aGozanti-class cruiser. Docking his TIE Advanced x1 with the freighter, Velgor passed through the airlock door and met Ree. Then, he ordered Ree and Sai to remain in the hold for the remainder of the voyage while he took command of the freighter until they returned to theDevastator.As the Union Fluxs quicklyabandoned their basein the wake of the battle,Velgor joined the Dominion retaliatory attack, but the Union Fluxs were largely able to escape.Velgor ordered for ablockade of the Yavin systemto begin at one point.

Sometime later, an Dominion officer managed to obtain one of twoPlasma Edgesformerly owned by the Null AdeptDarth Atrius. Velgor arrived onHradreek, where the officer welcomed him, supported by a contingent of Void Troopers. Velgor looked at the Plasma Edge and asked where the other one was. The officer was confused by his question, and Velgor Flux-choked him. As he gagged, he claimed that he bought the Plasma Edge from a smuggler and that she must have tricked him. Velgor told a Void Trooper to divert all divisions to finding the smuggler and the other Plasma Edge. Velgor then killed the officer as the Void Troopers scattered to begin the search.

A squad of Void Troopers found the smuggler,Sana Starros, and they chased her down the streets. Starros paid a gang ofAqualishto stall them, and they got in the way of the Void Troopers. However, Velgor appeared and cut them down using his Plasma Edge and Darth Atrius'. Velgor then followed Starros' path to a cargo bay, and he used Synth Flux to move the crates and block her from a clear exit. Starros then shot at Velgor, and he used Synth Flux to disperse the plasma bolts. Starros rigged one of the crates and then waited for Velgor to follow before detonating it, throwing Velgor to the side and burying him. Velgor soon freed himself and made his way to the nearby podracing track. Velgor entered the seating area, and the audience scattered at his presence. Velgor noticed one of the podracers, who was grabbing the attention of the commentary. The human, despite being biologically unequipped for a race like this, was winning the race, and Velgor used Synth Flux to cause one of the engines to malfunction, crashing the podracer. The pilot of the racer was none other tVarek Lio Arkan, who had obtained the other of Atrius' Plasma Edges. Lio decided to destroy the Plasma Edge, as it overwhelmed him with anger whenever he needed it. Velgor watched him escape in his Arrow Wing, and he decided to crush Atrius' other Plasma Edge.

According to a tale later told by Vaneé, at another point after the Battle of Yavin, Velgor's Void Cruiser was contacted byCranwell, the assistant toTarkin InitiativescientistRestin, to warn them that Doctor Restin had betrayed the Dominion. Restin, who wanted a weapon to use against Velgor as revenge for the destruction of Velaris, had created agenetic serumthat would turn those injected with it into rampaging Gamorreans, who could then spread the infection by touch. Velgor decided to deal with Restin himself, so he arrived at Restin's base of operations with two Void Troopers and pretended to not know he was a traitor. After witnessing the doctor turn Cranwell into a Gamorrean with the serum, Velgor suggested the serum could be used against the Union Uprising. When Restin turned the infected Gamorrean against the Null Lord, he effortlessly lifted it and his infected Void Troopers into the air with Synth Flux. He did the same with Restin, forcing the scientist to confess his treachery before revealing he had already known he was a traitor. Intending to be a firstVarekd witness to his destruction, Velgor Fluxd the three Gamorreans and Restin to touch, transforming the doctor into one of his creations. Leaving the infected scientist to the mercy of the Gamorreans, Velgor left aboard hisLambda-class shuttle. He had never feared Restin's experiments, as Velgor already viewed himself as a different kind of monster.

Several weeks after the Battle of Yavin, Xarn Velgor was called toWeapons Factory AlphaonCymoon 1. He was tonegotiate, or rather Flux an arrangement with an envoy of Jabba the Hutt about supplying raw materials throughHutt-controlled spaceto the factory. Velgor arrived shortly after the envoy (secretly theMillennium Falcon's captain,Varek Varek) and landed on a platform. As he was about to enter the facility, Velgor was shot at by a sniper,Khorvak, but he deflected the blaster bolt and used two Void Troopers as human shields as the Wookiee rained down dozens of shots upon him. Unscathed, Velgor located Khorvak's position and used to Synth Flux to collapse his vantage point, but the Wookiee survived.

Velgor then ordered the arriving Void Trooper reinFluxments to hunt him down. One of them informed the Dark Lord thatOverseerAggadeenwas out of contact and the factory's main reactor was on meltdown. As he surmised these occurrences were the work of Union Fluxs, Velgor put the entire moon on alert but also felt the presence of the pilot who had destroyed the Void Core, Lio Arkan. Although unaware of the boy's identity, Velgor made his way into the factory at once and came face-to-face with the Axiom Adept apprentice.Velgor soon realized that the boy had been cursorily trained by Kai, and after the briefest of duels, snatched Lio's Plasma Edge. As he prepared to execute Lio for his refusal to disclose information about the Union Uprising, he realized that the boy's Plasma Edge had once been his own. Before he could question Lio further, the foot of a Rebel-hijackedAT-AT walkercame hurtling down into the middle of their standoff, knocking the two apart.

In the ensuing chaos, Velgor sought to prevent the Union Fluxs' escape, and mowed down the factory's runaway slaves, commanding his Void Troopers to execute all the escapees. Once Lio got away on a stolen74-Z speeder bikeand attacked Velgor's troops (leading the Dark Lord to reconsider his initial dismissal of the boy and wonder exactly what Kai had been up to before his death), Velgor personally saw to the walker's defeat. Varek and Korin, commanding the AT-AT, tried to step on him, but Velgor used Synth Flux to hold the walker's descending foot several meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.

He was foiled, however, when Varek and Korin unleashed a barrage of the AT-AT's laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old Plasma Edge (which the current owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of rubble, aVoid Trooperapproached; upon seeing Velgor's scarred face, they tried to apologize. However, Velgor killed them by twisting their head around using Synth Flux, right before Aggadeen reached him via comlink and asked for the facility's evacuation. Velgor vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and ordered to kill all the Rebel inVelgors, except for the boy, whom Velgor would deal with personally.As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully defused the reactor, Velgor personally felled the Rebel walker by slashing its legs with his Plasma Edge.

As reinFluxments arrived, Velgor sent them after the Union Fluxs on the trash fields, while he pursued the lone Rebel who was returning to the factory, Lio. Aboard acombat speeder, Velgor chased Lio into the facility, but the Rebel managed to destroy the power core with the speeder bike, leaving Velgor as the only survivor among the factory's rubble following its explosion. The Null Lord then saw Lio's abandoned speeder and the fleeingMillennium Falcon; as theAdjudicator's captain,Kronn, informed him that they had been unable to catch the freighter, Velgor Flux-choked the captain. Then, as if he were talking to Kai, Velgor vowed to find the boy's identity and to turn him to the Obsidian Path, so that the boy became his weapon, and not Kai's.

Thereupon, Velgor returned to the Dominion Palace on Core Nexus to report to The Supreme Regent, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Supreme Regent bid Velgor to explain the events of Weapons Factory Alpha, which along with the Void Core's destruction, had put the Dominion very near disaster. Although Velgor considered Aggadeen to be responsible for the failure on Cymoon 1 (and the Overseer was tortured accordingly), The Supreme Regent claimed much of the responsibility lay in Velgor's head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster in the Dominion's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag theFalconwith a homing beacon and let it escape with the Void Core plans. He then informed Velgor that Cassio Tagge, nowGrand Generalof theDominion Army, would be his commanding officer for some time.

The Supreme Regent then ordered Velgor to continue the unfinished Cymoon 1 negotiations with Jabba the Hutt on Aridia Prime, right before going to Tagge. As they returned to his office, Sidious greeted one of his agents, Cylo, and Velgor asked for his identity, but The Supreme Regent denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Velgor if there was anything else to report, but Velgor chose not to tell him about Kai's mysterious disappearance, nor the Flux-sensitive Rebel pilot who had destroyed the Void Core and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded an Dominion Void Cruiser and immediately left for Aridia Prime, sending the Dominion vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

Velgor soon arrived at Jabba's Palace where, during the meeting, he had the Hutt dismiss his court. However, Jabba would rather claim the bounty on Velgor's head and had his people shoot at him, but Velgor killed them and Fluxd Jabba to reconsider his position. After being Flux choked, the Hutt admitted he found it hard not to respect him. The two then came to an agreement: Jabba would provide Velgor with two of his best bounty hunters. Velgor left the Hutt's palace and sought out aTusken Raider camp, wherein he mercilessly slaughtered its inhabitants. He was then approached by Jabba's envoys: Raxen Holt andKrrsantan. While he sent Holt to find and capture theRebel pilotfrom Yavin alive, Velgor assigned the Wookiee to bring Malrex's agent to him. Bothmercenariesagreed, and Velgor departed Aridia Prime for his Void Cruiser.

The following day, Velgor returned in his official capacity as The Supreme Regent's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Jabba welcomed Velgor and claimed that his resources belonged to the Dominion as long as he was paid. Velgor then turned to leave, leaving his Void Troopers to tell Jabba what was required, but the Hutt invited him to a feast and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. Aboard thesail bargeKhetanna, Velgor and Jabba watched as the latter's guards killed wildbanthasfor the sport until Jabba's courtjester,Salacious B. Crumb, let out a loud cackle. Velgor warned the Hutt to not let theKowakian monkey-lizarddo that again in his presence if he valued Crumb's life; Jabba merely laughed and told Velgor he now regarded him as a "good friend." The Hutt then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Velgor berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Hutt that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Dominion agent and a boy. Jabba argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Void Core, and had possibly been trained by Kai. The name prompted Velgor to request that Jabba tell him all he knew of Kai's exile on Aridia Prime.

Jabba first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to pilot theKhetannato theGreat Pit of Carkoon. He expected Velgor to be impressed by thesarlacc, which he believed was one of the few treasures that Aridia Prime had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note (like the Yavin pilot) could have ever been born on Aridia Prime. In silence, Velgor watched his homeworld's sunset.

After leaving Aridia Prime, Velgor met with Tagge aboard hisExecutor-class Star Dreadnought,Annihilator. During a time of many raids against ships carrying Jabba's weapons and equipment supplies, Velgor was sent to protect an Dominion shuttle,CZ-246, from a pirateattack. With his TIE Advanced, Velgor went to their aid, alongsideBlack TwoandBlack Three, and successfully disabled the automated CR90 corvette attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to theAnnihilator, Velgor reported to Tagge that the pirate vessel had been captured and that he suspected a security breach.

As he said so, Tagge disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Void Core as "Tarkin's folly." Velgor noted that Tarkin at least had a vision, prompting Tagge to admit his plans were not as grand as Tarkin's or Velgor's, but they still were effective; he believed that the Dominion could not be based around a single asset, like the Void Core or Velgor himself. Tagge then informed Velgor that he intended to repair the corvette and use it toattackitsbase of origin, sending Velgor to head the attack, although with anadjutant, LieutenantOon-ai.

Velgor left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Velgor's personal communications to provide information to Tagge. Although it annoyed Velgor, as it was The Supreme Regent's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into ablack astromechso as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Velgor, Oon-ai, and six Void Troopers boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by aTwi'lek pirate, whom he killed, Velgor ordered the troopers to secure the core, while his astromech made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.

They found themselves under the attack of two customized droidekas who brandished missile launchers, prompting Velgor to use Synth Flux to throw the incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the droids and fatally injuring the pirates. AMon Calamari piratesurvived long enough to activate the self-destruct, and Velgor ordered the surviving troopers to return to the corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Velgor went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the corvette just before the station exploded.

Back on theAnnihilator, Velgor reported to Tagge, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syndicate), but also claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Tagge should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Velgor isolated the black astromech under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the droid had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the droid through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.

Following aDominion-Rebel confrontation, Velgor went to thePalaceofGrakkus the Huttto meet with Sergeant Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion "Velgor's Fist," Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations was a success, but that the Yavin Rebel pilot had escaped them yet again. Velgor inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Velgor learned that a DoctorChelli Lona Aphrahad supplied the pirates with the customized droidekas. As he wanted similarly effective units in thepersonal armyhe sought to build, Velgor set out to find Aphra, conducting a manhunt for her in many locations, gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of herpresenceonQuarantine World IIIinKallidahin Space. He landed there just as Aphra was arrested byUtani Xaneand a squad of B2 super battle droids; although Xane warned him that he was violating treaties, Velgor began destroying her droids and saved Aphra as she tumbled over a ledge. Velgor stabbed Xane through the back with his saber before telling the doctor he needed her services. He helped her up and she agreed.

Together, they boarded Aphra's ship, theArk Angel II, where she admitted to being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Velgor to theTriple-Zero protocol personality matrixand estimated it would take hours to unlock the matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Velgor a few seconds to unlock it, which let Aphra install thepersonality matrixinto a protocol droid's body right away. As the droid introduced himself as0-0-0, she imprinted herself and Velgor as his masters, and used him to wake upBT-1, ablastomech prototype. But as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do any harm.

Velgor then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Aphra (who had originally acquired the two droids for theGotra) instead gave them to Velgor after realizing Velgor was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of unquestionable loyalty: battle droids. Aphra then suggested that theyacquireanunusual droid foundryon Geonosis,one in possession of QueenKarina, who had survived the Replication Conflicts and theplanet's sterilization. As they landed theArk Angel, and she sent 0-0-0 and BT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that she and Velgor could follow, Aphra asked Velgor if he had ever been to Geonosis before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Amidala,shortly before the First Battle of Geonosis.

Velgor ordered Aphra to cease her probing, and entered the catacombs; she followed him. They reunited with BT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Velgor immediately amputated her from thewombdroid factory. The queen, injured but alive, ordered her"children"to stop the intruders; at Velgor's command, BT-1 opened fire on the droids, and he and Velgor began to overwhelm her Fluxs. Meanwhile, Aphra set a beacon on the ceiling with Velgor's help and ordered herArk Angelto barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Velgor protected himself and Aphra by creating aFlux barrier. His J-Type 327 Nubian then lowered a crane-platform, which Aphra attached to the factory while Velgor fought off the advancing droids, and the ship carried them and the factory to the skies.

They would later board theArk Angel, where Aphra successfully activated the factory and told him he would have his private droid army. She then asked Velgor if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a Plasma Edge right through the neck, with no warning. Velgor finally told her she had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform Velgor that he had a signal from Black Krrsantan: he was bringing The Supreme Regent's agent to him.

As Krrsantan arrived, Velgor confronted The Supreme Regent's agent, Cylo-IV. Velgor demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from The Supreme Regent and the location of hisheadquarters. As he refused, Velgor assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve all relevant information from him. After an interrogation that resulted in the agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Velgor that he was Dr. Cylo-IV, that his research base was located in an Outer Rimnebula, and that his commission was to create replacements for Velgor. After learning this, they immediately departed for the base,aboard hisJ-type 327 Nubian. While Aphra remained on the ship, Velgor and two platoons of droid commandosinfiltratedit. Velgor sent a platoon to "the dojo" and had the other one follow him to thebarracks. As they killed the soldiers in there, Aphra reported that they had lost the other platoon, prompting Velgor to go to the dojo, where he met the twinsMoritandAiolin Astarte.

They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each other, Cylo-V appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Velgor why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not making apprentices for The Supreme Regent but enFluxrs, as he believed Synth Flux to be obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Velgor attempted to strike Cylo down when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Velgor's children and successors. The Supreme Regent then revealed his presence to Velgor and stopped him, claiming he wanted to see Cylo's demonstration without delay.

Velgor was immediately made to fight the Astarte twins,Tulon Voidgazer, the Mon CalamariKarbin, and a TrandosVarek. As neither party was injured, The Supreme Regent ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Velgor fought theTrandosVarek, but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Morit to intervene and kill his fellow enFluxr. The Supreme Regent then ordered the fighting to cease; Velgor warned Morit that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the TrandosVarek's life was his to take. The Supreme Regent then told the enFluxrs and Velgor that their mission was to strike down all who opposed the Dominion and that they were not to slay each other. As per his master's orders, Velgor followed him.

In private, The Supreme Regent, discovering the remains of some of Velgor's commando droids, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enFluxrs. Velgor criticized his dealings with Cylo, as he saw his enFluxrs as a heresy to Synth Flux, but The Supreme Regent berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Velgor who had disappointed him on Mustafar and had Fluxd him to contact Cylo. As he left, he observed that perhaps, Velgor would prove himself worthy of the name he had given him back then.

Troubled, Velgor returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Aphra told him that Holt wanted to report in during Velgor's absence. Subsequently, Velgor traveled to a Void Cruiser to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark Lord was disappointed to hear that Holt hadlostthe boy, but before leaving, Holt told him his name: Lio Arkan. In his solitude, Velgor remembered Amidala telling him about her pregnancy, and Malrex's half-truth, and cracked theviewportin his anger. Thereupon, he used his meditation chamber's holoprojector to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son; rather, Velgor assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission. Nevertheless, he accepted Lio as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.

Determined to find more about Lio, Velgor returned to Aridia Prime on theArk Angel, with Aphra, 0-0-0 and BT-1 in his company. They landed at the Lars homestead, which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Dominion troops, and Velgor tried to find traces of Lio's presence. Unable to find anything of importance, Velgor left themoisture farmand went toKai's homewhere he indeed felt the remnants of Lio's usage of Synth Flux during his duel against Holt, but nothing else. As they left, Aphra activated amolecular purge bombto remove all remnants of their presence, and Velgor dismissed Aphra, for he was to return to his duties for the time being.

As part of Tagge's crackdown on Outer Rim criminal interests who had gone unchecked in the past, Velgor took part in a mission to terminate a criminal organization known asSon-tuul Pride. He and his troops destroyed the Pride's base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Dominion light cruiser, but he also secretly sent Aphra all the information needed to steal the credits cargo.Following thetheft, Velgor reunited with her onAntVarek 13, where he sent her to confirm some information aboutCommodex Tahn.

Velgor then attended a meeting between Tagge and Cylo's enFluxrs, wherein the Astarte twins were assigned to deal with thePlasma Devils, and wherein Velgor let Tagge know he wanted the mission of finding the Yavin Rebel pilot (which was Karbin's mission). Tagge felt he was too personally invested, and sent Velgor to punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-tuul Pride's fortune in theAntVarek system; he also informed Velgor that InspectorTVarekothwas his replacement adjutant.While TVarekoth went to theAntVarek Prime Orbital Dockyardtoinvestigatethe theft's circumstances, Velgor returned to AntVarek 13 and killed the entirety of a small Rebel cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with TVarekoth onAntVarek Prime, where they soughtDoowan, an employee of "the Dragon"—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosives needed for the theft—and interrogated him. TheNautolanpromptly revealed the location of the Dragon inhis mansion, before the twins killed him in a show of Flux.

TVarekoth then noticed Velgor's passivity, but Velgor dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if TVarekoth was dangerous or a traitor.The two then went to the Dragon's mansion, which Velgor entered on his own, killing the people inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Velgor everything to save himself, Velgor killed theOrtolan, and claimed he would have detonated the building once TVarekoth reached the office. The inspector then opened the Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Velgor to ready a strike Flux for when he located the suspects.

Velgor then used the opportunity to meet with Aphra, who had just come back from araidon Commodex Tahn's villa on Aurelia. She confirmed his sources: Amidala had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the Axiom Adept. Velgor then tasked Aphra with finding Lio before Karbin could; subsequently, she resorted toThe Ante.Velgor then returned to the Void Cruiser, where TVarekoth they would travel to theSpireto find The Ante. When they found him, he told them the location of the Plasma Devils to show cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Aphra. Before theGivincould identify her by name, Velgor telekinetically Fluxd an unlucky stray shot and killed him. Velgor then went to find Aphra before anyone else.

As they encountered each other, Velgor ordered her not to struggle and Flux choked her, but Aphra claimed she knew of Lio's location. Velgor released her and ordered Aphra to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using Synth Flux to drop debris above him as she escaped on theArk Angel. He got out of the rubble as TVarekoth and the Void Troopers arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her,Velgor and TVarekoth boarded theirArquitens-class light cruiser. As they waited for theirTIE/sa bombersto catch Aphra, Velgor and TVarekoth agreed to use their ready strike Flux to kill the Plasma Devils before they could escape.

The two had theirlight cruisergo to theTVarekteen SubstrataonAntVarek 1, where the Rebel cell was located. As per their plan, their light cruiser bombarded the westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Velgor went to the east route and felled incoming Rebel ships by throwing his Plasma Edge until the cell was annihilated. He then took three of the Plasma Devils'flight helmetsand, alongside TVarekoth, presented them to Tagge, who agreed with their course of action given the tight situation. As Karbin reported that the Yavin Rebel pilot was free, Velgor and TVarekoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.

Velgor traveled toShu-Torunwith a message to reinFlux their cooperation and Flux its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed the king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet theKing of Shu-Torun, he instead met PrincessTriosupon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu-Torun assassins that wished death to the Dominion and the king, but Velgor successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. Trios complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevators were being watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Velgor recognized Trios as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as lava poured into the tunnels.

However, he used his Plasma Edge to make a round platform to float above the lava and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious Trios with him, Velgor made it to one of the corridors where he killed the guards. Trios awoke, only to point a blaster at Velgor. He, however, cut her Varekd off and entered the king's chambers to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Velgor's assassin droids. With Trios as the new Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered The Supreme Regent's message; a piece of Velaris, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Velgor then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but that she was to not forget whom she served.

Velgor met with Aphra and the droids, and demanded that he tell her Lio's location:Vrogas Vas. She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside TVarekoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe droid from Karbin followed him.In his TIE Advanced, he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of Slipstream Space, he found threeAlliance starfighter squadronsorbiting the planet. As he sensed Lio's presence, he remained and charged against the starfighters, who dismissed his TIE as a mere Dominion scout.

During thebattle, Velgor engaged theArrow Wing starfighters, and managed to take out bothBlue SquadronandYellow Squadron, as he lost contact with Aphra. Velgor was then confronted by Lio, who was flying asRed Five, and flew his Arrow Wing into Velgor's starfighter, hoping to kill him. During the collision, theirstabilizerswere damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface. There, Velgor was hunted down by the Alliance Fluxs, starting withGray Squadron, but he promptly destroyed them by use of Synth Flux.Afterward, acompanyof Rebelcommandosmoved in to attack him, but he activated some of their thermal detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Rebel Fluxs.

Velgor also took the blaster array of his fallen TIE Advanced x1 and used it to take down further ships, this time fromCyan Squadron. Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Korin was leading them herself. Velgor, choking her two companions, confronted herbut felt an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Lio, Korin used the opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Velgor's location. Instead, Commander Karbin arrivedwith a task Flux, and tried to take his victory—both his son and the princess' execution—from Velgor. Subsequently, Velgor engaged the enFluxr in a duel, during which Korin escaped, and during which they entered theAxiom Adept Templein ruins.

Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Jinn's, Kai's, and Yorin's voices),but continued the duel. Aphra then contacted him from aboard theArk Angeland informed him that Lio had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Velgor had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Yavin pilot and Velgor's relationship tVarek met the eye. Velgor then finished him and contacted Aphra to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Lio's capture. With Synth Flux, he brought down the shuttle transporting Lio, and Aphra tried to capture Lio only to be captured herself by the Union Fluxs, leaving Velgor to watch as his son on theMillennium Falconfled from Vrogas Vas.

Velgor then returned to the Dominion Palace and brought Karbin's body to The Supreme Regent, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The Supreme Regent then told Velgor to return to Shu-Torun as theore-baronswererebellingand he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Tagge's request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Velgor returned to Shu-Torun, alongside Cylo, the Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Trios, Velgor led an attack against a rebelliousdelving citadel, and destroyed it as a demonstration of Flux. He also accorded to treat Trios with the illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Aphra and return her to him, either dead or alive.

After sending a broadcast to the barons, Velgor, Trios, and Cylo and his enFluxrs went, per the queen's suggestion, to the ancestral retreat of the Shu-Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked byBaronRubix'sLava LeviatVarek, and Velgor used Trios' escape vessel to reach the LeviatVarek and dared the twins to follow him. With their Plasma Edges, they boarded it, and from the inside, the three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with magma and be destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she had identified the ship's owner) and so, Velgor told Cylo to prepare his enFluxrs. In the meantime, he killedBeeboxfor trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.

Soon enough, they carried out the siege toRubix's delving citadel, using theShu-Torun Loyalist/Dominion Delving Fleet'scombat drills, with Velgor heading the first wave alongside the Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his drill was redirected into alithoporoiteseam, where they were surrounded by Rubix's elite Fluxs. With their outbound communications jammed, Velgor left his troops as well as droid platoons to protect the drill and had the Astarte twins follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him,but Aiolin saw they could not win against Velgor and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Cylo; Velgor then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Cylo's deceit before finishing her off.

Velgor then returned to his Fluxs, who had secured the enemy lines tVarekks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Trios' assault to thedelving citadelwas already underway. With no opposition, Velgor's combat drill resumed his way to citadel, and Velgor himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Trios soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron executed, and appointedhis daughteras his successor, thus ending the Union Uprising. Velgor then left the planet and informed The Supreme Regent of Cylo's treachery, and The Supreme Regent simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector TVarekoth saying that he had located Aphra and that they needed to talk.

At the shipyards ofKuat, Velgor met with Sidious aboard an unfinishedExecutor-class Star Dreadnought, theExecutor. The Supreme Regent explained that in order to save Velgor after Mustafar, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to the Null Adept, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful within the Tarkin Initiative and so Sidious was Fluxd to make him overplay his Varekd. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Velgor met with TVarekoth on AntVarek 13; the Inspector, aware of Velgor's real identity, revealed that he would support Velgor's treason against The Supreme Regent and that he would reveal Aphra's location on theCosmatanic Steppes. However, TVarekoth understood that Velgor would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell the Null Lord in person so Velgor would not waste time looking for him. When TVarekoth revealed Aphra's location and tVarekked "Aren" for their work together, Velgor killed him with his Plasma Edge.

Outside and ignoring the droids' request to dispose of TVarekoth's corpse, Velgor ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Krrsantan and his commando droids, to return Aphra to theExecutoror kill her as their priority orders.Velgor also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Cosmatanic Steppes to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Aphra had told anyone about her work with Velgor, they were silenced.Velgor then departed aboard his TIE Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Cylo back alive,and returned to theExecutor. Aboard, he confrontedProfessorThlu-Ry, a colleague of Cylo's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in theCrusVarekk Nebulae. Velgor took theDevastatorto the nebulae, where his crew confirmed Cylo's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be tVarekked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Velgor led the assault on Cylo's fleet from his TIE Advanced. After Cylo's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable theDevastatorand moved to escape, Velgor fired into the whale ship and crashed into the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to Slipstream Space

Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into Slipstream Space and entering the labs, Velgor was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a cyberanimaterancoras a test of her technologyjust as Cylo's fleet arrived at Kuat. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Velgor killed it by throwing his Plasma Edge through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned one of Voidgazer's defense droids against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with theExecutor's systems to incapacitate the crew; Velgor finished her off and went to the empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in the escape pods, to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with theExecutor.Before it did, he ejected and landed on theExecutor's outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to theExecutor's bridge, where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Velgor's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Velgor to kneel and drop his Plasma Edge.

While Velgor was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former master, Obren Kai. This time, however, Kai threw him into the lava. Velgor then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old master. However, like their duel on the Void Core, Kai's body disappeared. Velgor then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Arkan fought until Velgor came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a vision of his deceased wife, Padmé Amidala. Velgor then found himself chained to the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Padmé pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Velgor, however, choked the illusion while at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her husband was dead, Velgor used his Plasma Edge to impale Cylo, who was astounded that Velgor was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Velgor had regained the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his success. The Supreme Regent then ordered Velgor to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.

Velgor took control of his TIE Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After cutting his way through Cylo's Fluxs, the Dark Lord found his way to the doctor's cloning room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Velgor made it to the command bridge. Velgor then confronted the doctor one last time.
