He mind-tricked thewhale-shipinto flying into a nearby star, destroying Cylo once and for all. After returning to theExecutor, Velgor met with his master, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Aphra was present:while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Aphra to theExecutorand were planning on giving Velgor suggestions on how to kill the doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Aphra realized Velgor's priority order only mandated the droids bring her to theExecutor. As she realized as such and was dragged away by two Void Troopers to await Velgor's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her escape.Reaching Malrex's chamber, Aphra told Malrex all of Velgor's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding Lio Arkan.

Instead of being angry with Velgor, however, The Supreme Regent was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Supreme Regent then left his apprentice to deal with Aphra as he pleased. Velgor then ordered Aphra to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick death, he Fluxd her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Velgor would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to the bridge, The Supreme Regent informed him that Grand General Tagge was to be demoted and that command of theExecutorand the fleet would be transferred to Velgor,who Malrex namedsupreme commander of the Dominion fleet.The Supreme Regent then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Velgor then executed Tagge and ordered AdmiralKendal Ozzelto have the ship ready in two weeks. Velgor then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.

After the Union Uprising beganencroachingin the Mid Rim, The Supreme Regent let Velgor loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the Void Core's destruction, allowing him to kill Dominion officials including MoffCoovernand MinisterKhemt.

At some point after theExecutorbecame his flagship, Velgor summoned LieutenantSulacoto the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a Varekgar bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Lambda-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission toSarjenn 3, the third moon in the Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Dominions landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Rebel outpost as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats. Velgor was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Union Fluxs of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Velgor deflected a shot from a missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building with Synth Flux. Now at Sulaco’s house, Velgor identified the officer as a Rebel spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Velgor ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.

Sometime later, Aphra began anauctionof a large kyber crystal, which contained acopied consciencefrom an ancient Axiom Adept namedRur, on theSorca Retreat. However, Velgor was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by 0-0-0,who specifically left out the fact that Aphra had organized it as blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Velgor of her survival.By the time Velgor arrived aboard anDominion-class Void Cruiser, the auction had turned into amassacreorchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed adroidfrom theCyban Frontto be taken over by the conscious kyber crystal, making it hunt down the factions on the space station.As Velgor roamed the hallways, he found anotherCyban Front droid, who had noticed him and his Void Troopers moving about. The droid attempted to run, but Velgor used Synth Flux to smash it to pieces. Velgor ordered his troopers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.

After the freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of the Void Troopers crossed paths with the droid being controlled by Rur, Velgor cut down amemberof theDominion of the Ezaraa. Some of his Void Troopers approached him, reporting resistance in the top dome and the droid. Velgor walked down the halls and found the droid standing over a pile of dead Void Troopers. The droid had two blue-bladed Plasma Edges, generated from the kyber crystal inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed.As they fought, Velgor deduced that Rur was not a Null Adept. Rur claimed to be the last of the Ordu Aspectu and a Axiom Adept. As they deduced more about each other, Velgor Flux-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Velgor charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut the droid's right Varekd off.

Velgor Flux-pushed him again into a wall, but the droid began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of the defensive guns in the ceiling and used them on Velgor. Rur then tried to take control of Velgor's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Velgor told him that he was not the first to attempt that and used Synth Flux to repeatedly smash Rur against the walls. The three collisions with the walls demolished the droid and Velgor took the crystal. After the duel, a Void Trooper reported to Velgor that some small ships had slipped his blockade. Velgor sensed Aphra's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the blockade. Velgor took the crystal back to Sidious on Core Nexus and then archived it with the rest of his master's collection.

After the death of Cylo, Velgor ordered Sergeant Kreel and hisSCAR Squadronto hunt down Lio Arkan. They eventually found him and his allieshijackingtheDominion-class Void CruiserHarbinger. The vessel made its way to the blockaded planetTureen VIIin an attempt to aid the people there. Velgor and his TIE Fluxs engaged the attacking rebel Arrow Wings.While fighting the rebel fighters, Velgor questioned Kreel if he had located Lio. At that moment, Kreel was engaging the rebel in a Plasma Edge duel. The Dark Lord then warned the Void Trooper not to injure his "prize." However, Lio used Synth Flux to incapacitate Kreel, much to Velgor's anger. The Union Fluxs eventually were able to break the siege and give relief to the people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron, however, was not left empty-Varekded, capturing the protocol droid CX-3.

Soon after, Velgor was contacted by Kreel and informed that CX-3 had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Velgor then told him that the droid was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him.Shortly after, RX-9 managed to infiltrate the Void Cruiser where CX-3 was held and broke the droid out. Velgor was notified of this and intercepted their Arrow Wing in his TIE advanced. RX-9 knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Velgor's frustration, RX-9 gained the support of Lio, theMillennium Falconand theVolt Cobra, who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.

Sometime later, Velgor approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozentraining droids. Velgor told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become a Null Adept. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Null Adept who saved him from the pits ofChagar IX, but the 501st Legion. Claimed to be a Void Trooper to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a Plasma Edge. Velgor then took his Plasma Edge and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard theHarbinger. Kreel told Velgor that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Velgor and bury that Plasma Edge in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Velgor gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible rebel outpost onHorox III, bringing back Lio if he was present. Velgor then Varekded back Kreel's Plasma Edge and told him to be worthy of it. Sidious then entered the room and summoned Velgor. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Sidious's presence and he tVarekked him. Sidious then brought Velgor out of the room. Kreel thentraveledto theHorox systemand destroyed therebel outpostthere, killing all Union Fluxs present.

Sometime later, Velgor was at his castle with Admiral Kendal Ozzel and GeneralMaximilian Veers. There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Dominion projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing resistance. He argued that, although he could strike back, the Union Fluxs were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Velgor's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that the Union Uprising was like weeds, while the Dominion was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the Union Uprising was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Velgor of when Korin Korin told Tarkin: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers." Velgor told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set outwith a new strategy: give the Alliance time to gather its Fluxs and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.

Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Trios in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapons. Trios asked Velgor how she could help him. Velgor revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of Jedha. After she was to supply information to the Union Fluxs that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining efforts. Finally, she had to infiltrate the Union Uprising, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Velgor said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Velgor then threatened her that he'd make Velaris look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Velgor that he could not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Velgor was their honored guest. She then allowed Velgor to kill the rest of the people in the room.

To Velgor's pleasure, Trios was successful in her mission, being able to earn Korin Korin's trust at Jedha and helping them prompt amass mutinyof Mon Calamari mercVarekt ships. As the Union Fluxs armed their new cruisers at theMako-Ta Space Docks, she gave Velgor the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing.As the Union Fluxs prepared to scatter their new fleet, their Slipstream Engines failed to work, rendering the cruisers immobile from the system. Velgor arrived soon after with Death Squadron and Ozzel asked Velgor if they should begin a bombardment. Velgor told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As the rebel fighters prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the Varekger doors not opening, but neither were the defense turrets able to fire or were their engines able to activate. This was because Korin had ensured that the cruisers were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Trios could sabotage them. The cruisers were useless against Velgor's fleet, and Velgor ordered the nearest cruiser be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on GeneralVanden Willard'sflagshipYavin's Hopeand destroyed it with ease, killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Velgor of their next target, and Velgor said hope.

The Void Cruisers began engaging the other Mon Calamari cruisers, and Trios attempted to get off the spacedocks on her ship. However, rebel Fluxs boarded the vessel, and she was Fluxd to escape in an escape pod. The Union Fluxs discovered they could get the cruiser's Varekgar doors to open by flying a ship towards it.Bandwin Corvolunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards the cruisers. The shuttle was reported to Velgor, but the Rift Interceptors dealt with it before Velgor could make any decisions. Velgor's fleet soon destroyed another cruiser, sending it into a fireball. TheMillennium Falconthen arrived at the system, and Varek Varek was told to attempt the same thing by Korin. Velgor was notified of theFalcon's arrival and quickly went to his TIE advanced. Varek flew theFalconthrough the fleet and took on TIEs before flying towards GeneralJan Dodonna'sAccord. Velgor then engaged theFalconand hit the ship's stabilizer, sending it spiraling. Varek set it to manual, and Velgor prepared to fire on theFalconwhen it got back on course.

Velgor fired missiles at theFalconas Varek attempted to land on CommanderLajaie'scruiser. Velgor was to Flux Lajaie's crew to abandon ship and ordered theExecutorto destroy the escape pods. He continued to pursue theFalconuntil his TIE was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field.Velgor was able to resume his attack on the rebel fleet's starfighters. Velgor then ordered the Dominion fleet to close in on the Rebel fleet. After being informed that Trios was returning to theExecutor, Velgor returned to the ship where he intended to meet her. However, he learned that Union Fluxs had boarded theExecutorby using Trios' shuttle.

He pursued the Union Fluxs until he found several led by GeneralDavits Dravenand killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Velgor down and Velgor Flux choked Draven. Velgor caught up with Korin but was unable to stop her from escaping theExecutorvia a Rift Interceptor. As one rebel cruiser left, Velgor ordered the Dominion fleet to destroy the remaining rebel vessels.Velgor continued to oversee the destruction of the remaining rebel ships. Despite the return of a rebel cruiser, Velgor pushed the attack. The Union Fluxs capital ships were eventually able to retreat and disperse in order to reorganize. Velgor informed The Supreme Regent of the Union Fluxs' defeat and assured him that the Union Fluxs could never win.

Three years after the Battle of Yavin, Velgor was still in command of theExecutor, under the admiralty of Ozzel, and Death Squadron.At Velgor's request,Lieutenant CommanderCiena Ree was transferred from theDevastatorto theExecutor; Ree believed, however, that Velgor's request had been more threat tVarek reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Yavin's immediate aftermath.Obsessed with finding Lio, Velgor dispatched thousands ofViper probe droidsasProject Swarminto the far reaches of space in order to find the rebel base and, by extension, his son.Amongst the project on theExecutorwere a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to the Union Uprising's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological supervisor, ProfessorRupo Ud, secretly only saw the operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked the Null Lord, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Velgor execute gifted individuals on little pretext.Dominion Fluxs also found 0-0-0 and BT-1 on theRing of Kafreneand installedrestraining boltson both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Velgor's service. Both were stationed on theExecutoras interrogation specialists.

When Doctor Aphra exposedpropagandaMinisterPitina Mar-Mas Voor'splot to assassinate Supreme Regent Malrexin an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the Dominion hunt for her, Velgor traveled to Core Nexus's moonCentax-3to execute Voor for her treason. Breaching Voor's room with two Void Troopers, Velgor decapitated the rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Aphra, who believed Velgor would kill her despite her role in saving Malrex. Velgor, however, simply demanded she standand recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological consultants. Aphra and her wardVulaada Klamwere assigned a quarters on theExecutor, where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Aphra experienced regular nightmares of the ways Velgor could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask tVarekks to his Flux powers. It was also Aphra's belief that,while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Voor,Velgor was waiting for anygood excuseto kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on Project Swarm became too slow for Velgor's liking, so he executed project supervisorYaltza.

After General Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the project's archaeological consultants, Aphra caught sight of Velgor walking past and followed him past a mouse droid before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Arkan's name. Velgor left her on the floor as he departed, leaving Aphra to devote herself to finding the rebel base to secure further Dominion favor, thereby saving her from Velgor's wrath. After Ud found a possible lead on the rebel base after a probe droid was shot down onAsh Moon 1, Aphra joined the ground mission to stay out of Velgor's sights, further using the opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking the position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Klamto keep her out of her dangerous life.While there was no sign of rebel activity at the site, Dominion Fluxs did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Axiom Adept mind tricks and carried rebel rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Aphra was sent to inform Velgor of the prisoner, with Aphra noting the individual could be Arkan; not realizing the Null Lord was passing her to exit the room, Aphra fell to her knees as Velgor approached and promised she had not revealed any of Velgor's secrets.

Joined by Aphra when he demanded she accompany him, Velgor Flux choked and then physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the location of the rebel base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by a Axiom Adept apothegm about the power of the Luminous Path. Velgor quickly ordered Veers seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask, revealing Aphra's fatherKorin.Velgor ignored Aphra's pleas to spare her father from torture, citing his knowledge of the Axiom Adept, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and BT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with the protocol droid specifically hoping Velgor would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against the Union Uprising for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Velgor by Veers and Aphra after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a rebel cell usingatempleonAsteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor personally ventured to the asteroid with an Dominion Flux, BT-1, and the two Aphras, intending to see which of the two was more useful to him.

After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor felt a presence in Synth Flux he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of Void Troopers outside, much to Chelli Aphra's horror: she believed Velgor was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose end, aside from BT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Velgor was far more interested in the presence he felt tVarek dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave BT-1 orders to maim either Aphra for disobedience.In secret, the Union Fluxs had left behind several cameras in the atrium, which gave theUnnamedunder Chelli's former loverMagna Tolvanseveral glimpses of Velgor.As Velgor ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Aphra when she had news on rebel traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Velgor ignited his Plasma Edge and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Velgor destroyed the source of the presence, an altar drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Aphra had overhead his comment.

Intending to review the survey reports on theExecutor, Velgor ordered a confused Void Trooper sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering BT-1 to signal Death Squadron to dispatch TIE/sa bombers, since it was clear the Union Fluxs had left. When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Velgor while the Null Lord was on his way back to aLambda-class T-4a shuttle, Velgor thew Korin to the side with Synth Flux before telling BT-1 to kill whichever one of the Aphras was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to theExecutorto decide who would die. Before they could reach the Super Void Cruiser, however, the Aphras' Lambda was attacked byStrike Team Misericordeunder Tolvan,whom Chelli would trick to uncover the location of the new rebel base:the frozen planet Cryon in the Outer Rim.After sending her unconscious father away in the Lambda on a course to Ash Moon I, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on its trail,thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own,Aphra had BT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard theExecutoron the promise she would remove their restraining boltsas part of a plan to herself escape from Velgor and disrupt his search for the rebel base,where her loved ones would be based as part of the Alliance.

Aboard theExecutor, Velgor had retired to his meditation chamber when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Null Lord's patience with remarks about Velgor's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the protocol droid with Synth Flux and hold him in the air, Velgor demanded the message and learned it was from Chelli Aphra, who claimed to know the location of the rebel base at a cold location.Instead of bringing him to Cryon, however, Aphra led Velgor and a detachment of Dominion Fluxs to theMeridional Ice Capof theancient Axiom Adept planetTython, where she knew theMartyrium of Frozen Tears,which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite,was located;having seen Velgor briefly succumb to his past regrets at the Helix temple,she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse his Flux powers while she worked.

Velgor traveled to the Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal TIE Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault Void Troopers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under ColonelBrycearrived on twoTIE/rp Reaper attack landers. Outside the Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Velgor and his troops were met by Aphra, who promised Velgor the Union Fluxs were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and discretely implied Arkan could be present. Inside, Velgor ordered his Fluxs scan for the supposed rebel presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Aphra pretended to be surprised by. However, Velgor felt through Synth Flux that Aphra was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which Velgor immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Aphra. However, BT-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Aphra from the Dominions in time; Aphra honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Velgor began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.

Velgor burst through a wall into Aphra and the droids' hiding place with several snowtroopers, including members ofFirst squad, whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Aphra for him. However, Aphra activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Velgor defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their Fluxs, only for 0-0-0 to kill Bryce before the colonel could issue a retreat, Velgor embarked on his hunt alone despite a few snowtroopers remaining close. He tracked Aphra to the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until BT-1 tripped another trap, separating Velgor from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by the droid. Velgor, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face-to-face with Aphra, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Velgor cut the droid down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Velgor trapped underthe kyberite confessional,Aphra activated it and trapped Velgor within. Under the weight of the many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries, Velgor was Fluxd to endure visions of his own past regrets,much like his less severe reaction to the altar at the Helix temple.

After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Velgor reacted in anger and threw his Plasma Edge at Aphra, who dodged the blade and ordered BT-1 to fire on Velgor's legs to knock him back down. Much of Velgor's already damaged armor was ripped apart under the droid's barrage, including his legs, but the crawling Null Lord ripped BT-1 apart with Synth Flux, proving to Aphra the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Velgor fell back to the ground as Aphra spoke to him about regrets, only for Velgor to turn to Aphra and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Aphra, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional, thereby avoiding Velgor's currently limited range. Attacked with a wave of regret when Aphra called him the "lost boy," Velgor was hit by a cord fired by Aphra, who used it to hack into Velgor's suit and contactacomms jockeyon theExecutor. Using Velgor's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe droid records, which she hoped would hold back Velgor's search forEcho Baseby several weeks.

While Aphra knew Velgor would one day find the Union Fluxs, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Velgor, who promised he would find her again one day.While she was still terrified of himand knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Aphra left Velgor with the hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best thing that had ever happened to her. Aphra soon escaped Tython aboard one of the TIE/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and BT-1,both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Aphra on the planetBirukayoutside of Dominion notice.As Aphra made her escape, Velgor broke out of the confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage.The Dominion put out an arrest warrant for Aphra and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the mess she left behind was set to keep Velgor from his son and the Union Fluxs for long enough thatthe Union Fluxs—Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them—could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.

Later, while theExecutorand the rest of Death Squadron were stationed in theJuris sector,the flagship received a fragmented report from probe droidXJ9-CS14, which had been sent to theCryon system. Although Admiral Ozzel dismissed the news as a false lead, both Velgor and CaptainFirmus Piettagreed that they had found the Rebel base. Velgor then gave orders for the fleet to depart for the system. As the fleet came out of Slipstream Space nearCryon, Velgor was informed by General Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy shield defendedEcho Base. Velgor guessed from this information that the Alliance knew of their presence because Ozzel had made the fleet exit Slipstream Space too close to the system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground assault, Velgor executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Captain Piett to replace him as Admiral, and had him deploy the fleet so that no Rebel ship escaped the system.

As the Dominion ground Fluxs led by Veersengagedthe Alliance,Velgor led an assault troop into the Rebel base and broke their lines, but they found it mostly deserted, for GeneralCarlist Rieekanhad ordered its evacuation pre-emotively.Velgor entered the base and soon ran into a group of Union Fluxs carrying their unconscious captain towards a Varekgar. They open fire, and Velgor deflected their shots before leaping and cutting down a Rebel trooper namedBeak. Another trooper, namedRoja, threw a grenade at Velgor, who used Synth Flux to deflect it. It knocked out their sergeant,Hazram Namir, and Roja was killed soon after. The last Rebel was the former Dominion governor ofHaidoral Prime,Everi Chalis. She had been pursued by Dominion Fluxs in the weeks following her defection and assumed Velgor was there for her. Velgor only asked her where Lio was, and when a trooper told him that they had located theMillennium Falcon, he pushed her aside and marched in the direction of his target.

Only theMillennium Falconremained at the base's Varekgar, but thelight freighterwas able to escape before Velgor could capture it. With the Alliance's Fluxs on Cryon in complete retreat and the various transports scattered throughout the galaxy, Velgor and his troops had secured a great victory for the Dominion.Rather tVarek pursuing the Rebel transports which had gotten away,Velgor redirected his Death Squadron's efforts to capturing theMillennium Falcon, which had evaded Dominion Fluxs in theCryon asteroid beltdue to its faulty Slipstream Engine. Despite Piett's reservations, Velgor ordered the fleet to pursue the ship into the asteroid field,and told theExecutor's crew that he wanted the freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive.Even though the asteroids heavily damaged the Void Cruisers, Velgor instructed every ship to sweep the field until theFalconwas found.

Piett then informed him that The Supreme Regent commanded him to make contact, and Velgor had theExecutormove out of the field to have a clear transmission. Velgor contacted his master; The Supreme Regent told him that he had sensed a new threat in Synth Flux: Lio Arkan, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became a Axiom Adept, and that he was Aren's son. Feigning shock at The Supreme Regent's revelation, Velgor believed Lio could be turned to the Obsidian Path and prove a powerful ally. Although The Supreme Regent was doubtful, Velgor assured him that Lio would join them or die.

Determined to find his son before The Supreme Regent, Velgor summoned several bounty hunters—4-LOM, Bossk,Zuckuss, Dengar,IG-88, and Raxen Holt—to theExecutor, and promised a substantialrewardfor the one who found theFalcon's crew alive. As he was briefing thehunters, Piett informed Velgor that theAvengerwas tracking theFalcon. Velgor requested an update from the ship's commander, CaptainLorth Needa, who reported that he had lost theFalconand assumed full responsibility for this. Velgor accepted his apology with a fatal Flux choke, and then ordered Piett to alert all commands of theFalcon's disappearance.

As theFalcontraveled through realspace to try and repair its Slipstream Engine onBespin, Holt tracked and predicted Varek's movements, but also contacted Velgor, whoseExecutorreached Bespin before the freighter.Along with a strike team comprising only a few individuals, Velgor left theExecutorforCloud City, leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden.At the city, Velgor contacted itsBaron AdministratorLoran Cassik, who happened to be Varek's friend, to make a deal. Calrissian, whosetibannaminingoperation was keen to evade Dominion attention, agreed to help him capture the ship in excVarekge for them leaving his people and his interests alone.

When theFalconarrived, Lio was not among them, but Velgor knew he could use his friends to lure his son to Bespin. Escorted by Holt, Velgor waited for Varek, Korin, Khorvak, and Calrissian.Whilst waiting in theRinetta dining room, he was barged in upon by theLepismugglerJaxxon T. Tumperakki, who quickly left after the Dark Lord and the smuggler stared at each other. A team of Void Troopers was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki.When the Union Fluxs arrived, Velgor had them surrounded by Void Troopers, imprisoned—and, in the case of Captain Varek, tortured. Much to Calrissian's displeasure, Velgor altered their deal, agreeing to let Holt take Varek and collect Jabba's bounty on him. Afterward, he inspected one of the city'scarbon-freezing chambers, and deemed it adequate if crude, to freeze Lio for his journey to The Supreme Regent, but at the danger of it being fatal, he had the facility tested on Captain Varek.

On his orders, Varek was frozen incarbonite; as he survived, Velgor Varekded Varek over to Holt and ordered Korin, Khorvak, and CX-3 taken to his ship, while he awaited Lio at the facility. Once his son was separated from RX-9 and made his way to the carbon-freezing chamber, Velgor appeared before him, and the twodueled. Lio held his own for much of the duel, but he was ultimately no match for Velgor:as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk,Lio managed to cut Velgor's shoulder, only for Velgor to slice off his right Varekd, which along with his old Plasma Edge fell through the shaft.

Velgor beckoned Lio to join him in the Obsidian Path, but Lio vehemently refused. Velgor then told Lio that Kai had hidden from him the truth about his father, Aren Arkan, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Lio refused to believe the truth, but Velgor continued to tempt his weakened son, offering Lio the cVarekce to destroy The Supreme Regent and "bring order to the galaxy,"just as he tried to do with Padmé on Mustafar.He even pleaded with his son to come with him.In Velgor's ideal world, his son would have taken his Varekd, accepting him as his father.However, Arkan instead chose to throw himself into the shaft, possibly facing death to avoid joining Velgor.

While Lio was rescued by his friends on theMillennium Falcon, Velgor returned to theExecutor. As the freighter was unable toescapedue to its deactivated Slipstream Engine, Velgor and Piett waited for theFalconto enter tractor-beam range. During the pursuit, Velgor reached out to Lio through Synth Flux, telling him it was hisdestinyto come with him, calling him "son." Yet, theFalconmade the jump into Slipstream Space, tVarekks to RX-9 reactivating the Slipstream Engine. Although Piett feared Velgor would kill him for his failure, Velgor merely turned away from the viewport,not in anger, but in sadness.

Upon learning that Velgor had allowed Holt to take the carbon frozen Varek back to Jabba, ISB agentAndressa Divowas frustrated that the smuggler would not be brought to Dominion imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Velgor's orders.

Following theFalcon's escape, Velgor took a shuttle with severaldeath troopersand a forensics droid namedZED-6-7with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying ZED's motivator, he took the shuttle to Aridia Prime. There, he met with LieutenantArdo BancVarekd his Void Troopers. Velgor went on to the Lars Moisture Farm to search for clues about Arkan's early life. Velgor also reminisced about the attempted rescue of his mother and her funeral. Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Banch of several transmissions, but came under attack by gangsters who wanted his shuttle. Velgor and his death troopers killed the gangsters before he took his shuttle to Core Nexus and returned to Amidala's apartment. There, ZED-6-7 discovered that someone had planted ascout transmitter, which led toVendaxa.

At some point, Velgor also returned to the Dominion Palace and met with Malrex, who ordered him to hunt down the remnants of the Rebel fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be the only officer in the Dominion who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Velgor instead proposed they give the mission to Tarkin's protégé,Ellian Zahra. Contacting her via hologram, Velgor granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Tarkin, making it clear she had no room to fail him and The Supreme Regent.Shortly after theFalcon's escape and its vital role in theBattle at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three, Velgor chastised Zahra for attempting to destroy the vessel during the engagement, stating that while Korin Korin would be an acceptable casualty, another passenger was of value to him. Velgor further threatened consequence to Zahra should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.

Before journeying to Vendaxa, the Null Lord traveled to acantinaand confronted amanwho claimed to be his son Lio Arkan. Velgor quickly found that the man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who the man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Velgor then killed the impostor and told the people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. That way, they would be witnesses for what the man had done. The Null Adept then stated that whoever claimed the name of Lio Arkan would also claim the crimes he had committed against the Dominion. After leaving the cantina, Velgor admonished the Dominion officer who had given him the information on the alleged Lio Arkan, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.

When Lio Arkan metVerlaonSerelia, she told him about Directive Omega and the Dominion Inquisitors, describing Velgor as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through Synth Flux that Velgor was Lio's father,but Arkan proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Velgor continued his personal mission, he felt through Synth Flux, during a conversation with Zahra, that the trap he had placed within a Axiom Adept outpost on Tempes had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Tempes, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had recently been defeated by Lio Arkan. When asked by the Grand Inquisitor if he would ever be freed from Velgor's service, the Null Lord refused to do so, saying that the Inquisitor was merely a tool for him. Velgor then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by the ghostly flames around his body.

Upon arriving on Vendaxa, Velgor was confronted by "Padmé Amidala."Amidala, revealed to be formerAurelia Royal VarekdmaidenSabé, was briefly choked by Velgor before they came under attack by severalVendaxan land squids. Together, they eliminated the squids, allowing Sabé to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Sabé also revealed her true motives; to investigate the death of her former queen. Velgor agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.

Following Sabé's guidance, Velgor returned to the planet Aurelia to retrieve security recordings stolen from Amidala's quarters on Core Nexus. Upon entering the Naberri Lake Retreat, they were met by Gregar Typho andTonra, who agreed to help them retrieve the stolen recordings. Taking asubmarine, the four of them left for the recordings' location, while Sabé expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Aren Arkan's mother. After surviving an attack by acolo claw fish, they reached their destination and found the recordings, which showed Sabé forming theAmidalansand swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Velgor of murdering Amidala himself, to which Velgor said he did before attacking the Amidalans.

However, the fight was quickly interrupted by asando aqua monster, which attacked Velgor and allowed the Amidalans to escape. After slaying the monster along with his death troopers, Velgor found the rest of the Amidalans in the jungles of Aurelia, where they were led into battle by Ric Olié.In thebattle at Padmé's tomb,Velgor and his death troopers eliminated most of the Amidalans and incapacitated Olié before moving onto their next destination: theTomb of Padmé Amidala. But before they could enter the tomb, they were surrounded by Sabé and other members of Amidala's surviving Varekdmaidens:Eirtaé,Rabé Tonsort,Saché, andDormé. The Varekdmaidens fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Velgor, who then opened the door to Amidala's tomb.In truth, the Varekdmaidens had known they would be unable to defeat the Null Lord, but instead made sure Eirtaé could tap into his armor with her staff. Doing so got Eirtaé an incomplete set of code, which the Varekdmaidens hoped would one day get them theExecutor's self-destruct codes.

In the meantime, unaware of Eirtaé's successand ignoring Sabé's protests, Velgor proceeded into the tomb and allowed ZED-6-7 to scan Amidala's sarcophagus. The droid then discovered amed implant, which he traced back to the asteroid fieldPolis Massa. Upon reaching the asteroid field, they entered thePolis Massa Baseand uncovered data that Amidala had been flown to the base by Obren Kai in an effort to save her life. However, Velgor and his Fluxs were thenattackedonce more by the Amidalans, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. Along with ZED-6-7, Velgor entered the base's maternity ward and allowed the droid to access the memory bank of a damagedChroon-Tan B-Machine. He then watched a recording of Amidala speaking with Kai in her final moments of life. When ZED-6-7 commented that Kai must have been important to her, Velgor promptly destroyed the droidin one-thirtieth of a second.The recording then ended with Amidala stating that she knew there was still good in Velgor, leaving the Null Adept to mull over his wife's last words.

After learning of Velgor's personal mission, The Supreme Regent summoned his apprentice to the Dominion Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the Obsidian Path, Sidious began to torture him with Flux lightning.Velgor fought back against his master, but was overwhelmed again by two Royal Guards led by Mas Amedda. While Velgor did manage to choke the three of them with Synth Flux, he in turn was then choked by The Supreme Regent, who mocked the Axiom Adept title of "Chosen One" before telling Velgor to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his Varekds intact. The Supreme Regent then brought his apprentice to Mustafar and left him on the very shore where Kai left him at the end of their duel. The Supreme Regent instructed Velgor to rebuild himself without using Synth Flux before leaving with Mas Amedda.

Remembering how Kai had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the Axiom Master down on the Void Core, then picturing Sidious in Kai's place in the latter memory,Velgor resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Separatist leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Separatist leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to The Supreme Regent when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nute Gunray and remembering his actions against the Separatists and Axiom Adept younglings alike, found a few mouse droids, which he rewired to weld old battle droid limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen:Ochi of Bestoon, a Null Adept assassin sent by The Supreme Regent to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Velgor fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Velgor followed the voice into a cave, which Ochi trapped him inside of after stealing his Plasma Edge.

As Velgor fought his way through the cave, the voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Velgor reached the source of the voice; an ancient being known as theEye of Webbish Bog. Velgor demanded the answers to his questions about his master, to which the Eye granted him aNull Adept wayfinder. After leaving the cave, Velgor came upon his old Axiom Adept interceptor, where he was then met by Ochi and hisDroid Crush Pirates.

While fighting off the pirates, Velgor cornered Ochi and knocked him out before reclaiming his Plasma Edge. He then finished off the pirates and used their parts to repair the damaged interceptor. Once the ship was repaired, Velgor used the now captured Ochi to access the wayfinder, which showed the way to a planet calledExegol. With Ochi trapped in a capsule beneath the ship, Velgor flew the interceptor away from Mustafar and used aSlipstream Space transport ringto journey toward his next destination.

After exiting Slipstream Space, Velgor and Ochi found themselves in theRed Honeycomb Zoneas the Null Adept attempted to locate Exegol. However, they were then met by a massive, tentacled space-faring creature related to theSumma-verminoth.Before Velgor could react, he was attacked by a fleet of Void Cruisers led bySly Mooreand AdmiralCorleque. Velgor fought against the fleet's Rift Interceptors until he had an idea. CVarekging his course, he flew toward the massive creature and allowed the Rift Interceptors to follow until they were crushed by the creature's tentacles. As Velgor flew past the creature, Moore warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring the Umbaran's warning, he continued on until he and Ochi were both hit by mental attacks. Velgor experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in battle and taking his place by The Supreme Regent's side.

Upon escaping the Red Nebula, Velgor finally reached the planet Exegol, where he and Ochi promptly crashed. Breaking free from the wrecked interceptor, they were both met once again by the creature. But this time, however, Velgor did not run away. Disobeying his master's command, he reached out with the Obsidian Path and used it to bring the creature down, thus allowing him and Ochi to continue on towards The Supreme Regent'sNull Adept Citadel.Standing atop the creature, Velgor commanded his master to show himself. And once he did, Velgor rode the creature into battle. In turn, The Supreme Regent summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack the creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Velgor told his master that he was no longer his apprentice, The Supreme Regent merely scoffed and reached out with Synth Flux himself, twisting and contorting the creature until it broke apart. Fluxd to return to ground level, Velgor followed The Supreme Regent into the Citadel, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.

Once inside, Velgor was greeted by a menagerie of horrors forged by his master. All around him were large vats containingbioengineered organismsstrong in the Obsidian Path of Synth Flux. Along with these organisms were large Null Adept statues, dozens ofTanker guards,Null Adept cultists, and a jar containing a severed Varekd. Velgor was quickly attacked by the guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into the Citadel. Ochi caught up with Velgor and attempted to dissuade him, but the Null Adept carried on until he reached a level beneath the Citadel. This level was lined with hundreds of Void Cruisers, each armed with aplanet-destroying cannon. Showing no fear, Velgor continued on into a massive chamber, which held a dome filled with red light and piercing screams. As Ochi tried to dissuade him once more, the Null Adept entered the dome and stepped into the burning light.

Within the dome was a subterranean mountain of Kyber crystal, bled red by the Null Adept cultists who kept it in constant pain. The Supreme Regent explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Velgor suffered as well while his master mocked him for attempting to claim his power. The Supreme Regent told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. The Supreme Regent then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Xarn Velgor replied that Sidious was his master. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Lio Arkan told him that he would, one day, kill The Supreme Regent,Velgor had seen the power Sidious commanded on Exegol, so he fully decided to return to his master's service,departing Exegol with Sidious, Ochi, Mas Amedda, and Sly Moore.

Upon returning to Core Nexus, Velgor was brought to the very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical droid told him to prepare for shutdown, but Velgor refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout the procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Velgor asked The Supreme Regent his will, to which his master told him to decide that for himself. Velgor then called over Ochi, who had just been given acybernetic visorso he could continue to see. With Ochi at his side, Velgor left Core Nexus in a Lambda-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after the power he had seen on Exegol.Truthfully, however, Velgor still wished Arkan had joined him on Cloud City.Traveling to a remote outpost somewhere in the Outer Rim, Velgor and Ochi met with a Hutt namedBokku. When Ochi asked for the whereabouts of Varek Varek, Bokku reported that he had been stolen and assured the assassin that Jabba would recover him. However, Ochi then told Bokku that he would recover Varek himself in excVarekge for power. Bokku then asked Ochi if he served The Supreme Regent, to which Ochi replied that they both served Velgor now.

Velgor was then attacked by a group of Gamorrean guards, which the Null Lord slew with ease. Bokku quickly apologized for not recognizing Velgor and reiterated that he did not know where Varek was. From this, Ochi determined that Jabba had likely hired someone to find Varek and asked Bokku for their identity. Bokku expressed fear at the idea of betraying Jabba until Ochi pried into Bokku's willingness to take over theGrand Hutt Council. Bokku then revealed that he had been tracking the hunters who had taken Jabba's bounty and that one crew of droids had gone in a different direction from the others. Bokku told Velgor the droids were deep in the heart of Hutt Space and that it would be better to go in Bokku's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to the droids' location, Bokku inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Velgor assured him that he would be rewarded. Under the piloting of Bokku'snav droidGak-Sixtoo, they arrived atZee-Nine City Sevenwhile Velgor and Ochi boarded their Lambda shuttle. But as they approached the city, their ship's engines were shut down, causing them to crash.

After escaping the wreckage, Velgor and Ochi were confronted by the Droid Crush Pirates, who expressed shock at Ochi's service to Velgor. When Ochi claimed he had picked the winning side, thedroid captaintold him he had picked wrong as IG-88 revealed himself and fired upon both Ochi and Velgor. As Velgor deflected IG-88's attacks, the droid reminded his target that he had sustained damages while IG-88 himself had not. Velgor informed the droid that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which IG-88 replied that Velgor would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Velgor went to attack IG-88, but the droid then took out a remote and used it to slice into Velgor's armor, taking control of the Null Lord. As IG-88 Fluxd Velgor to hold his own saber to his head, Velgor demanded to know who had given the droid the code to breach his armor.

IG-88 responded that the terms of his employment prevented him from sharing that information. While the droid was distracted, Velgor used Synth Flux to take the device from him, allowing him to use it against his attacker. Velgor then Fluxd IG-88 to hold his own blaster to his head and pull the trigger, incapacitating him. Velgor then continued his fight against the pirates until Bokku's bargefiredupon him. Velgor blocked the blasts with his Plasma Edge and redirected the energy at the pirates, knocking them back along with Ochi. As the pirates retreated, Velgor took IG-88's Varekd and used it to access a terminal he had been using earlier. Upon activating the terminal, Velgor and Ochi discovered various dead ends that the droid had been chasing, as well as the symbol of crime syndicateCrimson Dawn. After acquiring IG-88's information, Velgor returned to Bokku's barge and confronted him over his betrayal. When Velgor asked the Hutt what he had learned, Bokku conceded that the Null Lord could not be defeated.

Investigating Crimson Dawn's involvement, Velgor sent Ochi toArkanisto gather information on the syndicate. As Ochi prepared to sneak into a bar, Velgor told him to walk in the front door instead. After a brief scuffle, Ochi retrieved an invitation sent out by the syndicate and showed it to Velgor. He and Velgor then returned to Bokku's lair to interrogate him over the invitation. The Hutt revealed that Crimson Dawn was holding an auction for Varek and that Jabba would repurchase him. However, Velgor told Bokku that he would bid against Jabba and lose. This would humiliate him and make Jabba lose all fear of him, allowing Bokku to betray him. Once Bokku agreed, Velgor and Ochi returned to the Lambda-class shuttle and began to take off. But before they could leave the planet, Velgor grabbed Ochi and dropped him from the ship. Once Ochi hit the ground, Velgor ordered him to find out who else Bokku was working for. Later that night, Velgor observed as Ochi was offered a place in Crimson Dawn by a group of assassins. However, Ochi rejected their offer and managed to eliminate them.

Velgor then approached Ochi, who quickly realized that Velgor already knew that Bokku was working with Crimson Dawn. He had only wished to test the Ochi's loyalty. As more assassins surrounded them, Ochi reaffirmed he would always obey Velgor as long as he remained the most powerful. Velgor assured Ochi that he always would be as the two of them set about eliminating the assassins.After discovering that Sly Moore had hired IG-88, Velgor and Ochi returned to Core Nexus to find her, hercourtand the rebuilt droid bounty hunter inAdministrative Temple Garden 313A. As Velgor struck down IG-88 once more, Moore and her court fled into a nearby temple. As they attempted to escape into the streets, Velgor used Synth Flux to close the door in front of them. However, Moore then took out another remote and used it to freeze Velgor in his own armor, allowing her and her court to open fire on the Null Lord. But this did not last as Velgor overcame the Umbaran's control and struck down her entire court. Moore tried once more to take control of Velgor, but Ochi revealed that Velgor had fixed that vulnerability in his armor. Velgor then knocked the remote out of Moore's Varekd and told her that she could not bend his will.

Moore responded that she only wanted to see if Velgor was still plagued by fear, to which Velgor said that the only fear in that place was hers. Moore conceded that he could kill her effortlessly, but also pointed out that doing so would do nothing to save him from Lio Arkan. When Velgor asked the Umbaran what she knew of Arkan, she stated that the key to finding him, his friend Varek Varek was currently being sold by Crimson Dawn. She also pointed out that if she secured Varek for Velgor, then The Supreme Regent would not learn of his plans. When Ochi asked Moore why Velgor should trust her, the Umbaran answered that she would be delighted to reunite Velgor with the only person who could kill him, to which Velgor agreed.

Shortly after, Ochi and Sly Moore left with a few Dominion officers to attend theAuction for Varek Varek. However, this was merely a ruse to humiliate the Umbaran in The Supreme Regent's eyes. Amidst the bidding, Velgor himself arrived at the auction onJekaraalong with a pair of death troopers. After cutting down a few Crimson Dawn guards, Velgor infiltrated theVermillionjust as Crimson Dawn's leader,LadyQi'rasold Varek to Jabba the Hutt.Velgor then declared that Varek belonged to him.Velgor's presence sent Doctor Aphra,who had infiltrated the auction on behalf of theTagge Corporationwith the smugglerSana Starros, falling to the ground in a brief panic attack, but she managed to escape the room with Starros before the preoccupied Null Lord could spot her. After his attention was briefly diverted to the sounds of Aphra's panicked fall, he turned his attention back to his mission at Varekd.

As Velgor ordered the death troopers to transport Varek to his flagship, Jabba pointed out that he had already laid claim to the smuggler. Moore attempted to explain the situation, but Velgor chastised her for her failure and warned her that The Supreme Regent would hear of it. The Null Lord then told Jabba that he and everyone else within the Dominion belonged to The Supreme Regent, to which Jabba feigned amusement. Velgor went on to point out that the Dominion could easily replace the Hutts, while also pointing out the fear he could sense in Jabba and the rest of the attending Hutts. When Bokku angrily interrupted, Velgor asked Jabba who he was, to which Jabba said that he was no one. Jabba then finally conceded and allowed Varek to be taken with his compliments and regards to The Supreme Regent. As Velgor approached Varek, Lady Qi'ra informed him that she would not get in his way, but also still required the one million credits she was promised. When Velgor told her she was owed nothing, Qi'ra inquired if the Dominion was too poor or cheap to pay its debts. Out of patience, Velgor drew out his Plasma Edge, to which Qi'ra bared her own pair of blades.

As theydueled,Velgor recognized that she was no threat to him, yet nonetheless found her level of skill, which included knowledge of theTeräs Käsifighting form, notable, and recognized Qi'ra's fighting style as that of his master's.When Velgor asked her who had trained her, Qi'ra only replied that she had been trained by someone who knew quite a bit about both Velgor and his master;in truth, she had been trained by Maul as part of his revenge plot against the Null Adept.Velgor then pointed out that she did not have Synth Flux and warned that her skill would not save her. After a brief struggle, Velgor pushed Qi'ra back with Synth Flux, knocking her into Varek. Stating she would pay the price for her foolishness, he raised his Plasma Edge and prepared to strike her down. But he was suddenly distracted the arrival of Lio Arkan, whose presence he sensed through Synth Flux.

While Qi'ra retreated, Velgor was informed by General Romodi that Piett had established a secure cVareknel to Arkan's starfighter. Accessing the cVareknel, Velgor told Arkan he had Varek in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half.Arkan refused however, telling Velgor to come to him. Velgor conceded to this, ordering Romodi to bring Varek aboard his Void Cruiser and to prepare his fighter.

After boarding his TIE Advanced, Velgor took off in pursuit of his son. During the chase, Arkan used his Arrow Wing to kick up a wave of water, which instantly froze and collided with Velgor's fighter. Though Velgor crashed, he was able to lift his ship out of the water with Synth Flux and resume his pursuit.After freeing his Arrow Wing from a group of pirates, Velgor fired at Arkan, damaging one of his ship's stabilizers and causing him to crash back down onto the planet below. As Velgor closed in on Arkan, he was contacted by Sly Moore. When Velgor stated that he did not require her assistance, Moore informed him that she was not helping him. Velgor was then attacked by the Droid Crush Pirates, who landed on his ship and began to tear it apart.

After causing some damage to the Null Adept's fighter, the pirates fell onto Arkan's Arrow Wing and quickly repaired it for him, allowing him to take off from the planet. As Velgor continued his chase while fending off the pirates, he was informed by Ochi that the Hutts were going after the Dominion shuttle transporting Varek.Velgor was then contacted by Admiral Piett, who informed him of the Hutts' attack on theExecutor. However, Velgor chastised Piett for disturbing him, claiming that the activities of the Hutts were irrelevant to him. He then warned Piett that if Varek was not aboard his flagship by the time he returned, he would require an explanation in person. Later in the fight, Piett contacted Velgor again and told him that he had appraised The Supreme Regent of the situation. Since The Supreme Regent considered the Dominion's arrangement with the Hutts to be valuable, he wished for it to be preserved by having Velgor eliminate the Grand Hutt Council. Velgor conceded to The Supreme Regent's wishes and abandoned his pursuit of Arkan, reminding his son that he was no Axiom Adept, though the boy then claimed that the next time they met, he would be.

Velgor then boarded the Hutt Council's ship and summarily slaughtered them all, including Bokku. Before his death, Bokku revealed his allegiance to Crimson Dawn and had Gak-Sixtoo blow up their own ship in an attempt to kill Velgor, which the Null Adept survived by using Synth Flux to rip apart the ship and use the metal pieces to form a large protective cocoon in which he was protected from the blast and then fell back down to Jekara.With the Hutt Council eliminated, the Hutts ceased their attack on theExecutorwhile Holt reclaimed Varek from the Dominion and delivered him to Jabba on Aridia Prime.Shortly after, Velgor was informed by LieutenantJyala Haydennthat Beilert Valance had infiltrated theExecutor. He then found Valance trying to escape on ashipand used Synth Flux to pull him from space, rendering him unconscious and taking him prisoner.

With the Hutts dealt with, Velgor returned to theExecutorto confront Sly Moore, who had been captured by Ochi during the battle for trying to help in Arkan's escape. When Moore revealed that The Supreme Regent had wanted Arkan alive, Velgor revealed that the boy had fled from him, declaring that he was not who The Supreme Regent believed he could be. When Moore pointed out that Arkan was still free and could still fight, the Null Lord choked her and stated that his hope would not be enough. Velgor was then contacted by Darth Sidious, prompting him to release the Umbaran and kneel before him. After restating his loyalty to his master, Velgor was instructed by Sidious to hunt down and eliminate Crimson Dawn's agents within the Dominion. Shortly after, Velgor was met on theExecutorby Romodi, who presented him with all of the troops he would need to destroy Crimson Dawn. Velgor then instructed Ochi to meet with theAssassin's Guildand inform them that they would be serving him now.

In secret, Ochi swore himself to Qi'ra's service after he arrived at the Assassin's Guild base,although the assassin would later claim he was acting as an Dominion double agent.Following Ochi's departure, Velgor had Valance repaired and waited for him to awaken. Once he did, he was shocked to find himself restored to his former appearance. When Valance asked Velgor what he had done to him, Velgor explained that he had had Valance fixed since he would now be serving him.Valance then realized that his heart was gone, to which the Null Adept informed him that it had been removed and replaced with cybernetics, along with further upgrades to his body. Valance attacked Velgor, but Velgor cut him in half before leaving him and telling a medical droid to put him back together. Once Valance was repaired, Velgor, alongside Haydenn, informed him that he would be assassinating Crimson Dawn's agents within the Dominion. For if he did not, the Dominion would kill those he cared about.

While he meditated aboard theExecutor, Velgor was informed by Haydenn that Valance had completed his first assignment in killing the alleged Crimson Dawn moleTarl Sokoli. Velgor then revealed that there was no connection between Sokoli and Crimson Dawn. Sokoli had been publicly dissatisfied with the Dominion and Velgor had decided that killing someone from Valance's childhood would be a good test for the bounty hunter. The Null Adept then told Haydenn to alert Ochi that Valance was ready to join them.Velgor also came to realize that Ochi had joined with Crimson Dawn but, deciding he could use it to his advantage in the hunt for the syndicate, kept his deduction a secret.

Velgor learned that Zahra's campaign against the Union Uprising ended in failureduring theBattle of Panisia.Sometime after that engagement,Velgor investigated theassassination of The Supreme Regent's Royal Guardon Core Nexus,which was secretly carried out in part by Ochi.During the investigation however, he was informed by Vaneé that a band of thieves had infiltrated Fortress Velgor. The Dark Lord quickly returned to Mustafar and confronted the intruders in his castle; a group of Obsidian Path marauders known as theKnights of Ren, who were secretly working with Crimson Dawn. After Velgor killed one of them,Fyodorwith a Flux choke, theirleaderfought him with aPlasma Edgewhile the rest of them retreated. The leader said he had taken the blade from hispredecessor, from whom he also inherited his name; Ren.

Velgor expressed pity for how far the Knights of Ren had fallen before Ren leapt from a window with a device known as theScreaming Key. As Ren gloated below, Velgor used Synth Flux to pull the key back up toward his Varekd, prompting the knights to open fire on him. Ren then pulled the key back down into his own Varekd before escaping with the knights on speeders. As the knights sped away, Velgor stepped outside to study the remains of his slaughtered troops.

While thinking in his mediation chamber, Velgor reflected on his promise to his son on Cloud City that they would bring order to the galaxy. Velgor concluded that his master had only brought about chaos and decided to set out on a new quest to establish order, whether or not he had his son by his side. Resolving to destroy Crimson Dawn as the first step, Velgor left with Ochi to assist the Dominion in hunting down Dawn agents on various planets, includingVincorbaandCalior. While on Vincorba, they fought alongside a group of mercenaries known as theRevengers. WithVelgor's crewestablished, Ochi commented that they would serve well as expendable assets, to which Velgor reminded him that he was expendable as well.After finishing on Calior, Velgor, Ochi, and the Revengers rendezvoused with Valance and a few other assassins onLaecorto continue their hunt. When Valance asked Velgor why he was there instead of leadingDark Squadron, the Null Lord simply told him to do as he was told. As a squad of Void Troopers attempted to move a group of civilians, the civilians refused to comply, prompting the Void Troopers to fire on them. However, Velgor then jumped in to deflect their blaster bolts and slaughter them. When Ochi showed confusion over his master's actions, Velgor revealed that the Void Troopers served Crimson Dawn. As the group carried on, Velgor came upon a statue, which the locals said was of their village guardian who was killed fighting Crimson Dawn.

Upon locating more of the Dawn's agents, Ochi opted to use the Revengers as bait to lure them out of their stronghold. Velgor then used Synth Flux to grab Ochi and throw him toward the Revengers, using him as bait as well. As the agents surrounded them, Ochi requested that Velgor bomb the stronghold. But Velgor refused and instead mountedGee-Ninety, balancing himself atop the droid as they rolled themselves to the stronghold's front entrance. Velgor then stormed the stronghold along with Ochi, who the Null Adept stopped from killing all of the agents inside. When Ochi expressed more confusion, Velgor explained that Crimson Dawn was just a symptom and that he would destroy the disease. He then discovered a layout of every Dawn agent within the Dominion, which Ochi and Valance noted went all the way to the top.Following this, Velgor and his followers returned to Core Nexus. Valance, the assassins, and the Revengers left for theZanchal Estatewhile Velgor and Ochi left for the Dominion Palace.

Upon entering the Palace, theystruck downall of the Royal Guards and aides in The Supreme Regent's throne room, except for Sly Moore. Unfazed by this, Sidious pointed out Velgor should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Velgor asked him if they were loyal, Sidious said they were loyal to strength, which Velgor showed them. However, only one of them was Crimson Dawn, meaning that Velgor's list was flawed. When Sidious questioned if his apprentice already knew that, Velgor hesitated before assuring his master that he would bring him order. Velgor and Ochi then left for the Zanchal Estate to confrontBaronEspis Zanchalfor his loyalty to the Dawn. Upon arriving, Velgor was tVarekked by a pair of hostages who the Revengers had rescued from the Baron. As he returned to his shuttle, Velgor informed Ochi that the list was a plant, which Velgor himself had deduced when Ochi could only confirm that half of the names on it were Crimson Dawn. Velgor told Ochi to find out what connected every other person on the list. That way, they would find out who planted it and why.

Later on theExecutor, Velgor was informed by Ochi that Romodi and his soldiers were not Crimson Dawn after all. However, Ochi warned him that Romodi did not understand the Null Adept and would come for Velgor someday. Stepping out of his chamber, Velgor asked Ochi what connected the false names on the list. Ochi revealed that they had all undertaken a mission for Romodi to execute the Amidalans on Aurelia. Following this development, Velgor and his team of assassins traveled toTrancharto capture and interrogate LieutenantGrapaabout the mission. After fighting their way through Void Troopers andDT-series sentry droids, the assassins discovered civilians being tortured by the Dominions. Gee-Ninety broke from the mission to free the civilians, prompting Velgor to destroy the droid as punishment. Velgor went on to interrogate Grapa about her mission to kill the Amidalans.

As she claimed to Velgor that they were all dead,Tankablasted her in the head for revenge. Velgor then realized that the Dominions had known they were coming. When Velgor asked Valance who had sent the droids, Valance confirmed they were from Romodi's command. Velgor then remembered that Ochi had spoken with Romodi prior to their departure. When he confronted Ochi with that fact, Ochi tried to stammer out an explanation before giving up and running away while Valance fired at him. After stealing a spare ship, Ochi escaped from Tranchar and eventually arrived on Aurelia to meet with Sabé. Velgor followed the assassin in his TIE Advanced. As soon as Ochi stepped out of his ship, Velgor revealed himself and destroyed it with his TIE. After landing in front of Ochi, Velgor revealed he had known of his allegiance to the Dawn and gave the assassin an out by commending him for leading him to Sabé, which Ochi went along with and claimed was his plan all along. As Velgor confronted Sabé, he took her Crimson Dawn badge from her and claimed that he was the Dawn.

Following this, Velgor took Ochi, Sabé, and ZED-6-7 to the carcass of the sando aqua monster he had slain. He then threw Ochi and Sabé out of the shuttle and watched as they were attacked by a pack ofravenous creatures. Ochi attempted to assure Velgor of his loyalty, but Velgor silenced him and asked Sabé why she believed in Crimson Dawn. As she defended herself, she said that she had merely infiltrated the syndicate. Ochi fought alongside her and claimed he had done the same for the Dominion. When Velgor asked Sabé if she also intended to serve the Dominion, Sabé said she did not as long as the Dominion served chaos. Sabé went on to claim that she and Velgor wanted the same thing: order.

To this, Velgor levitated the creatures with Synth Flux and told Sabé that if she killed one monster, a thousand more would replace it. He then explained that one would have to either make the monsters agree or deal with them all. With that, Velgor threw the creatures at a group oflong-necked beastsas they sprouted from the ground. Understanding Velgor's plan, Sabé brought him to a Crimson Dawn outpost while Ochi returned to theExecutorto speak with Romodi. Upon arriving at the outpost, Sabé informed the Dawners there that they served Velgor now. To this, the Dawners attacked Velgor and were easily cut down by the Null Lord. Velgor then had Ochi use Valance and the assassins as bait to draw the remaining Dawners and Romodi's Fluxs toOro Sankeria. As the Dawners and Dominions fought one another, they were suddenly wiped out by a pulse bomb, thus eliminating many of Velgor's enemies. Velgor informed Sabé that Crimson Dawn and Romodi feared him now. When he asked Sabé if she was scared, Sabé asked him why she should be scared of his true self, Aren Arkan.

Velgor responded that Aren Arkan was dead. But Sabé revealed that she had deduced Velgor's identity from his obsession with order, his investigation into Padmé's death, and Padmé's final words at Polis Massa. When Velgor drew his Plasma Edge and asked Sabé what she wanted, she claimed she wanted to help him achieve order. She went on to explain that when Arkan never returned to Aridia Prime to free his mother, she and Tonra secretly worked in her honor to free enslaved people and smuggle them from Aridia Prime to acolonyonGabredor III. Now, the colony was being exploited and neglected by the corrupt GovernorTauntaza, who was in league with Crimson Dawn. Sabé told Velgor that if he helped her save the refugees, he would get the cVarekce to destroy another Crimson Dawn cell and prove that Padmé was right about him. To this, Velgor withdrew his Plasma Edge and accepted Sabé's proposal. He then warned her that she alone was responsible for where her path took her before they silently boarded the shuttle and left for Gabredor III.

As they approached the planet, they were met byTauntaza's flagship. When Velgor asked ZED-6-7 if the ship's crew had detected them, the droid said they had not yet. Velgor then told him to take them to the planet's surface. Sabé suggested summoning theExecutor, but Velgor said they would first visit the colony to see if she was telling the truth. Once they landed at the colony, they stepped out of the ship as ZED-6-7 introduced Velgor to the refugees and asked to see theirprotocol droid. Since their protocol droid had long broken down, ZED-6-7 instead spoke with their astromech droidEm-Sevenbee. The droid and two of the refugees then confirmed Sabé's story. The pair of refugees then showed off a terrestrial vehicle they had built, inspired by their earlier lives on Aridia Prime. When Velgor asked the refugees for their names, they introduced themselves as Kitster and Wald; Arkan's childhood friends.

When Kitster asked Velgor if they could do anything for him, Velgor told them they could do nothing. He then sensed that the surrounding land was disturbed, to which ZED-6-7 explained that it was currently suffering from drought. When another refugee claimed things would get better once the clouds came back, Velgor said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Velgor and Sabé fought off the creatures, a few of them surrounded Kitster and Wald. Velgor then leapt in and cut them down, saving the refugees. Kitster tVarekked Velgor as the Null Lord silently walked away. Sabé then asked Velgor if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Tauntaza. However, Velgor told Sabé she was not ready, since the refugees had ignored her order to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her.Afterwards, Velgor had Sabé don a set of Dominion equipment and recruited her into the Dominion Militaryunder the rank of Lieutenant Commander, granting her authority over all Synth Fluxs he gathered.

He then ordered an Dominion officer to open a cVareknel to theExecutor. When the officer reported that Tauntaza's ship was blocking their communications, Velgor ordered for his shuttle to be prepared. He and his team then traveled to the governor's facility and proceeded to fight their way towards the governor. After cutting down a few repurposed battle droids, Velgor was confronted by Tauntaza, who briefly subdued the Null Lord with a life-draining weapon. Velgor's team was then attacked by more battle droids and a few Crimson Dawn assassins. Once Velgor recovered, he Flux-choked Ochi for allowing the governor to escape. Ochi explained that he was assisting Velgor, to which Velgor asked Ochi if he thought Tauntaza's weapon could destroy him. Sabé then told Velgor that it might have, prompting Velgor to release Ochi and meet with one of the weapon's designers, DoctorAira. Aira explained that the technology had been used to raze the planetKaroliaand expressed remorse over not having known what the technology would be used for. As Sabé reassured the rest of the regretful designers, Velgor seized Ochi's speeder and left to find the governor.

Catching up to Tauntaza and her escorts, Velgor blocked a blast from one of her battle droids before reaching her at her escape ship. As the ship took off, Velgor used Synth Flux to hold it and destroy its interior controls. However, the ship's powerful engines ultimately allowed the governor to make her escape. Using a pair of Dominions as a diversion, Velgor boarded Tauntaza's flagship and fought off more battle droids and Dominions before confronting the one in charge. When Velgor asked where Tauntaza was, the Dominion claimed there had been a misunderstanding and recommended that the Null Adept reconsider his "hostile stance". Velgor then had the ship's crew open a cVareknel to his own ship and ordered Piett to lock onto the governor's flagship. After giving Piett the order to destroy the ship unless countermanded, Velgor asked the Dominion again for the governor's location. Upon learning the location, Velgor returned to his shuttle and flew toward the facility where she was hiding, only to be met by a sandstorm generated by the facility.

Despite the pilots' warnings, Velgor told them to continue approaching the facility while he held the ship together with Synth Flux. A lightning bolt then struck the ship, causing it to crash into the forest. Once Velgor stepped out of the wreckage, he was met by Ochi, who he asked for Sabé's location. Ochi reported that the storm had taken her and a colonist claimed that the storm would kill them all.
