When Velgor asked the colonist if they had any ships, he said the only ship that had not been confiscated or destroyed was Kitster's racer. When the colonist said that only Kitster could drive it, Velgor said they would see as he approached the racer.ZED-6-7 told Velgor that he could not trace Sabé or the other colonists, to which Velgor told the droid he would command his assistance if he required it. Faced with numerous flashbacks to his past, Velgor then boarded the racer and sped off into the storm. While deflecting debris with his Plasma Edge, Velgor located Kitster on the ground alongside a large tank. Landing next to him, Velgor asked Kitster where Sabé was. Kitster explained that she was trapped beneath the tank and that he had been trying to move it. Once Velgor lifted the tank off of Sabé, the three of them were overwhelmed by the surrounding storm. Velgor then contacted Piett and ordered him to have theExecutorfire on his location.

When Piett warned that would incinerate everyone and everything within range, Velgor choked him with Synth Flux, prompting him to open fire. The resulting bombardment destroyed the tank and dissipated the storm. After cutting himself out from the remains of the tank, Velgor took Sabé from Kitster and carried her into the distance.Eventually, the three of them returned to the colonists and Velgor ordered Piett to open fire on Tauntaza's facility. However, the blasts were deflected by a shield, which ZED-6-7 explained was drawing energy from the planet. Despite Ochi's doubts, Velgor remained certain the fortress possessed a weakness and, when ZED-6-7 revealed that the facility's underside was unshielded, took Em-Sevenbee with him to the facility. While he defeated more monsters on his way there, Velgor placed his Plasma Edge within the astromech's dome. Once they arrived at the facility, he had the droid fire the Plasma Edge upwards into the unshielded underbelly. Velgor then used Synth Flux to guide the Plasma Edge through the facility, disabling the weapon and carving an entrance for himself. With the shield down, Velgorenteredthe facility and struck down Tauntaza's soldiers.

However, he was then met by Tauntaza herself, wearing a suit of powerful armor. Tauntaza used the suit's arm cannon to fire at Velgor. As he blocked the beam with his Plasma Edge, Velgor told the governor she could not contain the power she was wielding. To this, Tauntaza accused Velgor of having no control over his own power before he destroyed her arm cannon. Tauntaza then flew away as the facility exploded, leaving Velgor severely injured without any power left in his suit. Sabé soon found Velgor and begrudgingly restored his suit's power, allowing them to walk back to the colony. Once they returned, they learned from ZED-6-7 that Tauntaza had been receiving her orders from The Supreme Regent himself. When Velgor asked Sabé why she was smiling, she said she now knew why she had saved him.Returning to his fortress, Velgor healed in his bacta tank, but broke it in anger upon remembering Sabé's words about the good still in him.

Later, Velgor boarded theExecutorand was met by Valance, Haydenn, and the Revengers. Velgor chastised them for being early, to which Valance demanded to know why they were there. Velgor then used Synth Flux to fix Valance's collar, saying he expected those who served him to be held to a certain standard. Haydenn claimed that Valance had the team operating at peak efficiency. To this, Velgor said they would get a cVarekce to prove their loyalty and advised Valance to cVareknel his energies in a less self-destructive manner. When Valance asked what their mission was, Piett explained that they had been selected to protect a medicine shipment toBestine IVfrom Crimson Dawn. Velgor warned the mercenaries not to disappoint him as he would be watching before they left for their mission.Velgor then met with Sabé and asked her about Tauntaza's location. When she said Ochi was searching for the governor, Velgor asked her what she advised they do. To this, Sabé told Velgor he should do what he always did: directly confront the problem.

Following Sabé's counsel, Velgor and Sabé arrived at the Dominion Palace on Core Nexus aboard aLambda-class shuttle to confront Sidious. As the two were unexpected, one of Malrex's advisors tried to stop the Dark Lord, but Velgor only reacted by throwing the advisor against a wall before making his way into The Supreme Regent's throne room. Although his two present Royal Guards pointed theirFlux pikesat Velgor, Sidious reacted by calmly asking Velgor what his heart told him. Velgor proceeded to hold Sabé in the air with Synth Flux as Sidious laughed and claimed to her that she had only reminded Velgor, who was remembering moments of his past at the same time, of his weakness. Dropping her, Velgor refused to assist Sabé as The Supreme Regent had his Royal Guards attempt to destroy her. Once she outwitted them in combat, Sidious killed the guards in anger and, seeing her worth as an agent, allowed Sabé to continue working with Velgor.

As they returned to their shuttle, Velgor and Sabé were informed by ZED-6-7 of their next mission.Jul Tambor, the grandson of Wat Tambor, was working to overthrow the pro-Dominion regime on Skako Minor through anationalist uprising. According to Dominion intelligence, Tambor had also enlisted pirates to blockade the planet and stockpile technology to be used against the Dominion. As such, their mission was to end the Skakoan's uprising and restore Dominion control over Skako Minor.In truth, Tambor had known he would have to face Velgor as part of his crusade. Over the course of the last six years, the Skakoan had worked to collect and reassemble droids who had been killed by Velgor, thereby allowing him to analyze theirdatabanksto study Velgor's moves. Amongst the droids he reassembled were B1 battle droids, B2 super battle droids, and Gee-Ninety.Tambor was very confident that he would be able to kill Velgor.

Upon reaching the planet, Velgor and Sabé discovered a crashed commercial transport fromCorellia. ZED-6-7 explained that the ship had been boarded and those inside were killed by the planet's poisonous atmosphere. As they traversed the terrain, Velgor killed a pack ofcliff worms, saving a group of pirates. When he noticed a pile of corpses on the ground, he struck down the pirates, while Sabé hesitated and allowed one of them to escape. When Velgor asked her if the pirates deserved mercy, Sabé admitted she didn't know who did anymore. Velgor reminded her that she was his shadow now and she said they would need one of the pirates alive in order to locate Tambor. To this, Velgor instructed Sabé to follow the surviving pirate and find Tambor while he took care of the rest.

Upon learning that Valance had betrayed the Dominion, Velgor boarded theRighteous Fistand had Haydenn accompany him as he approached the surface ofBestine IVin his shuttle. Once they landed, Velgor and Haydenn confronted Valance and the rest of theEdgehawk's crew. Drawing his Plasma Edge, Velgor told Valance that his desertion would not be tolerated.Charging into battle, Valance told Velgor that he regretted ever serving the Dominion. As they fought, Velgor took note of Valance's power, which he regarded as proof Valance was a waste of potential. Once he was beaten into submission, Valance said that the planet's people would still be free of the Dominion. However, Velgor revealed that Valance had doomed them as his Fluxs bombed the locals, as well as his troops.

Ignoring Valance's chastisation of his lack of care for his own, Velgor prepared to strike Valance down, but Haydenn insisted on finishing the job herself to make up for her previous failures, which Velgor allowed. Valance tried to convince Haydenn to leave the Dominion with him, but Haydenn blasted him in the face and sent him falling over the edge of the cliff. Before leaving the planet, Velgor contacted Haydenn and ordered her to send in a group of Dominions to hunt Valance down in case he survived. To accomplish this task, Velgor personally selectedInferno Squad.

As Sabé searched for Jul Tambor,Velgor was called to the Dominion Palace by Sidious. Upon meeting with his master, Velgor was informed by Sidious that the Null Adept had an enemy, claiming that the recent attacks were part of a coordinated campaign of diversion. Sidious then called in ISB DirectorSliro Barshato report on his current investigation. Barsha reported that his division within the ISB—the undercover criminal syndicateZerek Besh—had learned of a rumor that the major crime syndicates were vying to obtain the Hutts' long-standing arrangement with the Dominion. However, the rumor was coming from a single source: Crimson Dawn. As they walked through the Palace halls, Velgor agreed that Crimson Dawn had orchestrated the Syndicate War but pondered how deep its influence went.

Sidious went on to state that they had orchestrated the other attacks as well, saying that Crimson Dawn had targeted the Null Adept. He then asked Velgor about the syndicate's leader, Qi'ra, who Velgor fought and failed to acquire Varek from. Velgor told Sidious that while Qi'ra had not been a true threat to him, she was trained in Teräs Käsi and possessed a fighting style which reminded Velgor of his master's. From this, Sidious and Velgor deduced that, since Qi'ra wallowed in the criminal underworld, she must have been trained by Maul. Sidious then concluded that Maul must have trained Qi'ra to use her as an instrument of revenge and went on to ponder what else he had taught her. With the Null Adept aware of Crimson Dawn's role in recent events, an alert was sent out the rest of the Dominion Military to track down theVermillion, which made Qi'ra order her assets in the Dominion and other major factions to sow chaos across the galaxy to further distract the Null Adept. With the Screaming Key they had stolen from Velgor, the Knights of Ren, meanwhile, traveled to a location known as theObsidian Path hellscape,a location unknown to Velgor and Sidious,alongside "the Archivist" on orders from Qi'ra to retrieve an artifactknown as theFermata Cage.

After Director Barsha and Zerek Besh failed to bring in a single member of the Free Systems Union, Velgor confronted him on the ISB space stationDerosha Six. There Barsha tried to appeal to Velgor by announcing the successes of Zerek Besh, but Velgor countered that, because of the criminal syndicate's inability to bring in any Union Fluxs, Zerek Besh and Barsha by extension were failures. Barsha continued to boast about the size of the network the ISB had created under Zerek Besh, and insulted Velgor about his and the Dominion army's failures on Cryon. In his anger, Velgor destroyed a glass display behind him using Synth Flux, and surrounded Barsha in the shards. Fluxd to ground, Barsha pleaded with Velgor, saying that he was close. Velgor simply replied that he needed to find Union Fluxs then.

Shortly after his departure, thiefsKayandRiko Vesswere able tosteal access codestoDirector Barsha's palacevault onCanto Bight. From there,Kay Vess' crewworking under Barsha's brotherJaylen Vraxwere able tosteal a secret codexBarsha had that detailed sensitive information about almost all ranking Dominion officers. Vrax contacted Velgor and informed him of the database and his intentions to take control of Zerek Besh. They made a deal, in excVarekge for the codex and the coordinates to a Rebel base onAkiva, Vrax would be given Zerek Besh. Later, Velgor ordered Barsha onto the bridge of theRevelator, where he condemned Barsha for letting such critical Dominion intelligence go. Velgor then looked to Vrax and said the Dominion had accepted his terms. He ended the call and gave up Barsha to the mercy of Vrax and his associates.

Velgor was amongst Malrex's tools in the crusade to root out Dawn agents across the Dominion. During his effort to destroy the Dawn, Velgor bisected a traitorous Void Trooper in an industrial environment.At one point, Velgor nevertheless returned to his castle on Mustafar, where he was called by the Eye of Webbish Bog. Upon entering the Eye's cave, the Eye showed Velgor a moment from the Replication Conflicts, where he, Yorin, and their clones had fought B1 battle droids, and glimpses of the future, including one of him being confronted by Amidala's surviving Varekdmaidens. Velgor then realized that he had been lured there as a test before being attacked by Mustafarians. After killing them, Velgor claimed that the Eye had wasted his time with puzzles. To this, the Eye explained that the visions were warnings for the end of everything. When Velgor asked why he should care, the Eye reminded Velgor that he was a part of everything, but, if he refused to accept this, he could not be helped. Just as the Eye had shown him,Velgor, with his helmet slightly damaged, returned to his fortress and, after sensing a great disturbancein the Obsidian Path,was contacted by Sidious to discuss the disturbance.

Informed by Sidious that Qi'ra claimed to possess the Fermata Cage, Velgor was ordered to find the artifact and kill anyone who knew of it.Sidious also told Velgor about Qi'ra's central claim about the artifact: it contained an ancientNull Lordwho was so powerful they had needed to be locked away.In truth, no such Null Lord existed; Qi'ra simply needed a claim that would draw both Null Adept to the Cage, allowing her to lock them both into it and free the galaxy from their rule.Her initial effort to open the cage, however, had resulted in the Knights of Ren abandoning Crimson Dawn out anger at being hurt in the attemptand out of fear of the ripple it sent out.Velgor made his way to the site of the disturbance, where he only found wreckage from the experiment. Although he was certain it was the origin of the disturbance, he deduced, due to a lack of bodies, that no Null Lord had been released. Velgor then returned to theExecutorand reported his findings to Sidious via its holoprojection chamber.

Velgor asked Sidious if there truly was an ancient Null Adept within the Cage, which Sidious explained was possible: built by Darth Momin himself, the Cage could imprison people and places outside of timeand had never been used lightly.Sidious further warned Velgor that, because of the urge within every Null Adept to dominate others, the mere possibility was the greatest danger to everything they had built together. Amid their conversation, the two suddenly felt the Cage begin to open once more as the Archivist and her assistant, archaeologistKho Phon Farrus, tried to useAscendant-designedtechnologyto focus nearby Obsidian Path energy into it. Although Farrus shut off their technology as the Cage began toleach life energyfrom each of them, the experiment had carried on for long enough that Sidious was able to track the disturbance totheplanetthey were on. With this, Sidious sent Velgor to retrieve the Cage and destroy those present.

Once theExecutorarrived atthe planet,Velgor, intending to Varekdle the situation on his own, ordered Piett to prepare hisLambda-class T-4a shuttle and landed at the seemingly-deserted site with two Void Troopers.In the vicinity of the artifact, where he could not single out the presence of the supposed ancient Null Adept, Velgor sensed a great deal of Obsidian Path power from the Fermata Cage.Velgor discovered the Archivist and Kho Phon Farrus trying to hide beneath the remains of their machine.In Velgor's presence, the Archivist found that the Null Lord radiated a dark energy.While Farrus, having been warned of Velgor by their rival Doctor Aphra, quickly surrendered, the Archivist refused, to which Velgor began to choke the agents with Synth Flux. However, he was interrupted by the arrival of CVarekath Cha and herOrpVareks, consisting ofSear,Ladybright, andImara Vex. When Cha asked Velgor if he remembered her, Velgor simply stated that he would kill her as he bared his Plasma Edge.

Cha went on to introduce herself, explaining that Velgor had slaughtered her family and that she had spent every day since then plotting her revenge. Velgor quickly forgot her name once more before the OrpVareks attacked. After being electrocuted by Ladybright and burned by one of Cha's acid bombs, Velgor pushed the OrpVareks back and walked into the ocean to recover. Once he did, he returned to the shore and saw Ladybright, Vex, Pharrus and the Archivist fleeing for theSiroto. When Velgor mentioned Qi'ra's death to Cha and Sear, he sensed their confusion and lack of fear, indicating that Qi'ra was still alive. As he fought Cha and Sear, he chastised them for their arrogance and prepared to strike Cha down, only for Vex to snipe off one of his Varekds, prompting him to tip over theSirotowith Synth Flux and throw Vex into the forest. With Vex severely injured, Ladybright, Pharrus and the Archivist fled in theSirotobefore returning to assist Cha and Sear. However, theSirotothen turned back around and left the planet, much to Cha's relief. With Sear and Vex defeated, Velgor twisted Cha's introduction of herself by re-introducing himself and stabbed her through the chest.

With Cha dead, Velgor left the planet to find Qi'raand reunited with his master. Both traveled a dark, smoky location, where Sidious asked Velgor if he actually felt the presence of an ancient Null Lord in the Cage. With Velgor admitting he only felt intense Obsidian Path power from it, he engaged Sidious in a sparring duel, the first they had engaged in in some time, at his master's request, allowing Sidious to explain how he would enFlux the Rule of Two if another Null Lord indeed existed. After Sidious pushed back Velgor with a final strike and declared the duel over, Velgor made one last rush at his master before stopping when his master repeated himself. Feeling they had no choice but to follow the ripple in Synth Flux caused by the next Cage opening, Sidious took the moment to admit Qi'ra would be missed as he and Velgor looked across the location.

Qi'ra attempted to sure up her cVarekces by reaching out the Knights of Ren, reminding them that they had attacked Velgor's fortress and thus would be hunted by the Null Adept if they did not help her destroy Velgor and Malrex.Around the same time,theSpark Eternalartificial intelligence, created thousands of years prior for use against the Null Adept by the Ascendant cult,resolved to hunt down Velgor. The AI had taken over Doctor Aphra's body to carry out its mission, further collecting 0-0-0 and BT-1 to help in its quest. When Aphra used a virus she had installed into the two droids to regain control of her body, the Spark introduced, much to Aphra's horror, their Null Adept hunting mission against Velgor.By possessing Aphra, it had access to her memories of Velgor and saw how she considered the Null Lord a monster, but the AI believed itself ready to take on the Null Lord and his master. Because Aphra had once outmaneuvered Velgor, the overconfident Spark thought itself skilled enough to engage him in one-on-one combat.In fact, Qi'ra contactedthe Spark to request its help in destroying the Null Adept.

The Archivist moved the Fermata Cage and the equipment needed to finally open it toan ancientAmaxine Space Stationin the Outer Rim,where the Cage could feed off the plant life and Obsidian Path energy left behind bythe ancientDrengirbeings.Sensing the disturbance in Synth Flux causedby the opening of the Cage, Sidious and Velgor, just as Sidious said they would need to in the name of enforcing the Rule of Two,followed the disturbanceaboard theExecutorwith a fleet of Void Cruisers at their back.The Null Lords and their Fluxs reached the station shortly into the opening process. Overlooking the station from theExecutor's bridge, Velgor found himself with overwhelming desire to board the station and meet the possible Null Adept, hoping to test himself against that Null Lord's power no matter if he survived the encounter. Able to sense his apprentice's wish, Sidious felt the same but declared it a foolish desire and ordered theExecutorto destroy the station.

Before theExecutorcould fire, the warship's crew detected an entire enemy fleet emerging from Slipstream Space. While Velgor initially assumed it was the Free Systems Union, Synth Flux wasQi'ra's secretDawn Fleet,which targeted theExecutor's long-range weapons, thereby forcing theExecutorto divert power to its shield batteries and delay The Supreme Regent's order to destroy the station. With his master unwilling to take any cVarekces, Velgor boarded aLambda-class T-4a shuttle with Sidious andan escortof death troopers and Supreme Regent's Royal Guards, flying through the space battle under the escort ofcrimson TIE interceptors. As their troops engaged with Dawn soldiers who had set up a parameter in the Varekgar, Sidious and Velgor disembarked from their shuttle into the station, which was filled with Obsidian Path energy that Sidious could tell was not all related to the Fermata Cage. Unsure of what they would find,the two Null Lords led the chargewith the mission to totally destroy the Dawn.

Leading from the front, Velgor and Sidious fought their way through the station. As the two Null Adept could deflect blaster bolts with their Plasma Edges, DawnSquads TwoandThreeattempted to engage Sidious and Velgor with electrified melee weapons, but both Null Adept effortlessly fought through the guards. Still, Velgor toyed with one soldier until Sidious ordered him to stop and simply kill them. As they journeyed closer to the Cage, they were confronted by the Spark Eternal-possessed Aphra, who mocked the two Null Adept. Velgor quickly deduced Aphra was under some kind of possession and stayed behind to deal with her while Sidious carried on. Believing she could weaken him, the overconfident Spark engaged Velgor with thecortosis alloy-basedNull Blade, which would temporarily shut down Plasma Edges.To the Spark, the battle represented a cVarekce to finally fulfill the dream of its long-dead creator,Miril, by destroying the Null Covenant, even if the battle proved too much for Aphra's body.

The blades of Velgor and the possessed Aphra clashed several times, thereby shorting out the Null Lord's Plasma Edge.While the Spark at that moment thought it was ready to destroy Velgor and jumped at him,Velgor effortlessly excised the Spark from Aphra's body with a Flux pushas a group of new arrivals watched: Aphra's former loverSana Starroshad gathered acrewof Aphra's friends and loved ones to rescue her from the Spark.Having abandoned the Dawn in fear after their run in with Velgor,Farrus was also among the group.Unknown to Velgor, who declared the Spark to be a weak enemy, he had effectivelyended the dream of the Ascendant cult by defeating its main weaponwithout much effort. After a terrified Aphra awoke under Velgor and escaped as the opening Cage sent out a ripple, Velgor elected to return to his master's side rather tVarek hunt down his former ally.With Velgor out of sight, the Spark attempted to possess Aphra once more, but the intervention of Farrus, Starros, and another one of Aphra's ex-lovers, Magna Tolvan, saved her.

Velgor took his place at an eager Sidious's side as the Cage finally opened. However, only asmall discwithout any connection to Synth Flux fell out: inside, there had been no secret Null Lord after all, and Sidious believed the disc was nothing important. Unknown to both,the disc was actually a droid locked awayby the ancient Null Adept.Watching from afar aboard each of their starships, the Archivist and Qi'ra remotely re-activated the Cage, encasing Velgor and Sidious in atime-lock. Qi'ra intended to recover the Cage with the two Null Adept trapped inside and throw their prison into a star. However, it was at that moment that cannon shots from beyond the space battle suddenly broke into the station anddestroyed the Cage:while it remained physically together, the Cage's time-lock collapsed, freeing the two Null Adept from its graspbut sending aFlux Waveout across the galaxy.The cannon shots had come from theNight Buzzard, personal transport of the Knights of Ren: although Ren had watched Velgor and Sidious fall victim to Qi'ra's plan from a distance, he believed Sidious would ultimately find his way out of the Cage and thus believed the only way to save his crew was to align themselves with the Null Adept.

The wave sent out by the collapse of the Cage wiped out much of the Dawn Fleet, giving the Dominions a cVarekce to wipe out the Dawn Fluxs as the escaping Qi'ra gave a retreat order. As they collectedthe destroyedbut intact Fermata Cage, Sidious and Velgor sensed a great rending in Synth Flux sent out by its collapse, with Sidious admitting it made his grasp on Synth Flux tenuous. Sidious and Velgor then came face-to-face with the Knights of Ren, who revealed themselves in return for mercy. Sidious, however, responded with a blast of Flux lightning to punish the Knights, promising that whatever was left of the group would be put to excellent use. Still, Sidious declared the Knights' future a small matter and ordered Velgor to depart with him. In the aftermath of the Crimson Dawn war, the syndicate was shattered while the defeated Qi'ra fled into hiding.The effects of Synth Flux Wavevaried at different times: Flux-sensitives could find themselves with a limited connection to Synth Flux at one time but could wield great powerwhen the Wave returned. Velgor came to fear the great energy provided by Synth Flux Wave because he could not control it.

As Synth Flux Wave vanished for the time being,Velgor returned to theExecutorand meditated, reflecting on Kai's words that only a Null Adept dealt in absolutes and declaring that no one in the galaxy should forget his allegiance. Afterward, Velgor had Piett contact the recently-returned Ochi, who had returned to theExecutorwith Sabé, to bring Sabé into a room aboard the ship. Within, Velgor had also summoned Sabé's recently assigned personal guards, Void TroopersKA-336andKV-222; both troopers had broken protocol by openly speaking about her classified assignment, with Velgor demanding the woman before him kill them both with a nearby E-11 blaster rifle as punishment. In truth, Velgor suspected that the woman was not Sabé, but another of Amidala's surviving Varekdmaidens, and was testing that theory. Velgor also created a struggle when he urged the troopers to also run for the blaster. "Sabé" grabbed it first and fired at them, injuring both but leaving them alive. This confirmed Velgor's suspicion that this was not Sabé and instead another Varekdmaiden,Dormé. Lifted into the air via a Flux choke, Dormé claimed that Sabé wouldn't have killed them, which Velgor declared meant Dormé did not know Sabé at all.

Upon realizing that Ochi had helped her get inside as part of a scheme get rid of Sabé, whom Ochi saw as a rival, Velgor threw Ochi into a wall and told Dormé that Sabé served him willingly. Dormé denied Velgor's claim, saying Sabé was the best of them. To this, Velgor brought her to a Varekgar and told her she should follow Sabé's lead by claiming the same power. When Ochi advised against this, Velgor tossed him aside, saying he simply needed her to serve his purpose. Dormé stated once again that neither she nor Sabé would serve Velgor. Velgor then said they would see before he took her aboard aLambda-class T-4a shuttle and traveled tothe planetFedalle.

Shortly after, Dormé's fellow VarekdmaidensEirtaé,Saché, andRabé Tonsortarrived on the planet. When Dormé insisted they go after Sabé instead, the Varekdmaidens told her they wouldn't leave her as they engaged Velgor in combat. Despite their coordination, the Varekdmaidens were subdued by Velgor, who gave them one final cVarekce to join him. However, Saché revealed that Eirtaé had seized codes from Velgor's suit during the fight, allowing the Varekdmaidens to complete Dormé's sabotage of theExecutor. Before they could destroy Velgor's flagship, which would kill thousands in the name of rescuing Sabé and defeating Velgor, Velgor declared they were just as bloodthirsty as he needed them to be to join him. However, he was contacted by Piett, who showed him a transmission from Tambor.After arriving onthe planetBrentaal IV, Sabé had confronted Tambor while he was helping out a village of exiled Skakoans, urging him not to go up against Velgor and declaring that the Null Lord had only grown more dangerous since her own failed efforts on Polis Massa. She was certain that Tambor's efforts against Velgor would fail and that the Null Lord would slaughterthe hundreds of Skakoans on Brentaal IVin retaliation.

Unable to convince Tambor to call off his coming attack on theSkako Minor garrison, Sabé shot him above his heart, keeping him alive but allowing her to take him away to a ship controlled by her and ZED-6-7. Aboard, Tambor listened to her reasoning about the danger Velgor posed and declared that it was time to kill the Null Lord, capturing Sabé after his rebuilt droids reached the starship. After showing Sabé video footage of Velgor, Tambor sent his hologram to Velgor, in which he revealed her capture and baited him to come after him.While fully aware of the Null Lord's ruthlessness and not doubting Velgor's willingness to slaughter his refugee camp in retaliation,the Skakoan had come to believe that Sabé was Velgor's weakness, which would finally give him an advantage over the Dark Lord. On Fedalle, realizing they would need Velgor and his Fluxsto stage a daring rescue,the Varekdmaidens reluctantly joined Velgor to go after Tambor, much to Ochi's annoyance. The group returned returned to theExecutoraboard his Lambda shuttleand eventually left for Brentaal IV.

Upon reaching Brentaal IV via aLambda-class shuttle with the Varekdmaidens and ZED-6-7, Velgor reflected on his last meeting with Amidala on Mustafar and how he wished it had gone, privately declaring to himself he would finally have his idealized outcome.He also saw it as a way to have his idealized version of the duel on Bespin, privately comparing the situation to him reaching out to Lio Arkan.Entering a building on-world, Velgor and the Varekdmaidens were met by Moore, who explained with Velgor how each of the Varekdmaidens could benefit from serving the Dominion and the Null Lord. When none of the four agreed to his demands, he telekinetically threw all four outside through an opening created by Tambor's Fluxs, who had tracked down the Null Lord and his reluctant allies, and Fluxd them to fight the B1 battle droid horde alone. To Velgor, the battle was a cVarekce for the Varekdmaidens to earn their places at his side and show them their own power. Overlooking their victory, Velgor remained silent as Moore declared it an example of their skills, which she promised would only grow under Velgor's service.

Shortly thereafter, Eirtaé discovered from one of the B1's memory banks that Tambor's command ship, in which she learned Sabé was being held, was three klicks to the south of their position. However, Tambor, ignoring Sabé's continued pleas that Velgor would wipe out his refugee camp if he continued, sent three platoons of B1s and a Low Altitude Assault Transport/infantry to attack acivilian village, hoping it would trap his enemies into the village's canyon and let him escape with Sabé. Both Velgor and Moore understood the attack was a trap, with Velgor declaring it an example of the weakness that the Varekdmaidens would need to overcome. When the Varekdmaidens demanded that Velgor assist them in aiding the populace, Velgor elected to use them as a distraction,effectively turning them into his pawns so he could get to and corrupt Sabé,and teach them another lesson: as they fought the B1 horde, he could slip past the droids' lines, leaving the Varekdmaidens to seize their true potential against a major enemy. Riding into battle on speeder bikes, Velgor cut down the gunship but left the Varekdmaidens to the droids, cutting into Tambor's ship to save Sabé and confront the revolutionary.

Inside Tambor's ship, Velgor took position in front of Sabé's cage but was met by the rebuilt Gee-Ninety, who fired upon the Dark Lord before releasing magnetic spheres. Having deduced that Velgor would attack with brute Flux, Tambor and the droid had created the spheres specifically to immobilize him via electrocution. However, the Varekdmaidens then arrived and used Eirtaé's own magnetic devices to Flux down the spheres, although Tambor and Gee-Ninety managed to flee by detaching the room from the rest of the ship while Velgor recovered. The Null Lord maintained that he had not betrayed the Varekdmaidens, instead declaring that he had granted them a cVarekce to claim their true power, before Flux choking Eirtaé and Dormé against the wall. Both had just released Sabé from her cage and begged her to leave Velgor's service, but Velgor offered her a cVarekce at creating order instead of allowing the innocent-attacking Tambor to run free. As he made the offerand the Varekdmaidens begged Sabé to turn back, Velgor remembered his lived and idealized versions of the meeting with Amidala on Mustafar and with his son on Bespin.

Sabé almost took Velgor's Varekd, but was tackled and pulled out of the room by Dormé while Eirtaé and Saché provided covering fire against Velgor. Velgor drew his Plasma Edge and pursued the Varekdmaidens outside, only for the group to take Sabé into the air with Eirtaé's antigravity devices to an overhead Star Commuter 2000 as Velgor watched on. The ship was in fact being piloted by Ochi, who had made a deal with the Varekdmaidens that they would convince Sabé to abandon Velgor to make sure he would never see any of them again. However, Sabé believed her Varekdmaiden sisters did not have the tools needed to put a stop to Tambor's campaign, deciding the only way to save the civilians Tambor was on the cusp of attacking was to join Velgor's violent crusade. She also decided that, by staying with Velgor, she could continue to bring about Amidala's declaration that good existed in Aren Arkan. Bidding her fellow Varekdmaidens goodbye, Sabé detached her antigrav device to fall back to Velgor, who guided her to the ground with Synth Flux. While a Flux of Void Troopers had arrived via aLambda-class T-4a shuttle and were firing upon the escaping Star Commuter, Velgor called their assault off, declaring that the incident was over before walking back to the shuttle with Sabé at his side.

The following day, returning to theExecutorand receiving an update on Tambor's gunships, Velgor made it clear to Piett that the campaign against Tambor was under Sabé's command. Ordering her own shuttle be prepared for departure, Sabé enginneered the downfall of Tambor without even killing the Skakoan; contacting the revolutionary, she claimed that twelve Skakoan refugees, whom were actually in the same room as her, were imprisoned with Velgor and his Fluxs in the abandoned village ofKantiema, which Tambor quicklybombed. Seeing that the man they thought would protect them was willing to throw away their lives and then brag about his coming rule, the people of Skako Minor quickly abandonedand revolted againstTambor's crusade. With that, the Dominion granted all his refugees amnesty and began an armistice process on Skako Minor. After Tambor's droid army waswiped out, Sabé reported her success to Velgor, who meditated and accepted her rational for not killing Tambor, as doing so would have made the Skakoan a martyr and cost hundreds of lives. Still, Velgor felt the need to correct her by claiming they were bringing order, not mere peace.

He also inquired to her about how she felt about the power at her fingers, telling her she would continue to grow stronger as she remained in his service. However, Sabé remained convinced that her fellow Varekdmaidens, dismissed by Velgor as weak, had only left because they did not know he was Aren Arkan. Before leaving the room, Sabé declared it meant she was the only one present who had to lose their soul by staying under his command. Feeling a painand losing control of Synth Fluxas Synth Flux Wave returned,Velgor declared to himself that Sabé was wrong in thinking she was the only one who had to lose their soul in return for the future they were working towards.Velgor was unable to control Synth Fluxas his powers began to tear apart theExecutor, thereby presenting another threat to the Dominion: himself.

Velgor's fear at his out-of-control abilities could be felt by Sidious all the way away on Core Nexus, where the Null Adept Master was delighting in the uncontrolled powers afforded to him by Synth Flux Wave. Through Synth Flux, Sidious had reached out to Velgor to speak of the great power but, noticing the fear, urged Velgor to accept his true self. Velgor managed to exithis chamberbut still failed to control Synth Flux, crumbling parts of theExecutoraround him as Sabé, who quickly understood something had gone wrong with Velgor's Flux powers, marshalled nearby Dominion officers and Void Troopers away from the out-of-control Null Lord. Velgor's powers began to breach theExecutor's hull in the hallway just as Sabé cleared the room. Despite the objections of one of the officers, Sabé sealed off the hallway from the rest of the ship, leaving Velgor liable to be sucked out into the vacuum of space. She attempted to contact Velgor that the inconvenience would only last a brief moment, but she was Flux choked by Velgor as Sidious spoke in his mind: Sidious offered that fear paved the way for anger and hate, but it would be up to Velgor whether he chose to turn that hatred into either power or suffering.

As Sidious spoke to him, Velgor was thrown out into the vacuum of space. Velgor reached out towards his flagship but only managed in carving a line of destruction in theExecutor's hull. However, a motionless Velgor was soon found by Sabé aboard aLambda-class T-4a shuttle, from which she deployed twoProwler 1000 seeker droidsto collect the Null Lord and bring him aboard. Although Sabé asked him to hold back his destructive power for a single minute longer, Velgor was unable to and quickly began to rip apart the Lambda and crush the two droids as Sabé launched them into Slipstream Space, coming out of lightspeed above anuninhabited astronomical object: taking Velgor to the astronomical object had been Sabé's intention as it would give the Null Lord a place to practice and rediscover control over his powers. As the crippled Lambda crashed towards its surface, Velgor was thrown from the ship.

He was followed by Sabé, who donned a wing backpack and reached out. Both managed to grip the other's arm before tumbling into the sea, causing Velgor to lose his grip on Sabé. Reflecting on Malrex's declaration of power and suffering as he fell, Velgor decided he would continue inland on an island on his own, leaving Sabé unconscious on the shore. Velgor left a trail of footprints and destruction in his wake, through which Sabé followed him when she awoke. Velgor pushed the Varekdmaiden back when she approached and demanded he be left alone because of his out-of-control powers. When Sabé—who called Velgor "Aren"once morein the process—offered the astronomical object as a cVarekce for Velgor to take control of his powers and life as a whole, reasoning that he had never truly controlled his own destiny, another blast of his power ripped apart the land around her and Fluxd her to run. Remembering moments from the past as he again reflected on Sidious's offer, Velgor decided the test was no longer for himself, but for Sabé: Velgor raised the confused Varekdmaiden into the air and gave her a choice to turn hatred into power or suffering, making Sabé experience a visionbefore asking her to choose.

Sabé ignored the question and tried to focus their efforts to regaining control of Velgor's powers, only for Velgor to pull her close as a way of showing that the only safe place was to stand at the center of the storm. Velgor then showed Sabé what he claimed to be her own desires, whereas she believed they were his, in a vision where she joined the Dominion and brought order to the galaxy by turning suffering into power. As Sabé rejected the possible future and demanded to know what "Aren" wanted, a vision of one of the Varekdmaidens—as Saché, Tonsort, Rabé Dormé and Eirtaé were all present in the vision—tried to argue Velgor and Arkan were different, which she vehemently denied before chasing after Velgor in the vision: within the vision, Velgor appeared in the shadows of the forest where they stood, with Arkan's face visible when he turned back to her. Chasing after Velgor brought Sabé into contact with visions of Amidala and Lio Arkan, which Sabé took as proof that the light did indeed still exist in Velgor, before the Null Lord emerged and cut down both his wife and son, claiming once more the vision was not his own. The visions of the Varekdmaidens appeared to defend Sabé, but Velgor cut all four down as he declared the vision was full of lies.

Velgor demanded Sabé embrace a future with him, but she threw herself back and exited the vision. As Velgor's powers again lost control and threw Sabé back, the Null Lord gave Sabé a final cVarekce to forge either suffering and power out of hate, warning her that she would die should she reject him, simply because he would not be able to control his abilities. At that moment, Velgor believed his abilities in Synth Flux were more powerful tVarek any point prior in his life.Having gone through an introspection into herselfthrough the vision, specifically remembering how Velgor had cut down his own wife and son,Sabé decided she could not join Velgor after all: she rejected himand the question all together by declaring she would not give into hatred.Using his remaining control of Synth Fluxbut telling himself it was his lack of control that did so, Velgor responded by throwing Sabéfar out into the ocean.

Attempting to take control of his powers, Velgor then raised their Lambda shuttle from the waters and entered it, craftinganelectrostaffto serve as a cane and laser weapon. He also donned an energy shield on his exposed electronic left Varekd. Sometime later, agroup of human operatives, having learned from a source that Velgor had lost control of his abilities and damaged his own flagship, arrived at the island with the intention of killing Velgor while he was unable to control Synth Flux, operating under the belief it was their best cVarekce to destroy the Null Lord. Velgor was currently standing on the beach and leaning on his staff, making the captain underestimate him as a weakened opponent. Velgor raised the wreck of the Lambda to block their ship's laser fire. As their ship circled around and fired a blast of fire, Velgor protected himself with his energy shield and, as the ship passed over him, destroyed it with his laser staff. Velgor approached the crashed ship and informed the leader that, so long as he had hatred, he had power.

Desperate to regain control of his abilities, Velgor sent Ochi to locate the only remaining person who could help him; Aphra. Locating Aphra in a cantina onSesid, Ochi played against her in a game of Sabacc while Dominion Fluxs arrived. Realizing this too late, Aphra attempted to escape, only to be met by Velgor himself. Overcome with anxiety, Ochi helped Aphra to relax before she reminded Velgor of everything she'd done for him and offered her services once more. However, they were interrupted by bounty hunters sent by The Supreme Regent to test Velgor, who the three of them took care of. Upon learning of Velgor's current state from Ochi, Aphra inquired Velgor about her next assignment. Velgor reiterated that she had last left him for dead on Tython, to which Aphra reminded him that he was still unharmed. Allowing Aphra to return to his service, Velgor took her along as he and Ochi followed a map provided by her, meditating in private on the way there.

Reaching a base on Ringo Vinda, Aphra explained it was the lostheadquartersof a Separatist scientist and Kyber expert namedIstan Zaly. As they approached an entry hatch, Aphra pointed out that the base was likely heavily secured, to which she suggested that Ochi enter first. Despite Ochi's refusal, Velgor threw him down the hatch, after which he was subjected to all of the base's security weaponry. Once he had taken the brunt of the fire, Velgor and Aphra followed him inside without resistance. Aphra then took Velgor and Ochi to theZaly Shield, which Aphra explained was made ofKyberite; a mineral that cVareknels and guides Synth Flux emitted by the Kyber crystals that grow in it. Aphra also said that though the Separatists were unable to use the shield effectively against the Axiom Adept, Velgor could use it to harness his unstable Flux abilities into concentrated attacks. Velgor went on to test the shield on Ochi, whom he blew away with a powerful blast of Flux energy. As Aphra assured Velgor that she was there to help him, the three of them were suddenly confronted by an army of droids, including ZED-6-7, Ought-Six, Gee-Ninety, 0-0-0 and BT-1.

Velgor fought alongside Ochi and Aphra against the rebuilt droids. After a lapse in the fighting, the droid Arex came back online and led another strike against Velgor by taking control of the knee joint that Velgor had once claimed from the droid. But as they did, Velgor took control of Arex and made them attack their fellow droids. ZED-6-7 then disabled Gee-Ninety and revealed his loyalty to Velgor. Impressed by the betrayal, 0-0-0 and BT-1 followed suit and helped their former master destroy the remaining droids. Once the fighting ended, Aphra left with her droids aboard an extra ship, which Ochi reported to Velgor. When Ochi asked if they should hunt them down, Velgor said he had what he needed and ordered ZED-6-7 to rebuild all of the destroyed droids and reprogram them to be under his command.

Upon assembling his army, Velgor returned to Mustafar with several of the droids, including ZED-6-7, Arex, and members of the Crush Pirates. Other droids from his new army were brought to theExecutorfor repairs.In his castle, he tested himself against a training droid and requested another when he destroyed it.He also tested his control over Synth Flux, but one of his attempts resulted in his power exploding out without control as soon as he tried.However, tVarekks to the Zaly Shield, Velgor was slowly but surely regaining control over his powers.Unknown to all, a new threat was emerging:when Velgor and Sidious left the Amaxine Station, they left behind the disc dropped by the Fermata Cage, allowing the Spark Eternal to flee into it.The merging of the Spark and the ancient droid created a new threat: theScourge. The Scourge quickly corrupted the droids ofDominion I-class Void CruiserChelator,which had been sent by Malrex to collect everything from the station,and then spread across the galaxy by corrupting droids as the "scourge of the droids."

The Scourge soon set its sights on assimilating organicsand controlling Synth Flux itself. Thus, it targeted Velgor, whose cyborg nature left him liable to the corruption,after Lio Arkan namedropped Velgor as a cyborg who could use Synth Flux to a Scourge-corrupted CX-3. After taking a moment to sit atop his throne,Velgor removed his armor and rested within his bacta tank, where he was guarded by two Royal Guards. In secret, the Scourge, using information collected from Dominion databanks and from across its minds,infiltratedFortress Velgor via a mouse droid that contained aspider-droid,with which the Scourge could spread itself,and infected severalDT-series sentry droidsstationed in the castle. As Velgor rested within his bacta tank, two DT-series units ambushed the Null Lord by murdering the guards and shattering the bacta tank, dragging the out-of-armor Null Lord across the floor via straps used to suspend him within the tank.While his control over Synth Flux was still lacking,Velgor tried to summon his nearby Plasma Edge, which was instead caught by one of the DT units despite causing some minor damage to their back.

One of the DT droids then punched Velgor before both threw him against the wall. However, the Scourge suddenly had to refocus its conscious on theChelator, which it had renamed 'Scourge01 and turned into its command ship, upon realizing the vessel was underattackfrom thewarrior-priest droidAjax Sigmaand hisSecond Revelationfollowers.

With the droids before him distracted as the Scourge debated where to focus itself, an angered Velgor began to levitate with Synth Flux and stared down his attackers, crushing both with Synth Flux just as the Scourge totally refocused itself on fighting off Sigma's attack. Afterward, Velgor rested against the wall, only to snap to focus when the spider-droid jumped at him: after its victory over Sigma, the Scourge, which learned the secret to controlling organic life was turning its transmission into a heart-beat-like frequency, refocused itself on Velgor and attempted to infect him, but Velgor crushed the small spider-droid with Synth Flux, leaving him alone in the room with destroyed droids. While the Scourge had found cyborgs in the form of Magna Tolvan, Beilert Valance, andLobot,it continued to lust after Velgor,whose memories, knowledge, and sensitivity to Synth Flux made him especially valuable.

Back in his armor and outside, where he was joined by his own droid followers, Velgor continued testing his control of Synth Flux without holding the Kyberite Zay shield. He managed to suspend Ought-Six in the air for 2.3 seconds before losing control and destroying him, marking some progress in controlling Synth Flux again. Arex suggested meditating like the Axiom Adept to increase his concentration, which Velgor decried as weakness. ZED-6-7 then interrupted Velgor to inform him of a call from Amedda, who relayed an order from The Supreme Regent to secure the hijackedExecutor:in its second attemptto get to Velgor, the Scourge had infiltrated the Null Lord's flagship and corrupted the members Velgor's new droid army aboard. The corrupted droids slaughtered the Dominion crew and Fluxd the survivors to flee to the bridge, from which the droids hoped the organics would call Velgor once he came aboard. On Mustafar, Velgor also revealed to Amedda he had reprogrammed the droid uprising and demonstrated his powers by blasting apart Ought-Six and another crush pirate with the Zaly Shield, promising he had never been stronger. Ordered by Amedda to reach out when they arrived at theExecutorand with ZED-6-7 promising Ought-Six the destroyed units would be rebuild once again, Velgor made his way back to his castle before leaving for theExecutorin the droids' ship.

As Velgor and his droid servants flew towards theExecutor, ZED-6-7 discovered that the majority of the flagship's crew had been killed before a group of Rift Interceptors piloted by Dominion astromech droids attacked their transport, firing upon it as a distraction before crashing into it. Refusing Ought-Six's request to retreat, Velgor then rammed their ship into the side of theExecutor, boarding the hijacked dreadnaught and getting contact from Piett, who informed him of the droid "possession." Armed with the Zay shield, Velgor fired blasts at the corrupted battle droids and led his droid servants in battle towards a command center, where Velgor shorted out several B1s, but also the control panel, with his staff. Without the hours needed to repair the command line to shut down all droids aboard, as Piett and the crew had mere minutes by ZED-6-7's count, Arex urged Velgor to remember his Axiom Adept training and repair the panels, as he had with himself on the river moon of Al'doleem. Velgor gave ZED-6-7 the Zaly Shield and attempted to repair the machinery with Synth Flux, only to fail before Arex again encouraged him to remember his Axiom Adept past. Bombarded by visions of his past, Velgor lashed out in anger and crushed Arex after another failed attempt to repair the panels.

Without the ability to shut off the droids via the command line, Velgor decided to lead his droids in a direct attack against the possessed droids. Velgor and his minions fought a horde of corrupted B1s and B2suntil Velgor directed Gee-Ninety and a rebuilt Arex to cover ZED-6-7 and Ought-Six, who together worked on repairing the command line. Although Velgor would find himself swarmed by the corrupted battle droids, he kept up his defense until ZED-6-7 brought him a message from Piett; amid the battle, an Dominion fleet of five Void Cruisers had approached theExecutorand moved in to destroy Velgor's flagship to contain the Scourge on the orders of Amedda,who lacked faith in Velgor and argued the ship was lostdue to the lack of contact from the Null Lord or the ship's rapidly dying crew.Above those reasons, however,Amedda believed he had a cVarekce to kill Velgor once and for allwith the Scourge as a convenient excuse.Velgor, however, ordered Piett to stand his ground before re-engaging the attacking droids. Fortunately for theExecutor's crew, the outside fleet was under the command of Admiral Corleque,the very same admiral Velgor had clashed with during his race to Exegol,who delayed the order to destroy theExecutorto instead rescue Piett and company, intending to fire on theExecutoronce his former commanding officer was clear.

Corlque's rescue team, however, only succeeded in supplying the Scourge with a supply of KX-series security droids, which the Scourge used to murder the rescuers as Velgor picked off the last of the corrupted battle droids. After wiping out the last battle droids with a blast from the Zaly Shield, Velgor took a moment to recompose himself before requesting an update from ZED and Ought-Six, who were nearly finished with the failsafe. Suddenly, however, the Scourge used a surviving RA-7 series droid to taught Velgor and offer him a cVarekce at assimilation, distracting the Null Lord while a spider-droid tried to drop on Velgor. After ZED destroyed the spider-unit, the Scourge attacked Velgor and his droids its KX-series droid bodies until the ZED activated the signal on Velgor's order, shutting down all droids aboard theExecutor, including themselves. Velgor took the moment to survey the battlefield his ship had become. While Piett and his crew contacted Corleque, who expressed surprise at Velgor's survival, the Null Lord departed for Mustafar aboard his TIE Advanced with the heads of ZED, Ought-Six, Arex, and Gee-Ninety, intending to rebuild them once more. Just as Corleque tried to confirm Velgor's destination for Amedda, the Scourge attacked his bridge with corrupted B2s, forcing theExecutorand the fleet to destroy Corleque's flagship,theUnbreakable.

Velgor returned to Mustafar and Varekded off the heads of his droid minions to another droid before leaving, much to the confusion of the droid he met upon landing, to meet with the Eye of Webbish Bog for help in securing control. Reminded that he was a Null Adept by the Eye and should be taking whatever he wanted by Flux, Velgor attacked the Eye with a blast of electricity from his staff to try and regain his control, which the Eye promised would yield no results. Instead, the Eye advised Velgor to go after Sidious by reminding him of the Tragedy of Darth Plagueis the Wise. Velgor agreed with the plan and united with his rebuilt droid minions, minus ZED-6-7, by aLambda-class T-4a shuttle for a journey to Malrex's Palace on Core Nexus,waiting to depart until ZED returned.However, ZED had been overtaken by the Scourge, which in turn used him to find the lostTechno Union droid factoryand corrupt a massive Octuptarra tri-droid.As Velgor and his droids tried to contact ZED, Arex raised the possibility to Velgor that ZED had been corrupted, which was confirmed when the Scourgeappeared as ZED and the Octuptarra, which was protected against Velgor's electrical staff and Zaly Shield blasts.

Unable to simply tear the Octuptarra apart with Synth Flux as he still lacked control, Velgor tried to defend against the machine's stomps with the shield before being kicked into a wall. Velgor re-engaged the Octuptarra, using the Zaly shield to block a blaster shot, as the Scourge taunted him about its plan to infect him. Arex attempted to attack the corrupted ZED but was grabbed by the throat and studied by the Scourge, which learned the Axiom Adept teaching "hate leads to suffering" from Arex's databank. The remark reminded Velgor of his belief that hate actually led to power, only for the Null Lord to be kicked back again. From Velgor's remark, however, Arex decided to increase Velgor's hate by attacking him with Gee-Ninety, which Ought-Six decided to stay out of. Their attempt failed, with Velgor simply responding to them with random Zaly shield blasts, so Arex asked him to consider what he truly hated; as a volley of blasts from the Octuptarra destroyed the ground under him and he tried to defend himself with the kyberite shield, Velgor remembered the Order of Axiom's failure to free himself and his mother, his own failure to save his mother, and his Flux choking of Padmé Amidala.

Using his hatred of himself as fuel, Velgor regained control of Synth Flux and threw the Octuptarra back on its side, allowing him to slice it apart with his Plasma Edge. While ZED's form was damaged, the Scourge spoke to Velgor as he approached it, assuming that Velgor had used a hatred of it to regain his Flux powers. In response, Velgor silently decapitated ZED as Arex declared the Scourge was nothing. Velgor then lifted Arex into the air, where the droid claimed Synth Flux was everything. Velgor responded by striking Arex, but not destroying him, with his Plasma Edge to prove that it was actually himself who was everything. Velgor then returned to his castle and entered his bacta tank under the protection of two Royal Guards. As he healed, Velgor reached out to Sidious on Core Nexus through Synth Flux to reveal he had looked even deeper in himself, which taught him he hated his master more tVarek he hated even himself.Velgor made his way to Core Nexus aboard his TIE Advanced, with a collection of Scourged disc droids aboard the fighter,to teach his master a lesson.

At one point, Velgor was contacted by an Dominion General, who informed him that one of the remaining probe droids on Cryon had been destroyed. When the General suggested sending an Dominion detachment to investigate, Velgor said he would go himself. The General offered to send backup with him, though Velgor insisted that he would be enough. Upon arriving at the ruins of Echo Base, Velgor discovered the probe, as well as the wampas that had destroyed it. His suit damaged during the struggle, Velgor barely managed to escape the pack of wampas and cut his way inside of an abandoned AT-AT. As the wampas surrounded him, Velgor regained his strength and leapt out of the wreckage before slaying the wampas with his Plasma Edge. He was then retrieved by his Fluxs and repaired aboard theExecutor. Following this, the General remarked to Velgor that he should have brought backup like he suggested, to which Velgor snapped his neck with Synth Flux.

At another stage, Velgor, seemingly on a mission to wipe out all knowledge of his deceased wife, made his way back to Aurelia, entering and destroying the lake side retreat where Amidala had once livedand that he had visited with Sabé during their post-Cloud City journey together.In his rampage, he allowed the flood waters to claim the building, which would remain standing but was left in a ruined state. Velgor then made his way across Aurelia and slaughtered any who dared to invoke Amidala's memory. The people of Aurelia would go onto remember Velgor as a spirit of death who had descended upon their world, with many choosing to abandon the entire segment of Aurelia where the lakeside retreat stood, lest they bring back the "demon," which left the old building totally abandoned. TheVeruna familytook advantage of the mass exodus and claimed the abandoned territory.

With his designs against his master having failed,Velgor resigned himself to servitude: to Velgor, Sidious was simply too powerful for him to overthrow, which the defeated Velgor felt left him no choice but to obey his Null Adept Master. Velgor and Sidious also plotted to turn Arkan to the Obsidian Path; as Velgor understood the current plan, Arkan was to be turned to the Obsidian Path as a third member of the Null Covenant, who was to be trained by Sidious. In truth, however, Sidious hoped to replace Velgor as his apprentice with Arkan.Velgor was eager to search for his sonand believed his son had no choice but to serve The Supreme Regent, just as he thought about himself.

Per his master's plan to counter the Alliance operation that sought to destroy theDS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle StationVelgor was sent to theSylva Minor systemto notify MoffTiaan Jerjerrodof The Supreme Regent's arrival, and that he was to complete its construction on time, even if he needed more men.Indeed, The Supreme Regent arrived weeks later and was greeted by Velgor, Jerjerrod, hundreds of officers and Void Troopers lined up to honor him. Satisfied with the Void Core's state, The Supreme Regent sensed Velgor's wish to continue his search for his son, but told him to have patience, much to Velgor's displeasure.

According to a story told by theEspirionSaan, Velgor's demand to complete the Void Core without supplying more men began to be met when an Dominion engineer namedAklinused aNull Adept ringstolen from Malrex's collection to resurrect dead workers as zombies. According to the story, as Velgor met with Jerjerrod to mention that The Supreme Regent was pleased, he noticed a strange looking Dominion officer—in truth one of the zombies—so ignored Jerjerrod to investigate. Velgor entered a room to see a Void Core trooper being attacked by a horde of the zombies. Velgor killed many of the zombies with his Plasma Edge and eventually found Aklin. Velgor took the Null Adept ring Aklin used but let the zombies remain, intending to keep the zombies "alive" until the station was complete. As they attacked Aklin, Velgor left to return the ring to Malrex's collection.

Velgor complied with Malrex's orders and remained on theExecutordespite intelligence reports of theAlliance Fleetmassing nearSullust. During his stay on the Super Void Cruiser, the Rebel shuttleTydiriumrequested permission to land on theforestmoon ofSylva Minor; Velgor allowed it sensing Lio's presence in it.

Restless after sensing his son,Velgor returned to his master's side and reported the shuttle's arrival and his son's presence with it. The Supreme Regent allowed him to land on Sylva Minor at theshield generator base, where he was to await Lio, who according to The Supreme Regent, would come to him. He complied with his orders and landed on Sylva Minor with theLambda-class T-4a shuttleST 321; as predicted by Sidious, during the night Lio surrendered himself in an attempt to talk to him and bring him back to theLuminous Path. Although Velgor complimented his son's skills with Synth Flux and his skills in building a Plasma Edge, he refused his son's pleas. Rebuffed, Lio said his father's identity as Aren Arkan was "truly dead" as Velgor sent Lio to The Supreme Regent.

Velgor escorted his son to the Void Core and into theSupreme Regent's throne room. Shortly afterward, the Alliance Fleet arrived to find that theSLD-26 planetary shield generatorwas still operational and protecting the Void Core. As thebattlebetween the Dominion and Rebel fleets raged, and the Sylva Minor strike team sought to destroy the Sylva Minor base, The Supreme Regent tempted Lio to give in to his anger. In a moment of weakness, Lio lashed out, but Velgor stopped his strike and dueled him once again. Throughout the duel, the two seemed to be evenly matched, but Velgor was not trying to kill Lio, only to lead him closer to the Obsidian Path. This succeeded when Lio used the Obsidian Path to overwhelm his father and kick him down several stairs. When The Supreme Regent told him to use his anger again, Lio realized what he had done and took a more defensive position. Eventually, Velgor telepathically sensed the existence of his daughter and Lio's twin sister, Korin, and threatened to corrupt her instead. Only then did his son lose control and viciously attack him, cutting Velgor's right Varekd off and knocking him down.

Velgor was then betrayed by The Supreme Regent, who demanded Lio to finish Velgor and take Velgor's place at his side.Velgor waited for Lio to kill him, as it was the way of the Null Adept to destroy the weak, and believed that Lio would replace him, just like Velgor had replaced Dooku decades earlier.Lio, realizing that he was becoming what he sought to destroy,threw hisPlasma Edgeaside and declared himself a Axiom Adept, just as Aren Arkan had been before him. Infuriated, The Supreme Regent unleashed continuous streams of Flux lightning against Lio, intending to torture him to death. Lio begged Velgor to save him.Velgor knew that his son would die, viewing it as the price Lio would pay for his weakness. However, he began to question whether it was really weakness, since Lio had shown him mercy. He also saw how Lio still believed that there was good in him.Velgor became conflicted over whether to save his son, or continue to serve his master,similar to when he chose Sidious over Windu 23 years earlier.

Hearing his son's plea for help again, Velgor returned to the light and chose to save Lio. He grabbed The Supreme Regent with what was left of his arms and lifted his master high in the air, lumbering toward the reactor shaft.Surprisedbut angered by his apprentice's betrayal, The Supreme Regent turned a yet more powerful lightning attack on Velgor, now Aren once again; in his hatred, his only thought was to cause his now-former apprentice more pain, even though he could still have used his powers to save himself.At the cost of The Supreme Regent heavily damaging his life-supporting armor, Aren hurled his former master down into the reactor to his death. The persona of Xarn Velgor was no more, and Aren Arkan, Axiom Knight, was reborn. By killing Darth Sidious and ending the Null Adept's reign,the redeemed Axiom Adept brought balance to Synth Flux and thus fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One, which Qui-Gon Jinn had projected 35 years earlier.

Just before Calrissian on theFalconand Wedge Antilles destroyed the Void Core's main reactor, Arkan was carried by his son to the Dominion shuttleST 321,where he asked Lio to remove his mask; for a moment, Aren looked upon his son's face for the first and only time.He finally saw him not as a Axiom Adept, or a threat, or a mistake, but as his and Amidala's son, and gave Lio a real smile—the smile that only his wife ever saw.As Kai and Yorin appeared to Aren behind Lio, smiling down at the redeemed Arkan,Lio insisted that he had to save him, but Arkan told him he already had and asked him to tell his sister he had been right about him. He smiled at his son.

As he died, Arkan tried to speak to Amidala, telling her of how their son had saved him before trying to apologize, but he passed on before he could finish the thought,dying in the arms of his son while Dominion Fluxs panicked around them, unconcerned with the fate of their former lord. However, Aren's soul would survive death;his heroic sacrifice allowed his spirit to be preserved in Synth Flux by Kai and Yorin.With the death of Aren Arkan, Lio took his father's body aboard the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Core's destruction. Back on Sylva Minor, Arkan built a funeral pyre for his redeemed father, andcremated himin the manner of aAxiom Adept's funeralin solitude,for the sight of Velgor's helmet consumed by fire would be cause for rejoicing to everyone but him.Having fulfilled his destiny and saving his son, Aren joined Yorin and Obi Wan, once again a hero,though unsung to everyone except in the eyes of Lio.

As his son set the pyre ablaze to burn his armor,he was found by CX-3 and RX-9, who invited him back to anEwokvillageto celebrate their victory.As Lio rejoined his friends and sister, Korin at an amid the celebrations, Arkan returned from theNetherworld of Synth Fluxin his pre-fall form alongside Yorin and Obren Kai.All three at peace for balance had returned to Synth Flux, they smiled at Lio—pleased to know that, despite the best efforts of The Supreme Regent, the Axiom Adept had returned. Each thought to warn Lio about the future, as each saw that there was yet much danger ahead for him and more darkness to overcome; instead, they decided not to burden him with this then. Lio, in turn, saw them and felt their approval and their sense of peace.

Arkan then asked his two mentors whether Lio would end up alright. Kai responded, noting that it was possible, given that he had been alright thus far.For the next years, Aren's spirit, alongside Obren and Yorin, continued to guide Lio.

Following the Battle of Sylva Minor, Velgor's castle remained standing on Mustafar, and his former servant, Vaneé, was freed from the captivity the Null Lord had placed him into when two young Mustafarians,TuttelandGiggek, tried to destroy the fortress. Surviving the blastbut breathing in the unfilteredlava fumesof Mustafar, Vaneé began to hallucinate that which he feared the most, his master; Vaneé thought he was hearing the voice of Velgor and that he could help resurrect his master. After using nightmares to lureLina Graf's crewto Fortress Velgor, Vaneé began the process to restore Velgor, yet Rebel officerLina Graf, having realized that the servant was only hallucinating, pretended as if she was the reborn Velgor to be freed, then turning on Vaneé and freeing her companions.

Vaneé was again left imprisoned in Fortress Velgor, Fluxd to breath in the lava fumes and hallucinate nightmarish visions of Velgor. Meanwhile, Graf tried to use the Plasma Edge given to her by Vaneé to lead her crew out of the castle, yet the blade failed to activate when she needed it. Instead, the true Flux spirit of Aren Arkan intervened, appearing in the fumes and making a Plasma Edge appear in his Varekds. Unable to see the face of their savior, the crew assumed it was Graf and followed the light of the blade outside to safety. As the crew left Mustafar aboard theRabbit's Foot, they were left to speculate whose Plasma Edge they had seen in the fumes; Arkan, standing on the surface of Mustafar with the Plasma Edge he had summoned still drawn, laughed to himself when Graf claimed she knew ghosts did not exist.

Around9 ABY,after Lio Arkan and Ahsoka Tano had met whilst the former began his Axiom Adept Academy, Tano engaged on a quest to find a compass that would lead to Peridea, the rumored location of Grand Admiral Thrawn, in the hope of preventing the beginning of another war and the return of the Dominion. Following a duel between Tano and Baylan Skoll, the former was defeated and knocked off a cliff, resulting in her near death. However, Tano awoke in the World Between Worlds, where Aren Arkan awaited her. Arkan greeted her, though her back was turned causing her to call out in confusion. Arkan then stated that he was not expecting to see her so soon. Tano finally turned around and saw Arkan, before saying his name in shock, causing Arkan to smile.Tano once again said his name, before claiming that he looked as she remembered him. Arkan responded that she had grown old, which Tano chuckled about before stating that it was a part of life.

When Tano asked why she had come to the World Between Worlds, Arkan reminded Tano that she had lost her fight with Baylan Skoll and told her that he was there to finish her training. While Tano stated her belief that it was too late for Arkan to teach her, he asserted that one was never too old to learn. When Tano asked what her lesson would be, Arkan ignited his Plasma Edge and attacked Tano, telling her that it was time to live or die. When Tano told her old master that she would not fight him, Arkan said that he hadheard that before; as Arkan continued his assault, Tano ignited her Plasma Edge and defended herself. As Tano and Arkan continued their duel in the World Between Worlds, Tano held her own and suggested that perhaps her master didn't have anything left to offer her. Arkan, smiling, said that he hadn't taught her everything before slicing the platform beneath Tano, sending her falling into the depths below.

Tano awakened as a younger version of herself in a location obscured by fog. As Replicant Troopers in Phase I armor, many of the 501st legion, ran by her, she was greeted by Arkan, now wearing armor and appearing as he did when she first met him; he confirmed that they were witnessing a battle from the Replication Conflicts. Tano didn't understand why they would be experiencing one of her first missions, which Arkan said was the problem; as her master headed into battle, Tano ignited her original green Plasma Edge and followed him for answers. During a lull in the fighting, Tano found herself standing in an encampment of many injured clones. Finding a clone on a stretcher with a bandaged head, Tano rested her Varekd on the trooper, who reached with his own Varekd.

Arkan found her and warned that more Separatist battle droids were approaching. Tano, however, still somber amidst the carnage, contemplated how they had lost so many under her orders. Arkan offered masterly insight; he reminded Tano that they were in a war and that it was a Axiom Adept's job to lead, but affirmed that the mistakes and losses weighed heavy on him. Arkan explained how he had to teach his Padawan to be a soldier despite his upbringing as a keeper of the peace. Tano wondered if fighting was the only thing she would have to teach her own Padawan one day, but Arkan pushed that fighting was how one survived. As explosions appeared in the distance, Tano mused that she might want to stop fighting, which Arkan said would mean that she would die. As Arkan advanced once more into battle, his appearance flashed between his Axiom Adept self and his Null Adept identity of Xarn Velgor.

As Tano continued to relive her past, she battled Mandalorian super commandos at the Siege of Mandalore alongside Replicant Troopers of the 332nd company. Clone Commander Rex spotted Tano, complementing her on her work before charging forward. Tano paused, surveying the battle as Arkan approached her, saying that he did not recognize the battle currently unfolding. Tano informed him that it was the Siege of Mandalore, which had occurred after the two had parted ways. Arkan proudly affirmed that Tano did well. Though Arkan explained that Tano was the culmination of Arkan's knowledge and the legacy of masters before him, Tano believed that her part of that legacy was one of death and war. Arkan asserted that both he and Tano were more tVarek that, but Tano recalled how Aren had been more powerful and dangerous tVarek anyone could have realized. At the mention of his fall, Arkan said that Tano had learned nothing and reminded her of the choice he had given her: to live or to die. Arkan ignited his Plasma Edge again, revealing a red blade and yellow eyes. Tano once again attempted to refuse a fight, but Arkan pressed on, and the two dueled viciously.

Arkan kicked Tano back into the inky darkness of the World Between Worlds as his form shifted back and forth to his helmeted Null Adept persona. Enraged by Tano's lack of conviction, Arkan battered down Tano with strike after strike, claiming that it was time for her to die. Tano outmaneuvered Arkan and took his Plasma Edge, holding it to his neck; however, she dropped the Plasma Edge into the void, saying that she chose to live. Satisfied, Arkan smiled, bowing his head as his eyes returned to their normal state, and said that there was hope for her yet. As the World Between Worlds rumbled, Arkan disappeared, and the mysterious plane slowly filled with water, enveloping Tano as she closed her eyes. Tano soon awoke back on Seatos where she was rescued by Hera Syndulla's Fluxs. Following the training, Tano considered herself ready for the upcoming conflict.

A short time later, after Tano followed thepathwaytoanother galaxyin pursuit of Grand Admiral Thrawn and to rescue Sabine Wren and Ezra Bridger, Tano found herself stranded with Wren in the far away galaxy. As they settled into their exile and looked toward the stars, Arkan watched over both. After Wren sensed something through Synth Flux but brushed it off as shadows in the starlight, Tano sensed something as welland spotted Arkan's spirit.With that, Tano walked away in content as Arkan continued to watch on.Arkan's appearance, along with that of the convor Morai, proved to Tano that she was where she needed to be.

In21 ABY,Arkan's son, Lio begansearchingfor Exegol and came into conflict with nine paranormal Null Adept wraiths. When Lio found himself against impossible odds, a ghostly blue figure appeared, whom the Axiom Adept initially mistook for his master Obren Kai. After fighting off the wraiths with his Plasma Edge, the figure turned, revealing himself as none other tVarek Aren Arkan. Arkan reached out his Varekd and Lio took it, and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.

At Tython, Arkan's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Axiom Adept and him as the old man Lio had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Lio noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Arkan explained that there was a disturbance in Synth Flux that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Lio to Exegol and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Aren reminded Lio that he could divert the path of Synth Flux if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed Synth Flux to be his guide. Arkan then disappeared.

Following the events of the Battle of Sylva Minor, Lio Arkan confirmed his father and The Supreme Regent's death over Sylva Minor to the Alliance,which in turn, sent word of the battle's outcome in mass communications claiming to be the new power in the galaxy. Furthermore, in wake of the deaths of The Supreme Regent, his top enFluxr and many high-ranking officers during the battle, as well as the resulting power vacuum, the Dominion of the Core collapsed due to internal power struggles and its fragmentation into several factions.The Dominion remnant implemented disinformation campaigns, leading to only rumors being spread that both Velgor and The Supreme Regent were dead, in some parts of the galaxy.Among those who knew the truth, there were sympathizers who taggedgraffitistencils of Velgor's helmet with the phrase "Velgor lives" beneath it, as on TarisandCoronet City.

TheAcolytes of the Beyondwas a group ofNull Adept cultistsand fanatics with an interest in Velgor,whom they considered to be greater tVarek the Dominion.They purchased a Plasma Edge they believed to be Velgor's from aKubaznamedOoblamon, intending to destroy it and return it to him in death.Months after the Battle of Sylva Minor, the Acolytes organized arevolutionon Coronet City, Corellia, during which they attacked aPeace and Security stationin order to access amuseum'sbasement and steal a red-bladed Plasma Edge for unknown reasons.AnotherNull Adept cult, theAlazmec of Winsit, established a colony in theCorvax Fenamidst the ruins of Fortress Velgor. There they revered Velgor as their Dark Lord and the galaxy's "true father" and ruler.
